Vol. II Part II
Prāyaścitta Kāṇḍa
[1]
dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
atha ācārakāṇḍe tribhir adhyāyair yāvanta ācārāḥ pratipāditāḥ prāyaścittakāṇḍe cāṣṭabhir adhyāyair yāni prāyaścittāny abhihitāni tebhyo ‘tiriktānāṃ pariśiṣṭānāṃ keṣāṃcid ācārāṇāṃ prāyaścittānāṃ ca vidhānāyāyaṃ dvādaśo ‘dhyāya ārabhyate |
tatrādau duḥsvapnādinimittakaṃ snānam āha |
duḥsvapnaṃ yadi paśyet tu vānte tu kṣurakarmaṇi |
maithune pretadhūme ca snānam eva vidhīyate || 12.1 || iti |
svapno dvividhaḥ | susvapno duḥsvapnaś ca | tatra yo darśanakāle draṣṭuḥ sukhaṃ janayan bhāvi śreyaś ca sūcayati sa susvapnaḥ | tasyodāharaṇaṃ chandogā āmananti |
[2]        yadā karmasu kāmyeṣu striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyati |
samṛddhiṃ tatra jānīyāt tasmin svapnanidarśane || iti | (ChUp 5.2.8)
tadvaiparītyena darśanakāle duḥkhaṃ janayan puruṣasya bhāvi cāriṣṭaṃ yaḥ sūcayati sa duḥsvapnaḥ | etasya tūdāharaṇaṃ bahvṛcāḥ paṭhanti | tatra hi maraṇasūcakāni jāgaraṇāriṣṭāni bahūny abhidhāya ante pratyakṣadarśanānīty upasaṃhṛtyānantaraṃ svapnāriṣṭāny evam āmnāyante |
atha svapnāḥ | puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇadantaṃ paśyati svapne sa enaṃ hanti varāha enaṃ hanti markaṭa enam āskandayaty āśu vāyur enaṃ pravahati suvarṇaṃ khāditvāpagirati madhv aśnāti bisāni bhakṣayaty ekaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ dhārayati kharair varāhair yukto yāti kṛṣṇāṃ dhenuṃ kṛṣṇavatsāṃ naladamālī dakṣiṇābhimukho vrajati |
[3] ityādi | svapnādhyāyavidaś ca svapnasya bhāvisūcakaṃ vacanam āhuḥ |
ārohaṇaṃ govṛṣakuñjarāṇāṃ prāsādaśailāgravanaspatīnām |
viṣṭhānulepo ruditaṃ mṛtaṃ ca svapne tv agamyāgamanaṃ ca dhanyam ||
kṛṣṇāmbaradharā nārī kṛṣṇagandhānulepanā |
avagūhati yaṃ svapne mṛtyuṃ tasya vinirdiśet || iti |
etat sarvam abhipretya svapnādhikaraṇe bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ sūtrayāmāsa: “sūcakaś ca hi śruter ācakṣate ca tadvidaḥ” iti (BrSu 3.2.4) | tatra bhāvyariṣṭasūcake duḥsvapne dṛṣṭe sati prātar utthāya tannimittakaṃ snānaṃ kartavyam | tathā bhukte ‘nne vānte sati tadaiva snātavyam | tathā kṣauramaithunapretadhūmāghrāṇeṣu ca snānam ācaret | evaṃ durjanasparśanādāv api snānaṃ draṣṭavyam | tad āha yamaḥ |
[4]        ajīrṇe ‘bhyudite vānte śmaśrukarmaṇi maithune |
duḥsvapne durjanasparśe snānam eva vidhīyate || iti |
vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi |
duḥsvapne maithune vānte virikte kṣurakarmaṇi |
citiyūpaśmaśānānāṃ sparśane snānam ācaret || iti |
maithune tu snānam ṛtukālaviṣayam | tad āha śātātapaḥ |
ṛtau hi garbhaśaṅkitvāt snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchet śaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat || iti |
vamanādau viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret |
ācāmed eva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam || (MDh 5.144)
[5] iti | bhuktvānnam anantaraṃ vāntaḥ ācāmed ity arthaḥ || 12.1 ||
atha prāyaścittinaḥ punaḥsaṃskāranimittāny āha |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥsaṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || 12.2 || iti |
ayaṃ ca saṃskāro vratacaraṇānantarabhāvī | tatra vacanāni tu pūrvādhyāya eva prāyaścittaprasaṅgād udāhṛtāni || 12.2 ||
ādyasaṃskāravat punaḥsaṃkāre ‘py ajinādiprāptāv apavadati |
ajinaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣyacaryā vratāni ca |
nivartante dvijātīnāṃ punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || 12.3 || iti |
ajinādivad vapanam api nivartante | tad āha viṣṇuḥ: “sarveṣv eteṣu dvijānāṃ prāyaścittānte bhūyaḥ saṃskāraṃ kuryāt | [6] vapanamekhalādaṇḍabhaikṣacaryāvratāni punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi varjanīyāni” iti (ViDh 51.4–5) | vratāni saumyaprājāpatyādīni madhumāṃsāñjanādivarjanāni ca |
|| 12.3 ||
pūrvādhyāyādau amedhyaretogomāṃsaviṇmūtraprāśane cāndrāyaṇam ity uktam (11.1) tat kāmakāraviṣayam | ata eva tatra bālāpatyasahabhojanam asmādhir udāhṛtam | atra tv akāmato viṇmūtrādibhojane prāyaścittam āha |
viṇmūtrabhojī śuddhyarthaṃ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
pañcagavyaṃ ca kurvīta snātvā pītvā śucir bhavet || 12.4 || iti |
prājāpatyaṃ caritvā paścāt pañcagavyenaiva snānaṃ kṛtvā tat pañcagavyaṃ prāśya śuddho bhavati | etac ca punaḥsaṃskārāt prāg eva kartavyam, “prāyaścittānte bhūyaḥ saṃskāraṃ kuryāt” (ViDh 51.4) iti viṣṇuvacanāt | jale tu viṇmūtropahate kūrmapurāṇoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
apo mūtrapurīṣādyair dūṣitāḥ prāśayed yadā |
tadā sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ vrataṃ pāpaviśodhanam || iti | (KūPu 1.2.33.38)
|| 4 ||
caturṣu varṇeṣu yaḥ ko ‘pi svātmaghātārtham udyamya kathaṃcid ghātāt prāg eva nivartate tasya prāyaścittaṃ praśnapūrvakam āha |
jalāgnipatane caiva pravrajyānāśakeṣu ca |
pratyāvasitavarṇānāṃ kathaṃ śuddhir vidhīyate || 12.5 ||
prājāpatyadvayenaiva tīrthābhigamanena ca |
vṛṣaikādaśadānena varṇāḥ śudhyanti te trayaḥ || 12.6 ||
brāhmaṇasya pravakṣyāmi vanaṃ gatvā catuṣpathe |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyadvayaṃ caret || 12.7 ||
godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt śuddhiṃ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt |
mucyate tena pāpena brāhmaṇatvaṃ ca gacchati || 12.8 || iti |
jalāgnyādayaḥ pañca maraṇahetavaḥ | nadītaṭākādipraveśeṇāgnipraveśena bhṛgupatanena mahāprasthānagamanenānāśakena ca mriyante | tatra jalādimaraṇaṃ trividham | vihitaṃ pratiṣiddham [8] anujñātaṃ ca | vihitaṃ ca dvividham | kāmyataporūpaṃ prāyaścittataporūpaṃ ca | tatra taporūpaṃ kūrmapurāṇe narmadāmāhatmye pradarśitam |
agnipreveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāt somatīrthe narādhipa |
jale vānāśake vāpi nāsau martyo ‘bhijāyate || iti | (KūPu 1.2.39.49)
tatraiva prayāgamahātmye ‘bhihitam |
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye kārīṣāgniṃ viśet tu yaḥ |
ahīnāṅgo hy arogaś ca pañcendriyasamanvitaḥ ||
yāvanti romakūpāṇi tasya gātreṣu mānavaḥ |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
[9]        jalapraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāt saṅgame lokaviśrute |
rāhugraste yadā some vimuktaḥ sarvapātakaiḥ ||
somalokam avāpnoti somena saha modate || iti | (KūPu 1.1.37.6–7)
tad etat prāyaścittarūpaṃ maraṇam | krodhādinā yaj jalādimaraṇaṃ tat pratiṣiddham | tathā cāśaucaprakaraṇe “krodhāt prāyam” ityādismṛtivākyam udāhṛtam | prabalarogagrastasya yaj jalādimaraṇaṃ na tad vihitam | nāpi pratiṣiddham | kiṃ tu śāstreṇa kevalam anujñātam | tatra tasminn eva prakaraṇe “duścikitsyair mahārogaiḥ” ityādivākyenodāhṛtam | tatrābhyanujñātasyaicchikatvāt tasya phalālabdhimātraṃ kevalaṃ bhavati | na tv asau prāyaścittī | yo ‘pi prāyaścittārthaṃ marttum udyamya tato nivartate tasyāpi pūrvapāpaṃ na nivartate ity etāvad eva | na tu tena kartavyaṃ kiṃcit [10] prāyaścittāntaram asti | evaṃ ca sati pratiṣiddham eva pariśiṣyate | tatrāpi martum udyamya mṛtasya durmaraṇatve ‘pi na tatredaṃ prāyaścittam | tatkartur evābhāvāt | yas tūdyamya maraṇān nivartate tasyedaṃ prāyaścittam |
nanu – tasyāpīdaṃ na yuktam, ātmahatyāyāḥ śāstraniṣiddhatvena tannivṛtteḥ śāstrīyatvāt |
bāḍham | na hi vayaṃ nivṛttainimittam idaṃ prāyaścittaṃ brūmaḥ | kiṃ tu niṣiddhācaraṇodyam animittam | udyamamātrasya nimittatvād eva prāyaścittasya svalpatvam upapadyate |
jalāgnyādiṣu martum udyamya tato nivṛttāḥ “pratyāviasitāḥ” | tādṛśānāṃ caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ pṛthak pṛthak prāyaścittaṃ vaktavyam iti praśnavākyasyārthaḥ | tatra kṣatriyasya prājāpatyadvayam | vaiśyasya tīrthayātrā | śūdrasya vṛṣabhasahitagodaśakadānam | brāhmaṇasya vanagamanādivratam | ātmahananodyamena brāhmaṇatvam apagatam | caṇḍālatvam āyātam | punar vratācaraṇena caṇḍālatvanivṛttau punaḥ pūrvasiddhaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ pratipadyate | tad āha vṛddhaparāśaraḥ |
anāśakān nivṛttas tu cāturvarṇyavyavasthitaḥ |
caṇḍālaḥ sa tu vijñeyo varjanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ||
[11]        luptadharmās tu caṇḍālāḥ parivrājakatāpasāḥ |
tebhyo jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayet ||
jalāgnipatane caiva pravrajyānāśake tathā |
pratyāvasitavarṇānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena tīrthābhigamanena ca |
gavāṃ ca śatadānena varṇāḥ śudhyanti te trayaḥ ||
brāhmaṇasya pravakṣyāmi gatvāraṇyaṃ catuṣpatham |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā trisaṃdhyam avagāhanam ||
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japec caiva dine dine |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo brāhamaṇatvaṃ ca gacchati ||
bhaikṣārthī vicared grāmaṃ gṛhān sapta vane vasan |
tāṃ ca bhikṣāṃ samaśnīyād abdārdhena viśudhyati || iti |
atraiva vratāntarāṇy āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
jīvann ātmatyāgī kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret |
trirātraṃ vāpy upavasen nityaṃ snigdhena vāsasā |
prāṇān ātmani saṃyamya triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam ||
api vaitena kalpena gāyatrīṃ parivartayet |
api vāgnim upasamādhāya kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyāḍ ghṛta || (VaDh 23.19–21)
[12] iti | tatra japahomau vidvadviśeṣeṇa vikalpanīyau | dvādaśarātratrirātrau tv avidvadviṣayaśaktāśaktabhedena vyavasthāpanīyau |
mūlavacane pravrajyāśabdena pūrvaṃ mahāprasthānagamanaṃ vyākhyātam | atha vā parivrajyā tena vivakṣyate | tathā ca sati parivrajyātaḥ pracyutasya brāhmaṇasya prāyaścittam uktaṃ bhavati | tad idaṃ śraddhāloḥ punarupanayanādipuraḥsaraṃ pārivrajyaṃ jighṛkṣor veditavyam | yas tu punaḥ pārivrajyaṃ na jighṛkṣati tasya maraṇāntaṃ rājadāsatvādikam | yat tv atra nāradaḥ,
rājña eva tu dāsaḥ syāt pravrajyāvasito dvijaḥ |
na tasya pratimoko ‘sti na viśuddhiḥ kathaṃcana || (NSm 5.35)
iti, tat kṣatriyavaiśyaparam | yad āha kātyāyanaḥ,
pravrajyāvasitā yatra trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
nirvāsaṃ kārayed vipraṃ dāsyaṃ kṣatraviśau nṛpaḥ ||
[13] iti | dakṣaḥ |
pārivrajyaṃ gṛhītvā tu yaḥ svadharme na tiṣṭhati |
śvapadenāṅkayitvā taṃ rājā śīghraṃ pravāseyet || iti | (DaSm 7.34)
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pravrajyāvasito rājño dāsaḥ syāt maraṇāntikam | iti | (YDh 2.183)
|| 12.5–8 ||
athācārakāṇḍaśeṣatvena snānabhedān āha |
snānāni pañca puṇyāni kīrtitāni manīṣibhiḥ |
āgneyaṃ vāruṇaṃ brāhmaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyam eva ca || 12.9 ||
iti || 9 ||
āgneyādīnām upadiṣṭānāṃ pañcānāṃ krameṇa svarūpaṃ vivinakti |
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānam avagāhya tu vāruṇam |
āpo hiṣṭheti ca brāhmaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam || 12.10 ||
yat tu sātapavarṣeṇa tat snānaṃ dviyam ucyate |
tatra snātvā tu gaṅgāyāṃ snāto bhavati mānavaḥ || 12.11 ||
[14] iti | bhasmasnānaṃ dvividham | pādādikaṃ mūrdhādikaṃ ca | tatra pādādikaṃ viraktaviṣayam, saṃhārakramatvāt | tac ca kūrmapurāṇe darśitam |
āgneyaṃ bhasmanāpādamastakād dehadhūlanam | iti | (KūPu 1.2.18.13)
mūrdhādi liṅgapurāṇe darśitam |
īśānena śirodeśaṃ mukhaṃ tat puruṣeṇa tu |
urodeśam aghoreṇa guhyaṃ vāmena suvratāḥ ||
sadyena pādau sarvāṅgaṃ praṇavena tu śodhayet | iti | (LiṅPu 1.26.37–38)
jalāvagāhanarūpaṃ tu vāruṇasnānaṃ prathamādhyāye “saṃdhyāṃ snānaṃ japo homaḥ” (ParSm 1.39) [15] ity atra prapañcitam | tac ca triḥkṛtvo ‘bhyasteṣu snāneṣu kartavyam | tatprakāraś ca baudhāyanena darśitaḥ |
bhuvi mūrdhni tathākāśe mūrdhny ākāśe tathā bhuvi |
ākāśe bhuvi mūrdhnīti mantrasnānaṃ vidhīyate || iti | (BDh 1.2.10.21)
sāyaṃkāle goṣu mārgeṣv āgacchantīṣu vāyunā samutthite gopādarajasy avasthānaṃ vāyavyam | ātapayuktena varṣodakena dehasyāplāvanaṃ divyasnānam | tad idaṃ pūrvoktebhyaḥ praśastam | etena yaugikasārasvate api dve snāne upalakṣyete | yaugikaṃ yogena viṣṇucintanam | tad āha vyāsaḥ |
brāhmam āgneyam uddiṣṭaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyam eva ca |
vāruṇaṃ yaugikaṃ tadvat ṣoḍhā snānaṃ samāsataḥ |
brāhmaṃ tu mārjanaṃ mantraiḥ kuśaiḥ sodakabindubhiḥ ||
[16]        āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam |
yat tu sātapavarṣeṇa tat snānaṃ divyam ucyate ||
vāruṇaṃ cāvagāhas tu mānasaṃ tv ātmavedanam |
yaugikaṃ snānam ākhyātaṃ yogād viṣṇuvicintanam || iti |
vidvadāśiṣā saṃpāditaṃ sārasvataṃ snānam | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ proktam astaṃ gacchati gopatau |
vidvatsarasvatīprāptaṃ snānaṃ sārasvataṃ smṛtam || iti | (BēSm 3.49)
vidvaduktiprakāram āha vyāsaḥ |
svayam evopapannāya vinayena dvijātaye |
tajjñaḥ saṃpādayet snānaṃ śiṣyāya ca sutāya ca ||
dākṣāyaṇamayaiḥ kumbhair mantravaj jāhnavījalaiḥ |
kṛtamaṅgalapuṇyāhaiḥ snānam astu tadarthinām || (VyāSm 1.14–15)
ādau yāvat prabhāse bahuguṇasalile madhame puṣkare vā |
gaṅgādvāre prayāge kanakhalasalile bhadrakarṇe gayāyām ||
[17]        rāhugraste ca some dinakarasahite sannihatyāṃ viśeṣāt |
etair vikhyātatīrthais tribhuvanaviditaiḥ snānam acchidram astu ||
iti snānāni | eteṣu saptasu snāneṣu vāruṇaṃ mukhyam itarāṇi ṣaḍ snānāni gauṇāni | mukhyagauṇabhedena śaṅkhavacanam ācārakāṇḍa evodāhṛtam | tatra gauṇasnānāny aśaktaviṣayāṇi | tad uktaṃ kūrmapurāṇe |
aprāyatye samutpanne snānam eva samācaret |
brāhmādīni tathāśaktau snānāny āhur manīṣiṇaḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.18.11)
|| 12.10–11 ||
vāruṇasnāne tarpaṇasya pūrvabhāvitvaṃ vastraniṣpīḍanasyottarabhāvitvam upapattipuraḥsaraṃ vidadhāti |
snātuṃ yāntaṃ dvijaṃ sarve devāḥ pitṛgaṇaiḥ saha |
vāyubhūtās tu gacchanti tṛṣṇārtāḥ salilārthinaḥ || 12.12 ||
nirāśās te nivartante vastraniṣpīḍane kṛte |
tasmān na pīḍayed vastram akṛtvā pitṛtarpaṇam || 12.13 || iti |
snātuṃ gacchantaṃ ye ‘nugacchanti te sarve pūrvaṃ vastre niṣpīḍite [18] sati nirāśāḥ gacchanti – ity evopapattiḥ | asminn arthe yogiyājñavalkyādismṛtyantravacanāni pūrvam evodāhṛtāni ||
|| 12.12–13 ||
tarpaṇavelāyāṃ tilāḥ pāṇitale vilomapradeśe pātrāntare vā sthāpanīyāḥ na tu salomni pāṇipṛṣṭhe – ity abhipretya tatra sthāpane pratyavāyam āha |
romakūpeṣv avasthāpya yas tilais tarpayet pitṝn |
pitaras tarpitās tena rudhireṇa malena ca || 12.14 || iti |
asminn arthe devalavacanaṃ pūrvam evodāhrtam || 12.14 ||
snānavelāyāṃ keśadhūnanādi pratiṣedhati |
avadhūnoti yaḥ keśān snātvā yas tūtsṛjen malam |
ācāmed vā jalastho ‘pi sa bāhyaḥ pitṛdaivataiḥ|| 12.15 ||
[19] iti | snātvety etat padaṃ kākākṣivad ubhayataḥ saṃbadhyate | tathā ca sati yaḥ snānānantaram ārdrān keśān avadhunuyāt yaś ca snānānantaram ārdravastro malamūtre visṛjet yaś ca nadyādāv asnānakāle jalamadhye sthitvā ācāmet, sa trividho ‘pi puruṣaḥ pitṛbhiḥ daivataiś ca bāhyaḥ kṛtaḥ | paitṛkaṃ daivikaṃ cānuṣṭhānam anuṣṭhātum anarha ity arthaḥ || 12.15 ||
ācamanakāle śiraḥprāvaraṇādīni niṣedhati |
śiraḥ prāvṛtya kaṇṭhaṃ vā muktakacchaśikho ‘pi vā |
vinā yajñopavītena ācānto ‘py aśucir bhavet || 12.16 || iti |
vastreṇa śiraḥ kaṇṭhaṃ vā pravṛtya nācāmet | tathā muktakaccho vā mukthaśikho vā yajñopavītarahito vā nācāmet || 12.16 ||
ācamane niyamāntaram āha |
jale sthalastho nācāmet jalasthaś ca bahiḥsthale |
ubhe spṛṣṭvā samācānta ubhayatra śucir bhavet || 12.17 | iti |
yo hi padbhyāṃ jalam aspṛṣṭvā śuddhasthala evopaviśya īṣatprahvo jala eva bindūn pātayann ācāmati sa jalasthalayor ubhayor na śudhyati | yaś ca tadvaiparītyena jale pādadvayam avasthāpya sthale bindūn pātayann ācāmati, so ‘pi nobhayatra śuddhyati | atas tathā nācāntavyam | kiṃ tv ekena pādena sthalam [20] apareṇa jalaṃ spṛṣṭvā samācāmet | tathā cobhayatra śudhyati | etac ca snātvā ya ārdravastras tadviṣayam || 12.17 ||
dvirācamananimittāny āha |
snātvā pītvā kṣute supte bhuktvā rathyopasarpaṇe |
ācāntaḥ punar ācāmed vāso viparidhāya ca || 12.18 || iti |
ācamanapratinidhitvena śrotrasparśe nimittāny āha |
kṣute niṣṭhīvane caiva dantocchiṣṭe tathānṛte |
patitānāṃ ca saṃbhāṣe dakṣiṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ spṛśet || 12.19 || iti |
śrotrasparśasya śuddhihetutvam arthavādapuraḥsaram upapādayati |
prabhāsādīni tīrthāni gaṅgādyāḥ saritas tathā |
viprasya dakṣiṇe karṇe santīti manur abravīt || 12.20 ||
agnir āpaś ca vedāś ca somasūryānilās tathā |
sarva eva tu viprasya śrotre tiṣṭhanti dakṣiṇe || 12.21 || iti |
[21]
aruṇodayāt pūrvaṃ snānaṃ niṣedhati |
bhāskarasya karaiḥ pūtaṃ divā snānaṃ praśasyate |
apraśastaṃ niśi snānaṃ rāhor anyatra darśanāt || 12.22 || iti |
grahaṇasnāne rātrisnānasya pratiprasavaṃ vivakṣitvā rāhor anyatra darśanāḍ ity uktam || 12.22 ||
snānadṛṣṭāntena dānādīnām api pratiprasavam āha |
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ kartavyo rāhudarśane |
anyadā tv aśucī rātris tasmāt tāṃ parivarjayet || 12.23 || iti |
yady api rātrau dānādipratiṣedho na mūlavacane prastutaḥ [22] tathāpi śāstrāntarasiddhaṃ pratiṣedhaṃ hṛdi nidhāyāyaṃ pratiprasava iti draṣṭavyam | anyadā rāhudarśanarahite kāle rātriḥ snānādiṣv aśuciḥ tasmāt snānādikaṃ cikīrṣus tāṃ rātriṃ parivarjayet || 12.23 ||
somagrahaṇakālasya dānādyarhatvam upapādayati |
maruto vasavo rudrā ādityāś caiva devatāḥ |
sarve some pralīyante tasmād dānantu tadgrahe || 12.24 || iti |
rāhudarśana iva khalayajñādāv api rātridānam abhyanujānāti |
khalayajñe vivāhe ca saṃkrāntau grahaṇe tathā |
śarvaryāṃ dānam asty eva nānyatraivaṃ vidhīyate || 12.25 || iti |
punar api rāhidarśanaṃ dṛṣṭāntatvenodāhṛtya putrajanmādāu rātridānam abhyanjānāti |
putrajanmani yajñe ca tathā cātyayakarmaṇi |
rāhoś ca darśane dānaṃ praśastaṃ nānyadā niśi || 12.26 ||
[23] iti | atyayakarma maraṇam | tasmin prastute sati godānatilapātrādidānaṃ niśy api kartavyam || 12.26 ||
“nānyadā niśi” ity ayaṃ niṣedho dāna iva snāne ‘py astīty abhyupetya pūrvapaścimayāmayoḥ snānasya pratiprasavam āha |
mahāniśā tu vijñeyā madhyasthaṃ praharad vayam |
pradoṣapaścimau yāmau dinavat snānam ācaret || 12.27 || iti |
niśi niṣedho mahāniśāviṣayaḥ | mahāniśāśabdaś ca dvitīyatṛtīyayāmāv abhidhatte | tathā ca sati prathamacaturthayāmau dinavat snānādiyogyau | tasmāt tatra snānādikam ācaret | etac cāpadviṣayam | anyathā “bhāskarasya karaiḥ” (ParSm 12.22) ity anena virudhyet | kāmyanaimittike snāne tu mahāniśāyām apy aniṣiddhe | tad āha devalaḥ |
mahāniśā tu vijñeyā madhyasthaṃ praharadvayam |
tasyāṃ snānaṃ na kurvīta kāmyanaimittikād ṛte || iti || 12.27 ||
[24]
rātrau snānasya nimittaṃ rāhudarśanaṃ pūrvam uktam | idānīṃ nimittāntaram āha |
caityavṛkṣaś citir yūpaś caṇḍālaḥ somavikrayī |
etāṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ spṛṣṭvā savāsā jalam āviśet || 12.28 || iti |
citer yogyaṃ śmaśānasthānaṃ caityam | tatra samāropito vṛkṣaḥ caityavṛkṣaḥ | cityādayaḥ prasiddhāḥ | teṣāṃ sparśanaṃ snānanimittam | tad yadi kathaṃcit rātrau saṃpadyate tadā rātrāv api snātavyam ity abhiprāyaḥ || 12.28 ||
snānasya nimittāntaram āha |
asthisaṃcayanāt pūrvaṃ ruditvā snānam ācaret |
antardaśāhe viprasya hy ūrdhvam ācamanaṃ bhavet || 12.29 || iti |
“prathame ‘hni” (VyāSm 59) ityādiśāstrair vihitānāṃ [25] dinānāṃ madhye yadāsthisaṃcayanaṃ kriyate tataḥ pūrvasmin kāle rodanaṃ snānanimittam | asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvaṃ rodanaṃ tv ācamanasyaiva nimittam, na tu snānasya || 12.29 ||
nanu – somagrahaṇe rātrāv api snātavyam ity uktaṃ tad ayuktam, udakasya śuddhyabhāvāt | tad āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
apeyaṃ tu tadā toyaṃ rātrau madhyamayāmayoḥ |
snānaṃ caiva na kartavyaṃ tathaivācamanakriyā || iti |
tasmād rātrau snānaṃ katham abhyupagamyate — ity āśaṅkya somagrahādivyatiriktaviṣayeyam aśuddhir ity āha |
sarvaṃ gaṅgāsamaṃ toyaṃ rāhugraste divākare |
somagrahe tathaivoktaṃ snānadānādikarmasu || 12.30 || iti |
somagrahasyopalakṣaṇatvāt saṃkrāntyādiṣv api nāsty [26] aśuddhidoṣaḥ || 12.30 ||
somagrahādīnāṃ yathā udakaprāśastyahetutvaṃ tathā kuśānām apy āha |
kuśaiḥ pūtaṃ tu yat snānaṃ kuśenopaspṛśed dvijaḥ |
kuśena coddhṛtaṃ toyaṃ somapānasamaṃ bhavet || 12.31 || iti |
kuśair mārjanaṃ kṛtvā mantrasnānaṃ kriyate tat kuśapūtaṃ snānam | kuśān anāmikāyāṃ dhṛtvā yad ācamanaṃ tat kuśodakopasparśanam | saṃdhyāvandanādau mārjanārthaṃ kuśāgrair uddhṛtaṃ bindujātaṃ kuśoddhṛtatoyam | tat toyaṃ somapānavat praśastam || 12.31 ||
atha kṛtsnavedādhyayanaṃ kartum aśaktasyaikadeśādhyayanam abhyanujñātum adhyayanābhāvaṃ nindati |
agnikāryāt paribhraṣṭāḥ saṃdhyopāsanavarjitāḥ |
vedaṃ caivānadhīyānās sarve te vṛṣalāḥ smṛtāḥ || 12.32 || iti |
ubhayoḥ samdhyayor brahmacāribhiḥ samidādhānam agnikāryam | tena gṛhasthānām aupāsanam apy upalakṣyate | agnikāryādirahitānāṃ vṛṣalatvaṃ śūdrādisamānatvam || 12.32 ||
[27]
ekadeśādhyayanam abhyanujānāti |
tasmād vṛṣalabhītena brāhmaṇena viśeṣataḥ |
adhyetavyo ‘py ekadeśo yadi sarvaṃ na śakyate || 12.33 || iti |
na cādhyayananyāyenāgnikāryasaṃdhyopāsanayor apy ekadeśānuṣṭhānaṃ śaṅkanīyam, prayāsarahitatvena tayor aśaktyasaṃbhavāt, kvacit tadvarjanasya nāstikyālasyādimūlatvāt | vṛṣalabhītena vṛṣalatvād bhītenety arthaḥ || 12.33 ||
atha śūdrānnaṃ pratiṣedhati |
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭasyāpy adhīyānasya nityaśaḥ |
japato juhavto vāpi gatir ūrdhvā na vidyate || 12.34 || iti |
adhyayanajapahomās tāvad aśeṣapāpakṣayahetutayā pavitratām āpādayantīti dharmaśāstreṣu ḍiṇḍimaḥ [niyamaḥ] | tathā ca manuḥ |
vedābhyāso ‘nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā |
nāśayanty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api || (MDh 11.245)
[28] iti | bṛhaspatir api |
yathā jātabalo vahnir dahaty ārdrān api drumān |
tathā dahati vedajñaḥ karmajaṃ doṣam ātmanaḥ || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
yathāgnir vāyunādhmāto haviṣā caiva dīpyate |
evaṃ japyaparo nityaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ saṃpradīpyate || iti | (VaDh 23.16)
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
vaiśvānarīṃ vrātapatīṃ pavitreṣṭiṃ tathaiva ca |
ṛtāv ṛtau prayuñjānaḥ punāti daśapūruṣam || iti |
yas tu nairantaryeṇa śūdrānnaṃ bhuñjānaḥ tadrasena puṣṭo bhavati taṃ puruṣaṃ yathoktā adhyayanādayo ‘pi na pāvayituṃ kṣamāḥ tasmāt śūdrānnaṃ yatnena varjayed ity āśayaḥ || 12.34 ||
śūdrānnaṃ dṛṣṭāntīkṛtya tadvac chūdraasaṃparkādīn pratiṣedhati |
śūdrānnaṃ śūdrasaṃparkaḥ śūdreṇa tu sahāsanam |
śūdrāj jñānāgamaś caiva jvalantam api pātayet || 12.35 || iti |
śūdrānnaṃ vyākhyātam | ekasmin gṛhe śūdreṇa sahavāsaḥ [29] śūdrasaṃparkaḥ | kṛṣyādikaṃ kārayatāṃ kṛṣīvalaiḥ śūdraiḥ saha kadācid ekasmin śakaṭādāv upaveśanaṃ sahāsanam | purāṇaśravaṇādijātadharmādharmavivekāt prājñāt vāgminaḥ śūdrād dharmādharmaprāptir jñānāgamaḥ | ete śūdrānnādayaḥ jvalantaṃ brahmavarcasena vikhyātam api puruṣaṃ narake pātayanti | tasmāt te varjanīyāḥ || 12.35 ||
varjyāntaram āha |
yaḥ śūdryā pācayen nityaṃ śūdrī ca gṛhamedhinī |
varjitaḥ pitṛdevebhyo rauravaṃ yāti sa dvijaḥ || 12.36 || iti |
yo vipraḥ kenacin nimittena parasthalādau nivasan svakīyam evānnaṃ śūdrastriyā pācayet yasya ca grāme nivasato ‘pi ūḍhānūḍhā vā śūdrā gṛhiṇī bhavati dvividho ‘pi sa vipro havyakavye ‘nuṣṭhātum ayogyaḥ san narakam āpnoti || 12.36 ||
śūdrānnavat sūtakānnaṃ nindati |
mṛtasūtakapuṣṭāṅgo dvijaḥ śūdrānnabhojanaḥ |
ahaṃ tan na vijānāmi kāṃ kāṃ yoniṃ gamiṣyati || 12.37 ||
[30]        gṛdhro dvādaśa janmāni daśa janmāni sūkaraḥ |
śvayonau saptajanmā syād ity evaṃ manur abravīt || 12.38 || iti |
mṛtāśauce jātāśauce vā aśaucino ‘nnam anyo yo vipro daśasv api dineṣu bhuktvā śarīraṃ puṣṇāti so ‘yaṃ mṛtasūtakapuṣṭāṅgo vipraḥ bahuṣu kaṣṭayoniṣu jāyate | kāṃ kāṃ yoniṃ gamiṣyatīty etat na jāne ity uktvā yonīnām atikaṣṭatvaṃ varṇitam | na tu svajñānaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam | anyathā gṛdhrādijanmodāharaṇaṃ vyahanyeta | na ca – gṛdhrādivākye manur abravīd ity abhidhānāt tatpūrvakaṃ svājānam upavarṇitam iti śaṅkanīyam, tadvarṇane prayojanābhāvāt | manuśabdodāharaṇaṃ prāmāṇyadāṛḍhyārtham | tathā ca sati śūdrānnasūtakānnayoḥ kaṣṭatvasyātyantaṃ pramitatvāt | tad ubhayaṃ sarvathā varjanīyam ity uktaṃ bhavati |
|| 12.37–38 ||
śūdrārthe homānuṣṭhānaṃ pratiṣedhati |
dakṣiṇārthaṃ tu yo vipraḥ śūdrasya juhuyād dhaviḥ |
brāhmaṇas tu bhavet śūdraḥ śūdras tu brāhmaṇo bhavet || 12.39 ||
[31] iti | yo vipraḥ śūdrād dakṣiṇām ādāya tadīyaṃ haviḥ śāntipuṣṭyādisiddhaye vaidikair mantrair juhoti tasya brāhmaṇasyaiva tatra pratyavāyaḥ | śūdras tu homaphalaṃ labhed iti |
|| 12.39 ||
atha bhojanakāle maunaṃ vidadhāti |
maunavrataṃ samāśritya āsīno na vaded dvijaḥ |
bhuñjāno hi vaded yas tu tad annaṃ parivarjayet || 12.40 || iti |
yas tu maunena bhoktavyam iti vrataṃ taporūpeṇa svīkṛtya vartate sa bhoktum āsīna utthānaparyantaṃ kim api vacanaṃ na vadet | yadi pramādād bhojanamadhye kiṃcid vākyaṃ vadet tadānīm avaśiṣṭam annaṃ parityajet na tu bhuñjīta || 12.40 ||
bhojanapātreṇa jalapānaṃ niṣedhati |
ardhe bhukte tu yo vipraḥ tasmin pātre jalaṃ pibet |
hataṃ daivaṃ ca pitryaṃ ca ātmānaṃ copaghātayet || 12.41 || iti |
kāṃsyādipātre prakṣiptam annaṃ bhuktvā bhojanasamāpteḥ prāg eva tat pātraṃ uddhṛtya jalaṃ yaḥ pibet tenānuṣṭhitaṃ havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca devān pitṝn na tarpayet | tena doṣeṇa svātmānaṃ narake pātayitvopaghātayet || 12.41 ||
[32]
paṅktyucchiṣṭakāritvaṃ pratiṣedhati |
bhuñjāneṣu tu vipreṣu yo ‘gre patraṃ vimuñcati |
sa mūḍhaḥ sa ca pāpiṣṭho brahmaghnaḥ sa kalūcyate || 12.42 || iti |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭeṣu bhuñjāneṣu satsu tan madhye kaścid itarabhojanasamāpteḥ pūrvam eva svapātraṃ parityajya bhojanānte culakaṃ pītvottiṣṭhati tasya nindātimahatī | tadartham eva mūḍhādiśabdāḥ prayuktāḥ || 12.42 ||
śrāddhakāle bhojanapātracalanāt pūrvaṃ svastivācanaṃ niṣedhati |
bhājaneṣu na tiṣṭhatsu svasti kurvanti ye dvijāḥ |
na devās tṛptim āyānti nirāśāḥ pitaras tathā || 12.43 || iti |
asnātabhojanādikaṃ niṣedhati |
asnātvā naiva bhuñjīta ajaptvāgnim ahūya ca |
parṇapṛṣṭhe na bhuñjīta rātrau dīpaṃ vinā tathā || 12.44 || iti |
yady api snānajapahomānāṃ bhojanakālāt prācīnakāle [33] vihitatvād asnātabhojanādikaṃ na prasaktaṃ tathāpi kadācit kenacin nimittena svasvakāleṣv ananuṣṭhiteṣu yadi bhojanakālaḥ prāptaḥ syāt tadā snānādikam akṛtvā bhojanaṃ na kurvīta | tathā patrāvalyāṃ bhuñjānaḥ patrāṇām antarbhāge bhuñjīta, na tu pṛṣṭabhāge | tathā rātrāv andhakāre na bhuñjīta || 12.44 ||
athāpahārādinā dhanārjanaṃ pratiṣedhati |
gṛhasthas tu dayāyukto dharmam evānucintayet |
poṣyavargārthasiddhyarthaṃ nyāyavartī subuddhimān || 12.45 ||
nyāyopārjitavittena kartavyaṃ hy ātmarakṣaṇam |
anyāyena tu yo jīvet sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || 12.46 || iti |
gṛhasthaḥ putramitrakalatrādayaḥ sarvathā rakṣaṇīyā ity anayā dayayā yukto ‘pi poṣyavargapoṣaṇarūpasyārthasya siddhaye dharmam eva śāstrīyam eva yājanādirūpaṃ dravyārjanopāyaṃ cintayet | evaṃ ca sati nyāyavartī san paralokaviṣayapradhānatvena buddhimān bhavati | tasmāt nyāyopārjitenaiva yājanādisādhyena vittenātmanaḥ kuṭumbasya ca rakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam | yas tv [34] anyāyena cauryāpalāpādinā labdhena vittena jīvat sa daive pitrye ca karmaṇy anarho bhavati | yat tu manuvacanam,
vṛddhau ca mātāpitarau satī bhāryā sutaḥ śiśuḥ |
apy akāryaśataṃ kṛtvā bhartavyā manur abravīt || (MDh 11.11)
iti, tad āpadviṣayam || 12.45–46 ||
atha puṇyavastudarśanaṃ vidhatte |
agnicit kapilā sattrī rājā bhikṣur mahodadhiḥ |
dṛṣṭamātrāḥ punanty ete tasmāt paśyet tu nityaśaḥ || 12.47 || iti |
iṣṭakācayanasaṃskṛtenāgninā yukto ‘gnicit | kapilā kapilavarṇā dhenuḥ | sattrī dvādaśāham ārabhya sahasrasaṃvatsaraparyanteṣu sattreṣu yasya kasyacit sattrasyānuṣṭhātā | śāstrīyeṇa mārgeṇa prajānāṃ pālako rājā | caturthāśramasya yathāvat pālako bhikṣuḥ | mahodadhir lavaṇasamudrādiḥ | ete dṛṣṭāḥ santo draṣṭāraṃ punanti | kuśahiraṇyādayo draṣṭavyaviśeṣāḥ punanto ‘pi dhāraṇādikam apekṣante | na tu darśanamātreṇa punanti | atas tadvailakṣaṇyāya dṛṣṭamātrāḥ ity uktam || 12.47 ||
rakṣaṇamātreṇa śuddhipradān padārthān āha |
[35]        araṇiṃ kṛṣṇamārjāraṃ candanaṃ sumaṇiṃ ghṛtam |
tilān kṛṣṇājinaṃ chāgaṃ gṛhe caitāni rakṣayet || 12.48 || iti |
“yo ‘śvatthaḥ śamīgarbhaḥ” (TaitBr 1.2.8.8) ityādibhir mantraiḥ saṃskṛto vahnim anyenopayukteḥ kāṣṭhaviśeṣo ‘raṇiḥ | sumaṇiḥ padmarāgādiḥ || 12.48 ||
atha gocarmadānaṃ vidhātuṃ gocarmanirūpaṇam āha |
gavāṃ śataṃ saikavṛṣaṃ yatra tiṣṭhaty ayantritam |
tat kṣetraṃ daśaguṇitaṃ gocarma parikīrtitam || 12.49 || iti |
ekena vṛṣeṇa sahitaṃ gośataṃ saikavṛṣam | tac ca gośataṃ niyantraṇarahitaṃ viśrāmāya yāvantaṃ bhūpredeśam ākramyāvatiṣthate tāvad bhūpradeśo daśaguṇitaḥ san gocarmaśabdenābhidhāiyate | manus tu prakārāntaram āha |
tiryagyavodarāṇy aṣṭāv ūrdhvā vā vrīhayas trayaḥ |
pramāṇam aṅgulasyoktaṃ vitastir dvādaśāṅgulaḥ ||
vitastidvitayaṃ hastam iti svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt |
daśahastena daṇḍena triṃśad daṇḍaṃ nivartanam ||
pañca cābhyadhikaṃ kṛtvā etat gocarma kīrtitam || iti | (MDh 12.7–8)
vṛddhamanuḥ |
[36]        saptahastena daṇḍena triṃśaddaṇḍaṃ nivartanam |
tāny eva daśa gocarma dātā pāpaiḥ pramucyate || iti | (MDh 12.10)
|| 12.49 ||
pāpakṣayakāmināṃ gocarmadānaṃ vidhatte |
brahmahatyādibhir martyo manovākkāyakarmajaiḥ |
etad gocarmadānena mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ || 12.50 || iti |
manovākkāyakarmāṇi vyāpāras tebhyo jātāni manovākkāyakarmajāni | na caiteṣāṃ trayāṇām alpādhikabhāvena vyavasthitānāṃ sādhāraṇo nivṛttihetur ayukta iti vācyam, atyantāvṛtānāṃ mānasānām īṣadāvṛtānāṃ vācikānāṃ sakṛtkṛtānāṃ kāyikānāṃ ca samatvasaṃbhavāt || 12.50 ||
[37] dānasya phalātiśayahetuṃ pātraviśeṣam āha |
kuṭumbine daridrāya śrotriyāya viśeṣataḥ |
yad dānaṃ dīyate tasmai tad dānaṃ śubhakārakam || 12.51 || iti |
viśeṣata iti padaṃ kuṭumbitvādibhis tribhiḥ pātrātiśayaviśeṣaṇaiḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbandhyate | tathā ca sati yasya mahat kuṭumbaṃ dāridryaṃ cādhikaṃ bahuvedapāṭhena śrotriyatvam atiśayitaṃ tādṛśāya dīyamānaṃ śubhasya phalātiśayasya kārakaṃ bhavati || 12.51 ||
parakīyabhūmyapaharaṇe pratyavāyādhikyam āha |
vāpīkūpataḍāgādyair vājapeyaśatair makhaiḥ |
gavāṃ koṭipradānena bhūmihartā na śudhyati || 12.52 || iti |
vāpyādīnām ekaikasya śuddhihetutvaṃ śāstrāntareṣu prasiddham | tādṛśair bahubhir api na śudhyatīty ukteḥ pratyavāyādhikyaṃ pratīyate | na ca sarvātmanā śuddhir nāstīty evaṃparam idaṃ [38] vacanam | tathā sati suvarṇasteyadharmeṣu saṃbhāvitaśuddhiṣu madhye tatpāṭhānupapatteḥ || 12.52 ||
atha rajasvalāviṣaye kaṃcid viśeṣam āha |
aṣṭādaśadinād arvāk snānam eva rajasvalā |
ata ūrdhvaṃ trirātraṃ syād uśanā munir abravīt || 12.53 || iti |
rajodarśanam ārabhya caturthe divase snātā strī punas tata ārabhya aṣṭādaśadinād arvāg rajasvalā bhavati cet tadā snānam evācaret | na tu trirātrāśaucaṃ kuryāt aṣṭādaśadinād ūrdhvaṃ rajodarśane trirātrāśaucaṃ kartavyam || 12.53 ||
rajasvalāprasaṅgena buddhisthānāṃ cāṇḍālādīnāṃ vyavadhāne deśaparimāṇam āha |
yugaṃ yugadvayaṃ caiva triyugaṃ ca caturyugam |
cāṇḍālasūtikodakyāpatitānām adhaḥ kramāt || 12.54 || iti |
adhaḥ kramād viparītakramād ity arthaḥ | tataś caivaṃ yojanīyam | patitasya vyavadhānam ekena yugena | udakyāyā yugadvayena | sūtikāyā yugatrayeṇa | cāṇḍālasya yugacatuṣṭayeneti | yugaparimāṇaṃ lokavyavahārād avagantavyam || 12.54 ||
[39]
uktavyavadhānābhāve śuddhim āha |
tataḥ sannidhimātreṇa sacailaṃ snānam ācaret |
snātvāvalokayet sūryam ajñānāt spṛśate yai || 12.55 || iti |
mātraśabdena sparśo vyāvartyate | sparśābhāve snānam eva | sparśo ‘pi akāmakṛte snānaṃ sūryāvalokanaṃ ca | kāmakṛte dvaiguṇyaṃ śuddhyantaraṃ vā draṣṭavyam | saṅkaṭādiṣu saty api sannidhau sparśābhāve snānābhāvaḥ, yathāsaṃbhavavyavadhānasya saṃvartenābhyupagatatvāt,
saṃkaṭe viṣame caiva durge caive viśeṣataḥ |
haṭṭapaṭṭanamārge ca saṃbhavaṃ tu yathā bhavet || iti | (SaṃSm 189)
|| 12.55 ||
atha taḍāgādau paśuvat mukhena jalapānaṃ niṣedhati |
vidyamāneṣu hasteṣu brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
toyaṃ pibati vaktreṇa śvayonau jāyate dhruvam || 12.56 || iti |
“snānāni pañca puṇyāni” (ParSm 12.9) ityārabhya [40] “śvayonau jāyate dhruvam” (ParSm 12.56) ity etadantena granthasandarbheṇa yo ‘yam ācāraśeṣaḥ pratipāditaḥ tasya sarvasya prapañcaḥ pūrvakāṇḍe prāyeṇa kṛta iti nātra punaḥ prapañcyate | na caivaṃ sati punaruktiḥ śaṅkanīyā | mūlavacaneṣu tatra saṃgṛhītārthasyātra vivṛtatvāt || 12.56 ||
atha prāyaścittakāṇḍaśeṣaḥ |
tatra kenacin nimittena śapatham ullaṅghitavataḥ prāyaścittam āha |
yas tu kruddhaḥ pumān brūyāj jāyāyās tu agamyatām |
punar icchati ced enāṃ vipramadhye tu śrāvayet || 12.57 ||
śrāntaḥ kruddhas tamondho vā kṣutpipāsābhayārditaḥ |
dānaṃ puṇyam akṛtvā tu prāyaścittaṃ dinatrayam || 12.58 ||
upaspṛśet triṣavaṇaṃ mahānadyupasaṃgame |
cīrṇānte caiva gāṃ dadyād brāhmaṇān bhojayed daśa || 12.59 ||
iti | yaḥ kenacin nimittena kruddhaḥ san svabhāryāyā agamyatāṃ pratijānīyāt – “ataḥ paraṃ tvāṃ na gamiṣyāmi | tvaṃ mama mātā bhaginīti” | punar api kālāntare krodhopaśame sati enāṃ gantuṃ yadīcchati [41] tadā parṣadagre svakīyaṃ pāpaṃ vinivedayet |
“ahaṃ śapathapratijñāvelāyāṃ grāmāntaragamanena gṛhavyāpāreṇa vā śrānta āsam | madīye vacasy ullaṅghite sati kruddho ‘bhūvam | tasyā aparādhābhāve ‘pi tamasā bhrāntyā andhaḥ parāmarśarahito 'bhūvam | kṣudhā pipāsayā rājādibhayena vā pīṣita āsam | ata evamādidoṣaprayuktam idam agamyatāpratijñānam | na tu vivekapūvakam | tasmād asya pāpasya prāyaścittam anugṛhṇantu bhavanta”
iti | yas tu dānaṃ kariṣyāmīti śapathapūrvaṃ pratijñāya tayaivāśayā samāgatāya brāhmaṇāya kim api na prayacchati, tathā kāśīyātrādikaṃ saṃkalpya yathā kathaṃcit kiṃcit dūraṃ gatvā paścād aśraddhāṃ prāptas tat puṇyaṃ na karoti teṣv eteṣu triṣu nimitteṣu viprair nirdiṣṭam idaṃ prāyaścittam ācaritavyam | dvayor mahānadyoḥ saṅgame gatvā triṣavaṇasnānapūrvakaṃ trirātropavāsaṃ kuryāt | caturthadine gāṃ dadyāt daś brāhmaṇāṃś ca bhojayet | eteṣv ādyanimitteṣu varṇabhedena prāyaścittam āha yamaḥ |
svabhāryā tu yadā krodhād agamyeti ca yo vadet |
prājāpatyaṃ cared vipraḥ kṣatriyo divasān nava ||
ṣaḍrātraṃ tu cared vaiśyas trirātraṃ śūdra ācaret || iti | (YaSm 6.13)
“etasya kāryasyākaraṇe caturṣu varṇeṣu anyatamaṃ hatavān [42] asmi” – iti śapathaṃ kṛtvā yas tat kāryaṃ na karoti tasya prāyaścittaṃ yama eva āha |
viprasya vadhasaṃuyuktaṃ kṛtvā tu śapathaṃ nṛpaḥ |
brahmahā yāvakānnena vrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
kṣatriyasya parākaṃ tu prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
vṛṣalasya trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ śūdrahaṇaś caret ||
kecid āhur apāpaṃ tu vṛṣalasya vadhaṃ mṛṣā |
naitan mama mataṃ yasmāt taddhastena bhavaty asau || iti | (YaSm 6.16–18)
manur apy anṛtaprāyaścittam āha |
vāgdaivatais tu carubhir yajeraṃs te sarasvatīm |
anṛtasyainasas tasya kurvāṇā niṣkṛtiṃ parām ||
[43]        kūṣmāṇḍair vāpi juhuyād ghṛtam agnau yathāvidhi |
ud ity ṛcā vā vāruṇyā tṛcenābdaivatena ca || iti | (MDh 8.105–06)
prajāpatiḥ |
anṛtī somapaḥ kuryāt trirātraṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vā juhuyāt saptavatyā ghṛtena tu || iti |
pratiśrutyānṛtoktau hārīta āha |
pratiśrutyānṛtaṃ brūyān mithyā satyam athāpi vā |
sa taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || iti |
brahmacāriviṣaye garga āha |
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā anṛtoktau vrataṃ caret |
māsaṃ bhuktvā brahmacārī punaḥsaṃskāram ācaret ||
[44]        abhyāse caindavaṃ proktaṃ naiṣṭhiko dviguṇaṃ caret |
vanasthas triguṇaṃ kuryād yatiḥ kuryāc caturguṇam ||
māṃsāśane ‘nṛtoktau ca śavanirharaṇe tathā || iti |
kvacit tu nimittaviśeṣe anṛtam api buddhipūrvaṃ vaktavyam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
tatpāvanāya nirvāpyaś caruḥ sārasvato dvijaiḥ || iti | (YaSm 2.83)
satyābhibhāṣaṇe gurutarapāpasya varṇivadhasya nimittatā prasajjyeta | tat pāpaṃ mā bhūd iti svalpapāpam anṛtam api vaktavyam | tasya śuddhaye sārasvataś carur nirvāpyaḥ | varṇivadhavad ātmavadhaprāptāv api anṛtaṃ vadet | tad āha vyāghraḥ |
yatroktāv ātmanaḥ pīḍā nitāntaṃ tu bhaved yadi |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ vyāghrasya vacanaṃ yathā || iti ||
|| 12.57–59 ||
[45]
durbrāhmaṇagṛhabhojane prāyaścittam āha |
durācārasya viprasya niṣiddhācaraṇasya ca |
annaṃ bhuktvā dvijaḥ kuryād dinam ekam abhojanam || 12.60 ||
iti | durācāratvaṃ vihitānanuṣṭhānam | yo vipro vihitaṃ nānutiṣṭhati niṣiddhaṃ tv ācarati tasya gṛhe bhuktvā dinam ekam upavaset || 12.60 ||
tadaśaktau prāyaścittāntaram āha |
sadācārasya viprasya tathā vedāntavedinaḥ |
bhuktvānnaṃ mucyate pāpād ahorātrāntarān naraḥ|| 12.61 ||
iti | ekasmin dine sakṛd asakṛd vā durbrāhmaṇagṛhe bhojanena yat kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tasya sadācāraviprasya vedāntigṛhe vā bhojanena śuddhiḥ | yad vā ekasmin dine saṃprāptaṃ yat pipīlikāvadhādikṣudrapāpajātaṃ tat sarvaṃ viśiṣṭānnabhojanena viśudhyati | pavitragṛhānnabhojanena pāpaviśuddhiṃ baudhāyano ‘py āha |
[46]        bhaikṣyāhāro ‘gnihotribhyo māsenaikena śudhyati |
yāyāvaravanasthebhyo daśabhiḥ pañcabhir dinaiḥ ||
ekāhadhanino ‘nnena dinenaikena śudhyati |
kapotavrataniṣṭhasya pītvāpaḥ śudhyate tribhiḥ || iti | (BDh 4.5.27–28)
|| 61 ||
maraṇakālopahateḥ prāyaścittam āha |
ūrdhvocchiṣṭam adhocchiṣṭam antarikṣam ṛtau tathā|
kṛcchratrayaṃ prakurvīta aśaucamaraṇe tathā|| 12.62 || iti |
maraṇakāle vāntyādikam ūrdhvocchiṣṭaṃ mūtrādikam adhocchiṣṭam | tayor anyatarad yadā saṃpadyate tadā saṃskartā putrādir dhanādinā pratyāmnāyena prājāpatyatrayaṃ kuryāt | macakādau maraṇam antarikṣamṛtiḥ | rajasvalāsūtikāmaraṇam aśaucamaraṇam | tasmin dvividhe ‘pi maraṇe kṛcchratrayaṃ kuryāt || 12.62 ||
prājāpatyakṛcchrasya caturaḥ pratyāmnāyān āha |
[47]        kṛcchraṃ devyayutaṃ caiva prāṇāyāmaśatadvayam |
puṇyatīrthe ‘nārdraśiraḥsnānaṃ dvādaśasaṃkhyayā || 12.63 ||
dviyojane tīrthayātrā kṛcchramekaṃ prakalpitam | iti |
devī gāyatrī | tasyā ayutasaṃkhyāko japo devyayutam | sa ekaḥ pratyāmnāyaḥ | prāṇāyāmānāṃ śatadvayaṃ dvitīyaḥ pratyāmnyāyaḥ | anārdraṃ śiro yasyāsāv anārdraśirās tasya snānam anārdraśiraḥsnānam | sakṛt snātvā tadaṅgānuṣṭhānaṃ ca vidhāya keśān śoṣayitvā tato dvitīyaṃ snānam ācaret | evaṃvidhaṃ snānadvādaśakaṃ puṇyatīrthe kṛtaṃ tṛtīyaḥ pratyāmnāyaḥ | puṇyatīrtham uddiśya yojanadvayagamanaṃ caturthaḥ | tathodakavāsādayo ‘pi pratyāmnāyā avagantavyāḥ | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
kṛcchro ‘yutaṃ ca gāyatryā udavāsas tathaiva ca |
dhenupradānaṃ viprāya samam etac catuṣṭayam ||
tilahomasahasraṃ tu vedasyādhyāyam eva ca |
viprā dvādaśa vā bhojyāḥ pāvakeṣṭis tathaiva ca ||
anyāni vā pavitrāṇi samāny āhur manīṣiṇaḥ | iti || 12.63 ||
atha retaḥskhalanaprāyaścittam āha |
[48]        gṛhasthaḥ kāmataḥ kuryād retaḥskhalanaṃ bhuvi || 12.64 ||
sahasraṃ tu japed devyāḥ prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ saha | iti |
akāmakṛte yājñavalkya āha |
yan me ‘dya reta ity ābhyāṃ skannaṃ reto ‘bhimantrayet |
stanāntare bhruvor madhye tenānāsikayā spṛśet || iti | (YDh 3.278)
“yan me ‘dya retaḥ” (TaitĀ 1.30.2) ity ekasyā ṛcaḥ pratīkam idam | “punar mām aitv indriyam” (TaitĀ 1.30.2) ity uparitano mantras tābhyāṃ mantrābhyām anāsikayā reta ādāya stanayor bhruvayor vā madhyam upaspṛśet | yatīnāṃ vanavāsināṃ ca pratnotsarge kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
yatnotsargaṃ gṛhī kṛtvā vāruṇībhir upaspṛśet |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
[49] iti | svapne tu kāśyapa āha |
sūryasya trir namaskāraṃ svapne siktvā gṛhī caret |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva triḥ kuryād aghamarṣaṇam || iti | (KāśSm 6.11)
brahmacāriṇaṃ prati manur āha |
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śukram akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam arcayitvā triḥ punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || iti | (MDh 2.181)
bhayādau prajāpatir āha |
bhaye roge tathā svapne siktvā śukram akāmataḥ |
ādityam arcayitvā tu punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || iti | (PraSm 3.19)
naiṣṭhikasya hārīta āha |
upakurvāṇas tu yat kuryāt kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ kuryād brahmacārī ca naiṣṭhikaḥ || iti || 12.64 ||
atha brahmavadhasya mahāpatakasya prayaścittam āha |
[50]        caturvidyopapannas tu vidhivad brahmaghātake || 12.65 ||
samudrasetugamanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet | iti |
ṛgvedādayaś catasro vedāś caturvidyāḥ | tābhir upapannas tadadhyayanānuṣṭhānavān | etena “cāturvaidyo vikalpī ca” (AṅSm 3.19) ityādir aṅgirasā proktā sarvā pariṣad upalakṣyate | samudre dāśarathinā baddhaḥ setuḥ samudrasetuḥ | tadyātrāṃ brāhmaṇaghātake puruṣe yathāvidhyanuṣṭheyatvena nirdiśet || 12.65 ||
vidhivad ity uktam | ko ‘sau vidhir ityākāṅkṣayā taditikartavyatām āha |
setubandhapathe bhikṣāṃ cāturvarṇyāt samācaret || 12.66 ||
varjayitvā vikarmasthān chatropānadvivarjitaḥ | iti |
caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ yāni karmāṇi śāstravihitāni tebhyo viruddhāni vikarmāṇi teṣu tātparyeṇa tiṣṭhantīti vikarmasthāḥ | tān varjayitvā yathāśāstraṃ vartamānāc cāturvarṇyāt setubandhamārge bhikṣāṃ caret | chatropānadvivarjita iti na kevalaṃ bhikṣāvelāyām | kiṃ tu mārgagamane ‘pi draṣṭavyam || 12.66 ||
[51]
bhikṣamāṇena vaktavyam uktiprakāram āha |
ahaṃ duṣkṛtakarmā vai mahāpātakakārakaḥ || 12.67 ||
gṛhadvāreṣu tiṣṭhāmi bhikṣārthī brahmaghātakaḥ | iti |
pāpaprakhyāpanasya prāyaścittāṅgatvāt pratibhikṣāgṛham ahaṃ duṣkṛtakarmetyādivākyena svapāpaṃ prakhyāpayet | aprakhyāpane bādham āha aṅgirāḥ |
kṛtvā pāpaṃ na gūheta gūhyamānaṃ vivardhate | iti | (AṅSm 1.47)
duṣkṛtakarmā, mahāpātakakārako, brahmaghātaka iti padatrayasya mahāsāmānyāvāntarasāmānyatadviśeṣavācitvād apunaruktiḥ || 12.67 ||
adhvaśrāntasyāvasthātuṃ deśaviśeṣam āha |
gokuleṣu vasec caiva grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca || 12.68 ||
tapovaneṣu tīrtheṣu nadīprasravaṇeṣu vā | iti |
bahūnāṃ gavāṃ grāme sthāpayitum aśakyatvenāraṇye sthāpanāya kalpito vrajapradeśo gokulam | yatra vyāghrādibhayāt grāmād bahir nivāso ‘śakyaḥ | tatra grāme nagare vā praviśya [52] gośālādevāyatanādau puṇyapradeśe nivaset | asati tu vyāghrādibhyo bhaye tapovanādiṣu nivaset | nadīprasravaṇebhyo ‘nyāni tīrthāni pampāsarovarādīni || 12.68 ||
na kevalaṃ bhikṣāgṛheṣv eva pāpaprakhyāpanam | kiṃ tu nivāsasthāneṣv apīty āha |
eteṣu khyāpayann enaḥ puṇyaṃ gatvā tu sāgaram || 12.69 ||
daśayojanavistīrṇaṃ śatayojanam āyatam |
rāmacandrasamādiṣṭanalasaṃcayasaṃcitam || 12.70 ||
setuṃ dṛṣṭvā samudrasya brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati | iti |
yathokaprakāreṇa pāpaprakhyāpanapuraḥsaraṃ yātrāṃ kṛtvā setau dṛṣṭe sati tat pāpakṣayaḥ
|| 12.69–70 ||
setudarśanānantarakartavyam āha |
setuṃ dṛṣṭvā viśuddhātmā tv avagāheta sāgaram || 12.71 ||
[53] iti | spaṣṭo ‘rthaḥ || 12.71 ||
setuṃ draṣṭum aśknuvato bhūpateḥ pakṣāntaram āha |
yajeta vāśvamedhena rājā tu pṛthivīpatiḥ | iti |
aśvamedhe ‘py aśaktasya tadupāsanaṃ veditavyam | tathā ca taittirīyabrāhmaṇe śrūyate: “sarvaṃ vā etena pāpmānaṃ devā atarann api vā etena brahmahatyām ataran sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajate ya u cainam evaṃ veda” iti (TS 5.3.12.1.2) | tadupāsanaṃ ca “uṣā vā aśvasya” (TS 7.6.25.1) [54] ityādau vājasaneyibrāhmaṇe taittirīyabrāhmaṇe ca prapañcitam || 12.72 ||
setuyātrāṃ samāpya punaḥ pratyāgatasya kartavyam āha |
punaḥ pratyāgato veśmavāsārtham upasarpati || 12.72 ||
saputraḥ saha bhṛtyaiś ca kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
gāś caivaikaśataṃ dadyāc caturvidyeṣu dakṣiṇām || 12.73 ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena brahmahā tu vimucyate | iti |
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādo nāma – vipāpo viśuddhas tvam asīty evamādyuktiḥ | idaṃ ca vrataṃ guṇavihīnasya brāhmaṇasya vadhe draṣṭavyam | asminn eva viṣaye vratāntaram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pātre dhanaṃ vā paryāptaṃ datvā śuddhim avāpnuyāt | iti | (YDh 3.250)
vidyātapoyukte pātre gobhūhiraṇyādikaṃ jīvanopāyaparyāptaṃ dadyāt | vāśabdena sarvasvaṃ saparicchedaṃ vā gṛhaṃ dadyāt | tad āha manuḥ |
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ vā jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam || iti | (MDh 11.76)
aputrasya sarvasvadānaṃ putravataḥ saparicchadaṃ gṛhadānam iti vyavasthā || 12.72–73 ||
athānyamunipraṇītānāṃ vrataviśeṣāṇāṃ vyavasthāṃ varṇayāmaḥ | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
śiraḥkapālī dhvajavān bhikṣāśī karma vedayan |
brahmahā dvādaśābdāni mitabhuk śuddhim āpnuyāt || iti | (YDh 3.243)
svavyāpāditabrāhmaṇaśiraḥkapāladhvajavān, “kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam” (MDh 11.72) iti manusmaraṇāc ca, “brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaṃ ghātayitvā tasyaiva śiraḥkapālam ādāya tīrthāny anusaṃcaret” (ŚāSm 5.26) [56] iti śātātapasmaraṇāc ca | etad ubhayaṃ pāṇinaiva grāhyam, “khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇiḥ” (GDh 22.4) iti gautamasmaraṇāt | khaṭvāṅgaśabdena daṇḍāropitaśiraḥkapālātmako dhvajo gṛhyate | bhikṣārthaṃ tv anyed eva mṛtkapālaṃ grāhyam, “mṛṇmayakapālapāṇir bhikṣāyai grāmaṃ praviśet” (GDh 22.6) iti gautamasmaraṇāt | tathā śaṅkho ‘pi: “brahmahā pariṣadānumataḥ khaṭvāṅgī gardabhājino mṛṇmayapātrapatitaśiṣṭānnabhojī svakarma vikhyāpayan cared bhaikṣyam ekakālāhāraḥ | śūnyāgāranadī-[57]-parvatavṛkṣamūlaguhāniketanaḥ sa evaṃ dvādaśe varṣe śuddhim āpnoti” iti (ŚaṅSm 16.9) | vanādivāsinā tena bhavitavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
brahmahā dvādaśābdāni kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vane vaset |
kṛtavāpano vā nivased grāmānte govraje ‘pi vā |
āśrame vṛkṣamūle vā sarvabhūtahite rataḥ || iti | (MDh 11.72, 78)
“kṛtavāpano vā” iti vāśabdena jaṭādhāraṇena saha vikalpo ‘vagamyet | ata eva saṃvartaḥ: “brahmahā dvādaśābdāni vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī” iti (SaṃSm 109) | lohitamṛṇmayakhaṇḍaśarāveṇa bhikṣā grāhyā | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: [58] “lohitakena mṛṇmayakaṇḍaśarāveṇa bhikṣāyai grāmaṃ praviśet” iti (ĀpDh 1.9.24.14) | atra mṛṣṭaṃ labhyata iti saṃkalpam akṛtvā bhaikṣyam ācaret | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni cared bhaikṣyam ekakālāhāraḥ” iti (VaDh 21.9) | idaṃ ca bhaikṣyam aśaktaviṣayam | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
brahmaghnas tu gacched vanavāsī jaṭī dhvajī |
vanyāny eva phalāny aśnan sarvakāmavivarjitaḥ ||
bhikṣārthī vicared grāmaṃ vanyair yadi na jīvati |
cāturvarṇyaṃ cared bhaikṣyaṃ khaṭvākgī saṃyataḥ punaḥ ||
bhaikṣyaṃ caiva samādāya vanaṃ gacchet tataḥ punaḥ |
vanavāsī hy apaḥ spṛśyet sadākālam atandritaḥ ||
khyāpayann ātmanaḥ pāpaṃ brahmaghnaḥ pāpakṛttamaḥ |
anena tu vidhānena dvādaśābdaṃ vrataṃ caret || (SaṃSm 109–12)
[59] iti | niyamāntarāṇy āha yamaḥ |
atha vai brahmahatyāyāṃ khaṭvāṅgī mitabhojanī |
mṛṇmayena kapālena svakarma śrāvayaṃs tathā ||
brāhmaṇāvasathān sarvān devāgārāṇi varjayet |
śocan vinindann ātmānaṃ saṃsmaran brāhmaṇaṃ ca tam ||
cared vrataṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ devabrāhmaṇapūjakaḥ |
evaṃ dṛḍhavrato nityaṃ satyavādī jitendriyaḥ ||
saptāgārāṇy apūrvāṇi yāny asaṃkalpitāni ca |
saṃcaret tāni śanakair vidhūme bhuktivarjite ||
brahmaghne dehi me bhikṣām eno vikhyāpya saṃcaret |
ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣyam alabdhvopavased dinam ||
evaṃ saṃcaramāṇas tu brahmahatyāṃ bruvan sadā |
pūrṇe tu dvādaśe varṣe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || iti | (YaSm 6.21–26)
brahmacaryādiniyamam āha gautamaḥ: “khaṭvāṅgakapālapāṇir vā dvādaśasaṃvatsarān brahmacārī bhikṣāyai grāmaṃ praviśet karmācakṣānaḥ | [60] yathopakrāmet saṃdarśanād āryasya | sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharan savaneṣūdakopasparśī śudhyet” iti (GDh 22.4–6) | kāśyapo ‘pi |
pavitrapāṇir daṇḍī ca paṅkadanto rajasvalaḥ |
tīrthavāsī kuśācchādī jaṭilo brahmahā bhavet || iti | (KāśSm 3.14)
iyaṃ viśuddhir akāmakṛtabrāhmaṇavadhaviṣayā | tathā ca manur etad dvādaśavārṣikam upakramya bahūni vratāny abhidhāyānte nigamayati |
iyaṃ viśuddhir uditā pramāpyākāmato dvijam |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti | (MDh 11.89)
āvṛttabrahmavadhe caturthāt prāg vratam apy āvartanīyam | tad āhatur manu-devalau |
vadheḥ prāthamikād asmād dvitīye dviguṇaṃ caret |
tṛtīye triguṇaṃ caiva caturthe nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ ||
[61] iti |
yat syād anabhisaṃdhāya pāpaṃ karma sakṛtkṛtam |
tasyeyaṃ niṣkṛtir dṛṣṭā dharmavidbhir manīṣibhiḥ || iti |
tad etad devalena sakṛdanuṣṭhānasya sakṛtkṛtapāpaviṣayatvābhidhānāt | “pratinimittaṃ naimittikam āvartanīyam” iti nyāyāc cāvṛttau dvaiguṇyādisiddhiḥ | yas tu na sākṣād brāhmaṇaṃ hanti kiṃ tu tiraskārādidvārāt tannimittatām āpadyate taṃ prati sumantur āha |
tiraskṛto yadā vipro hatvātmānaṃ mṛto yadi |
nirguṇaḥ sahasā krodhād gṛhakṣetrādikāraṇāt ||
traivārṣikaṃ vrataṃ kuryāt pratiloma sarasvatīm |
gacched vāpi viśuddhyarthaṃ tatpāpasyeti niścitam ||
[62] iti | nirnimittaṃ bhartsane sa eva āha |
atyarthaṃ nirguṇo vipro hy atyarthaṃ nirguṇopari |
krodhād vai mriyate yas tu nirnimittaṃ tu bhartsitaḥ ||
vatsaratritayaṃ kuryāt tataḥ kṛcchraṃ viśuddhaye || iti |
yadā punar nimittī atyantaguṇavān ātmaghātī cātyantanirguṇaḥ tad ekavarṣam eva brahmahatyāvrataṃ kuryāt,
keśaśmaśrunakhādīnām akṛtvā vapanaṃ vane |
brahmacaryaṃ caran vipro varṣeṇaikena śudhyati ||
iti tenaivābhidhānāt | yathā hantā prāyaścittī yathā vā nimittī tathaivānumantrādayo ‘pi prāyaścittabhājaḥ | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
hantā mantopadeṣṭā ca tathā saṃpratipādakaḥ |
protsāhakaḥ sahāyaś ca tathā mārgopadeśakaḥ ||
āśrayaḥ śastradātā ca bhaktadātā vikarmiṇaḥ |
upekṣakaḥ śaktimāṃś ced doṣavaktānumodakaḥ ||
[63]        akāryakāriṇas teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet |
tathā śaktyanurūpaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ teṣāṃ prakalpayet || iti |
eteṣāṃ madhye yo yo vadhasya pratyāsannas tasya tasyādhikaṃ prāyaścittam | viprakṛṣṭasya tadapekṣayā nyūnaṃ prāyaścittaṃ prakalpanīyam | sākṣātkartur api vayoviśeṣe prāyaścittasya hrāso bhavati | tad āha yamaḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti vyādhitaś ca tathā striyam || iti | (YaSm 6.41)
kvacit kartuḥ pratinidhim āha sa eva |
apūrṇaikādaśābdasya caturvarṣādhikasya ca |
prāyaścittaṃ cared brātā pitānyo vātha bāndhavaḥ || (YaSm 6.3)
[64] iti | yat tu tenaivokam,
ato bālatarasyāsya nāparādho na pātakam |
rājadaṇḍo na tasyāsti prāyaścittaṃ ca neṣyate || (YaSm 6.4)
iti, tat prāyaścittālpatvābhiprāyam | na punaḥ sarvātmanā tadabhāvapratipādanaparam,
pādo bāleṣu dātavyaḥ sarvapāpeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ | (ViDh 1.46)
iti viṣnunā bālasya pādābhidhānena bālatarasya tato ‘py alpatvāvagamāt | yad vā mā bhūd bālatarasya pāpam, vacanena sākṣān nivāraṇāt | na hy asti vacanasyātibhāraḥ | yat tu punar madhyamāṅgirovacanam,
gavāṃ sahasraṃ vidhivat pātrebhyaḥ pratipādayet |
brahmahā vipramucyeta sarvapāpebhya eva saḥ || (AṅSm 2.36)
[65] iti, tac ca
dviguṇaṃ savanasthe tu brāhmaṇe vratam ādiśet | (YaSm 3.252)
ity etadvākyavihitadviguṇavratācaraṇāśaktasya veditavyam, prāyaścittasyātigurutvāt | yad api śaṅkhavacanam: “pramāpya dvādaś saṃvatsarān ṣaṭ trīn sārdhaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ ca vratāny ādiśet | teṣām ante gosahasraṃ tasyārdham ardhaṃ ca dadyāt | sarveṣāṃ varṇānām ānupūrvyeṇa” iti, tad ācāryādihananaviṣayam | tathā ca dakṣaḥ |
samam abrāhmaṇe dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve |
ācārye śatasāhasraṃ sodarye dattam akṣayam ||
samadviguṇasāhasram ānantyaṃ ca yathākramam |
dāne phalaviśeṣaḥ syāt hiṃsāyāṃ tadvad eva hi || iti | (DaSm 3.25–26)
yat punar āpastanbenoktam: “viprahāraṇye kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vāgyataḥ śaviśirodhvajaḥ śāṇīpakṣaḥ ātmany upari jānvācchādayet” (ĀpDh 1.9.24.11) [66] ity upakramya, “hatvā śrotriyaṃ vā etad eva vratam ottamād ucchvāsāc caret | nāsmiṃl loke pratyāpattir vidyate” (ĀpDh 1.9.24–25) iti, tan mātāpitrācāryādiviṣayam | tathā ca bhaviṣyatpurāṇe,
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hatvā sodaryaṃ bhrātaraṃ tathā |
guruṃ hatvā śrotriyaṃ ca ……………………… ||
ity upakramya asyaiva prāyaścittasya vidhānāt | śāṇīpakṣaḥ śāṇasūtramayavastraikadeśaḥ | ā uttamād ucchvāsāt ā maraṇāt | pratyāpatttiḥ vyavahāraḥ | atrāśaktasya śaṅkha āha: “pramāpya dvādaśasaṃvatsarān ṣaṭ trīn sārdhaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ ca vratāṇy ādiśet | teṣām ante gosahasraṃ tadardhaṃ tasyārdham ardhaṃ ca dadyāt | sarveṣāṃ varṇānām ānupūrvyeṇa” iti | “etad eva vratam ottamād ucchvāsāc caret” iti atrāsamartho dvādaśavārṣikavracaryānantaraṃ gosahasradānaṃ kuryāt | tatrāpy aśaktasya ṣaḍvārṣikavratacaryānantaram ardhaṃ gavāṃ sahasram | tatrāpy aśaktasya trivārṣikavracaryānantaraṃ sārdhaśatadvayaṃ [67] gavām | tatrāpy aśaktasya sārdhasaṃvatsaravracaryā | anantaraṃ pañcaviṃśatyuttaraśatam iti sarvavarṇānāṃ śaktyanusāreṇa ānupūrvyeṇa krameṇa dvādaśavāriṣikādigosahasrādīnāṃ samuccayo bhavati | etac ca pūrvoktaviṣaya eva kalpanīyam | prāyaścittasyātigurutvāt |
nanu – dvādaśavārṣikādivratacatuṣṭayaṃ gosahasrādidānacatuṣṭayasahitam abhidhāya, “sarveṣāṃ varṇānām ānupūrvyeṇa” ity abhidhānāt brāhmaṇādivarṇacatuṣṭayasya krameṇa vratacatuṣṭayavidhānaṃ kim iti na syāt |
maivam –
parṣad yā brāhmaṇānāṃ tu sā rājñāṃ dviguṇā bhavet |
vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā proktā parṣadvac ca vrataṃ smṛtam || (AṅSm 3.16)
iti | tathā,
śūdro vā brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā prāyaścittacatuṣṭayam |
iti yathākramam aṅgiro-bhaviṣyatpurāṇavacanābhyāṃ brāhmaṇāpekṣayā itaravarṇānām adhikaprāyaścittavidhānāt | tasmād asmaduktaiva vyavasthā jyāyasī iti |
nanu – evaṃ vacanāntaraṃ sākṣitvenodāhṛtya yatra viśeṣavyavathocyate tatra sāstu | yatra tv antareṇaiva vacanāntaraṃ vyavasthocyate tatra svakapolakalpitā kathaṃ śraddheyā – iti cet |
maivam, smṛtikārair eva kalpanīyābhidhānāt | tathā ca devalaḥ |
[68]        jātiśaktiguṇāpekṣaṃ sakṛd buddhikṛtaṃ tathā |
anubandhādi vijñāya prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
yad idaṃ dvādaśavārṣikaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ tad dvādaśe varṣe saṃpūrṇe vā samāpanīyam arvāg vā brāhmaṇatrāṇādinimittalābhe | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ: “dvādaśe varṣe śuddhim āpnoti | antarā vā brāhmaṇaṃ mocayitvā | gavāṃ dvādaśānāṃ paritrāṇāt | sadya evāśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānād vā pūto bhavati” iti | evaṃ ca sati yad yājñavalkyenoktam,
brāhmaṇasya paritrāṇād gavāṃ dvādaśakasya vā |
tathāśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānād vā śuddhim āpnuyāt ||
dīrghatīvrāmayagrastaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gām athāpi vā |
dṛṣṭvā pathi nirātaṅkaṃ kṛtvā vā brahmahā śuciḥ ||
ānīya viprasarvasvaṃ hṛtaṃ ghātita eva vā |
tannimittaṃ kṣataḥ śastrair jīvann api na duṣyati || (YDh 3.244–46)
iti, na tad vratāntarābhiprāyam | kiṃ tu samāptikathanābhiprāyam | vratāntarāṇi tu manur āha |
lakṣyaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syād viduṣām icchayātmanaḥ |
prāsyed ātmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākśirāḥ ||
yajeta vāśvamedhena svarjitā gosavena vā |
abhijidviśvajidbhyāṃ vā trivṛtāgniṣṭutāpi vā ||
japan vānyatamaṃ vedaṃ yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet |
brahmahatyāpanodāya mitabhuṅ niyatendriyaḥ ||
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ vā jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam ||
[70]        haviṣyabhug vānucaret pratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm |
japed vā niyatāhāras trir vai vedasya saṃhitām || iti | (MDh 11.73–77)
ete sarve pakṣāḥ kāmakārākāmakāravidvadavidvadviṣayatvena vyavasthāpanīyāḥ | kāmakṛtātyantābhyāse tu yājñavalkya āha |
lomabhyaḥ svāhety evaṃ hi lomaprabhṛti vai tanum |
majjāntaṃ juhuyād vāpi mantrair ebhir yathākramam || (YDh 3.247)
[71] iti | tatrāṣṭau mantrān āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
lomāni mṛtyor juhomi lomabhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti prathamām |
tvacaṃ mṛtyor juhomi tvacā mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti dvitīyām |
lohitaṃ mṛtyor juhomi lohitena mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti tṛtīyām |
māṃsaṃ mṛtyor juhomi māṃsena mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti caturthīm |
medo mṛtyor juhomi medasā mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti pañcamīm |
snāyuṃ mṛtyor juhomi snāyvā mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti ṣaṣṭhīm |
[72]        asthīni mṛtyor juhomi asthibhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti saptamīm |
majjānaṃ mṛtyor juhomi majjābhir mṛtyuṃ vāsaye | iti ṣaṣṭamīm | (VaDh 20.26)
iti | maraṇāntikasyedṛśasya sarvasya vratasya kāmakāraviṣayatvaṃ madhyamāṅgirā āha |
prāṇāntikaṃ ca yat proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
tat kāmakāraviṣayaṃ vijñeyaṃ natra saṃśayaḥ ||
yaḥ kāmato mahāpāpaṃ naraḥ kuryāt kathaṃcana |
na tasya śuddhir nirdiṣṭā bhṛgvagnipatanād ṛte || iti |
yat tu sumantunoktam: “brahmahā saṃvatsaraṃ kṛcchraṃ caret | adhaḥśāyī triṣavaṇasnāyī karmāvedako bhaikṣyāhāro divyanadīpulinasaṅgamāśramagoṣṭhaparvataprasravaṇatapovana-vihārī syāt sthānavīrāsanī | saṃvatsare pūrṇe hiraṇyamaṇigodhānyatilabhūmisarpīṃṣi brāhmaṇēbhyo dadan pūto bhavati” [73] iti, tad apahantur mūrkhasya dhanavato jātimātravyāpādane draṣṭavyam | yat punar vasiṣṭhavacanam: “dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo dvādaśarātram upavaset” (VaSm 23.38) iti, tan mano’vasitabrahmahatyasya tadaivaoparatajighāṃsasya veditavyam | yat punaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśanmatavacanam,
ṣaṇḍhaṃ tu brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ prakurvīta parākadvayam eva ca ||
iti, tad apratyāneyapuṃstvasya sapratyayavadhe draṣṭavyam | apratyayavadhe tu bṛhaspatir āha |
aruṇāyāḥ sarasvatyāḥ saṅgame lokaviśrute |
śudhyet triṣavaṇasnāyī trirātropoṣito dvijaḥ ||
[74] iti | etāni dvādaśavārṣikādidhanadānaparyantāni brāhmaṇasyaiva | kṣatriyādes tu dviguṇādikam | yad āha aṅgirāḥ |
parṣad yā brāhmaṇānāṃ tu sā rājñāṃ dviguṇā matā |
vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā proktā parṣadvac ca vrataṃ smṛtam || iti |
prajāpatir api |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi ca |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ brāhmaṇasya vadhe vratam || iti |
yat tu caturviṃśatimatavacanam,
prāyaścittaṃ yad āmnātaṃ brāhmaṇasya maharṣibhiḥ |
pādonaṃ kṣatriyaḥ kuryād ardhaṃ vaiśyaḥ samācaret ||
śūdraḥ samācaret pādam aśeṣeṣv api pāpmasu ||
iti, tat prātilomyānuṣṭhitacaturvidhasāhasavyatiriktaviṣayam | mūrdhāvasiktādīnām api daṇḍavat prāyaścittaṃ samūhanīyam | daṇḍatāratamyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
daṇḍapraṇyanaṃ kāryaṃ varṇajātyuttarādharaiḥ | iti | (YDh 2.206)
evaṃ ca sati mūrdhāvasiktasya brāhmanavadhe brāhmaṇād atiriktaṃ kṣatriyād ūnam adhyardhadvādaśavārṣikaṃ bhavati | [75] anayaiva diśā prātilomyotpannānāṃ api prāyaścittam ūhanīyam | tathottamāśramiṇām api prayaścittatāratamyam aṅgirasā darśitam |
gṛhasthoktāni pāpāni kurvanty āśramiṇo yadi |
śaucavac chodhanaṃ kuryur arvāk brahmanidarśanāt || iti |
prakrāntaprāyaścittasya madhye vipattāv api pāpakṣayo bhavati | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
prāyaścitte ‘dhyavasite kartā yadi vipadyate |
pūtas tad ahar eva syād iha loke paratra ca || iti | (HārSm 21.9)
vyāso ‘pi |
dharmārthaṃ yatamānas tu na cet śaknoti mānavaḥ |
prāpto bhavati tat puṇyam atra me nāsti saṃśayaḥ || iti | (VyāSm 3.41)
vadhodyame ‘pi vadhaprāyaścittaṃ kartavyam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
cared vratam ahatvāpi ghātārthaṃ cet samāgataḥ | (YDh 3.252)
[76] iti | yathāvarṇam ity anuvartate | ata eva smṛtyantaram |
ahatvāpi yathāvarṇaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | iti | (ŚāSm 1.26)
|| 12.72–73 ||
savanasthastrīvadhasya mahāpātakatvam abhipretya prāyaścittam āha |
savanasthāṃ striyaṃ hatvā brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret || 12.74 ||
iti | āhitāgner jāyā pativratā savanasthā | ata eva aṅgirāḥ |
āhitāgner dvijāgryasya hatvā patnīm aninditām |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ kuryād ātreyīghnas tathaiva ca || iti | (AṅSm 3.19)
|| 12.74 ||
[77]
brahmahatyāyāḥ prāyaścittam abhidhāya madyapānasya prāyaścittam āha |
madyapaś ca dvijaḥ kuryān nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
cāndrāyaṇe tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam || 12.75 ||
anaḍutsahitāṃ gāṃ ca dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām | iti |
panasādijanyaṃ madakāraṇaṃ dravadravyaṃ madyam | tad āha pulastyaḥ |
pānasaṃ drākṣamādhūkaṃ khātjūraṃ tālam aikṣavam |
madhūtthaṃ sairim āriṣṭaṃ maireyaṃ nārikelajam ||
[78]        samānāni vijānīyān madyāny ekādaśaiva tu | iti | (PulSm 1.12–13)
ekādaśānām anyatamasya madyasya pāne mahānadītīre cāndrāyaṇaṃ caritvā brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | tad etat kāmakāraviṣayam | akāmakāre tu bṛhaspatir āha |
pītvā pramādato madyam atikṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ |
kārayet tasya saṃskāraṃ śaktyā viprāṃs tu bhojayet || iti | (BṛSm 4.23)
tad etan madyapānaprāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva, na kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ, madyapānasya brāhmaṇaṃ praty eva niṣedhāt,
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnaṃ madyamāṃsasurāsavam |
tad brāhmaṇena nāttavyaṃ devānām aśnatā haviḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt | [79] bṛhadviṣṇur api |
mādhūkam aikṣavaṃ sairaṃ tālaṃ khārjūrapānasam |
madhūtthaṃ caiva mādhvīkaṃ maireyaṃ nārikelajam ||
amedhyāni daśaitāni madyāni brāhmaṇasya tu | iti |
rājanyavaiśyayos tu madyapānam anujānāti vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
kāmād api ca rājanyo vaiśyo vāpi kathaṃcana |
madyam eva surāṃ pītvā na doṣaṃ pratipadyate || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
ubhau madhvāsavakṣībau ubhau candanacarcitau |
ekapaṅktyaśitau dṛṣṭau me keśavārjunau ||
[80] iti | upanayanāt pūrvaṃ madhyapāne tatprāyaścittaṃ pitrādibhiḥ kartavyam | tad āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
anupetas tu yo bālo madyaṃ mohāt pibed yadi |
tasya kṛcchratrayaṃ kuryāt mātā bhrātā tathā pitā || iti || 12.75 ||
madyapānasya prāyaścittam uktvā surāpānasya prāyaścittam āha |
surāpānaṃ sakṛt kṛtvā agnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet || 12.76 ||
sa pāvayed athātmānam iha loke paratra ca | iti |
piṣṭādijanyo dravadravyaviśeṣo surā | tathā ca manuḥ |
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijottamaiḥ || iti | (MDh 11.94)
tatra paiṣṭyāṃ surāśabdo mukhyaḥ, gauḍīmādhvyor gauṇaḥ, pulastyenaikādaśasu madyeṣu gauḍīmādhvyāv anukramya, [81] “surāyāḥ sakṛtpāne gauḍīmādhvyor amukhyasurayor asakṛtpāne cāgnisamānavarṇotpattiparyantaṃ saṃtaptām atyuṣṇāṃ surāṃ pītvā brāhmaṇo mriyeta” (PulSm 1.14) ity abhidhānāt | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
surāpāne kāmakṛte jvalantī caiva tāṃ mukhe |
kṣipet tayā vinirdagdho mṛtaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt || iti | (BṛSm 4.25)
gauḍīmādhvyor abhyāsena matyā amatyā vā pāne tayā maraṇaṃ vyāghra āha |
matyā madyam amatyā vā punaḥ pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
tato ‘gnivarṇāṃ tāṃ pītvā mṛtaḥ śudhyet sa kilbiṣāt || iti |
kāmakṛte sakṛt paiṣṭīpāne mūlavacanoktaṃ maraṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | akāmakṛte tu yājñavalkya
āha |
vālavāsā jaṭī vāpi brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret |
piṇyākaṃ vā kaṇān vāpi bhakṣayet trisamā niśi || iti | (YDh 3.254)
etac ca varṣatrayapiṇyākādibhakṣaṇaṃ chardanaviṣayam | [82] tathā ca varṣatrayavrataṃ prakṛtya vyāsa āha |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryāt madyapaḥ chardane kṛte |
pañcagavyaṃ ca tasyoktaṃ pratyahaṃ kāyaśodhanam || iti | (VyāSm 2.51)
yat tu manunoktam,
kaṇān vā bhakṣayed abdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛn niśi |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī || (MDh 11.92)
iti, tat tālumātrasaṃyoge draṣṭavyam | yad api devalenoktam: “surāpāne brāhmaṇo rūpyatāmrasīsānām anyatamam agnikalpaṃ pītvā śarīraparityāgāt pūto bhavati” iti, yad api manunoktam,
[83]        gomūtram agnivarṇaṃ vā pibed udakam eva vā |
payo ghṛtaṃ vāmaraṇāt gośakṛdrasam eva ca || (MDh 11.91)
iti, tad ubhayaṃ mūlavacanena samānaviṣayam | vedavidaṃ praty aṅgirā āha |
bṛhaspatisaveneṣṭvā surāpo brāhmaṇaḥ punaḥ |
samatvaṃ brāhmaṇair gacched ity eṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ || iti |
sakṛdgauṇasurāpāne aṅgirā āha |
bhūmipradānaṃ yaḥ kuryāt surāṃ pītvā dvijottamaḥ |
punar na ca pibej jātu saṃskṛtaḥ sa viśudhyati || iti |
yat tu sumantunoktam: “brāhmaṇasya surāpasya ṣaṇmāsān anudvṛtasamudrodakasnānam | sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt pratyaham | trirātram upavāsaḥ | taptakṛcchreṇa pūto bhavati | aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānena ca” [84] iti, tat pūrvoktabṛhaspatisavaviṣaya eva draṣṭavyam | yat punar bṛhaspatinoktam,
gauḍīṃ mādhvīṃ surāṃ paiṣṭīṃ pītvā vipraḥ samācaret |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca cāndrāyaṇam anukramāt || (BṛSm 4.61)
iti, tad ananyaiṣadhasādhyavyādhyupaśamārthapāne veditavyam, prāyaścittasyālpatvāt | ata eva hetoḥ saṃkalpamātraviṣayatve vā yojanīyam || 12.76 ||
atha suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittam āha |
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ tu brāhmaṇasya tataḥ svayam || 12.77 ||
gacchen musalam ādāya rājābhyāśaṃ vadhāya tu |
hataḥ śuddhim avāpnoti rājñāsau mukta eva ca || 12.78 ||
kāmatas tu kṛtaṃ yat syān nānyathā vadham arhati | iti |
atra suvarṇaśabdaḥ parimāṃaviśeṣopetahemavacanaḥ | sa ca parimāṇaviśeṣo yājñavalkyena darśitaḥ |
[85]        jālasūryamarīcisthaṃ trasareṇurajaḥ smṛtam |
te ṣṭau likṣā tu tās tisro rājasarṣapa ucyate ||
gauras tu te trayaḥ ṣaṭ te yavo madhyas tu te trayaḥ |
kṛṣṇalaḥ pañca te māṣaḥ te suvarṇas tu ṣoḍaśa || iti | (YDh 1.362–63)
uktaparimāṇaviśiṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇaṃ yo ‘paharati sa svaviśuddhaye svayam eva rājakartṛkaṃ maraṇaṃ saṃpādayituṃ prahārasādhanaṃ musalam ādāya rājasamīpaṃ gacchet | gatvā cedṛśaṃ māṃ prahareti nivedayet | tato rājakartṛkāt susalasādhanād vadhād ayaṃ viśuddhyatīti | atha musalaprahāreṇa na mṛtas tadā rājñā muktaḥ śuddhiṃ prāpnoty eva | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
tato musalam ādāya sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam |
yadi jīvati sa stenas tataḥ steyād vimucyate || iti | (SaṃSm 120–21)
atha vā – ‘mukta eva ca iti pakṣāntaram eva vyākhyeyam | yatra stenena ye poṣyās tadīyaputramitrakalatrādayo bahavaḥ santi tatra stene mṛte bahavo vinaśyantīti matvā kṛpālunā rājñā [86] dhanadaṇḍādipuraḥsaraṃ steno ‘prahato mucyeta tadāpi viśuddhyaty eveti |
nanu – evaṃ sati “aghnann enasvī rājā” (GDh 12.45) iti gautamavacanāt rājā pratyavāyī syāt |
na, tasya poṣyavargādikṛpānimittatvena stenavyatiriktaviṣaye ‘py upapatteḥ |
nanu – brāhmaṇavadhasyātigarhitatvāt “mukta eva” ity ayaṃ pakṣas tadviṣayatvena kuto na vyākhyāyate |
manuvacanavirodhād iti brūmaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
suvarṇasteyakṛd vipro rājānam abhigamya tu |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyān māṃ bhavān anuśāstv iti ||
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛd dhanyāt tu taṃ svayam |
vadhena śuddhyati steno brāhmaṇas tapasaiva vā || iti | (MDh 11.99–100)
atra vadhapakṣo nāmadhārakamātravipraviṣayaḥ | tapaḥpakṣas tu savanasthatvādiguṇopeta-brāhmaṇaviṣayaḥ | na ca – tādṛśasya steyam asaṃbhāvitam – iti śaṅkanīyam, kadācit kvacic cattagatatāmasavṛttyā lobhātiśayodaye sati tatsaṃbhavāt | evaṃ ca sati “mukta eva” iti pakṣa etadviṣayatvenāpi yojayituṃ śakyate | yac cauryaṃ kāmakṛtaṃ syāt tatrāyam ukto vadho draṣṭavyaḥ | anyathā [87] prāmādādikacaurye vadhaṃ nārhati | na ca – lobhaikamūlasya cauryasya prāmādikatvam asaṃbhāvitam – iti śaṅkanīyam | yadā vastraprāntagrathitaṃ suvarṇaṃ suvarṇatvenājñātvāpaharati apahṛtya cānyasmai dadāti nāśayati vā na punaḥ svāmine pratyarpayati tadā saṃbhavaty evākāmato ‘py apahāraḥ | tādṛśo ‘pahāre savanasthabrāhmaṇavat tapasā śuddhir draṣṭavyā | tapaso viśeṣas tu manunā darśitaḥ |
tapasāpanunutsus tu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam |
vālavāsā dvijo ‘raṇye cared brahmahaṇo vratam || iti | (MDh 11.101)
suvarṇāt nyūnaparimāṇeṣu hemasu vratabhedāḥ ṣāṭtriṃśanmate darśitāḥ |
bālāgramātre ‘prahṛte prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret |
likṣāmātre ‘pi ca tathā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ budhaḥ ||
rājasarṣapamātre ca prāṇāyāmacatuṣṭayam |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ ca japet pāpaviśuddhaye ||
gausarṣapamātre tu sāvitrīṃ vai dinaṃ japet |
yavamātrasuvarṇasya prāyaścittaṃ dinadvayam ||
suvarṇakṛṣṇalaṃ hy ekam apahṛtya dvijottamaḥ |
kuryāt sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tatpāpasyāpanuttaye ||
[88]        apahṛtya suvarṇasya māṣamātraṃ dvijādhamaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāras tribhir māsair viśuddhyati ||
suvarṇasyāpaharaṇe vatsaraṃ yāvakaṃ pibet |
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇāntikaṃ jñeyam atha vā brahmahavratam || iti |
vatsaraṃ yāvakāśanaṃ kiṃcin nyūnasuvarṇāpahāraviṣayam | suvarṇāpahāre dvādaśavārṣikavidhānāt | yat tu caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam,
ātmatulyaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā dadyād iṣṭvātha vā kratum |
ṣaḍabdaṃ vā caret kṛcchraṃ japayajñaparāyaṇaḥ ||
tīrthāni bhramate vidvān tataḥ steyāt pramucyate |
tatrātmatulyasuvarṇadānam atyantadhanikaviṣayam | kratur dhanikaśrotriyaviṣayam | steyaprāyaścittaṃ sarvatra apahṛtaṃ dhanaṃ svāmine datvaiva kāryam,
steye brahmasvabhūtasya suvarṇādeḥ kṛte punaḥ |
svāmine ‘pahṛtaṃ deyaṃ hartā tv ekādaśādhikam ||
iti smaraṇāt | yadā tv aśaktyā rājā hantum apravṛttaḥ tadā vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: [89] “stenaḥ prakīrṇakeśo rājānam abhiyāceta | tatas tasmai rājā audumbaraṃ śastraṃ dadyāt | tenātmānaṃ pramāpayet | maraṇāt pūto bhavati” iti (VaDh 20.31) | audumbaraṃ tāmramayam | yadā suvarṇam apahṛtya tad bhuktvā tadānīm evānutāpena pratyarpayet tadā āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “caturthakālamitāśanena trivarṣam avasthānam” iti (ĀpDh 1.9.25.13) | mānasāpahāre tu sumantur āha: “suvarṇasteyī dvādaśarātraṃ vāyubhakṣaḥ pūto bhavati” iti (=VaDh 20.41) | guruśrotriyayāgasthatvādibahuguṇopetabrāhmaṇadravyasya bhūyaso ‘sakṛdapahāre vasiṣṭhoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “niṣkālako ghṛtābhyakto gomayāgninā pādaprabhṛti ātmānaṃ pramāpayet | maraṇāt pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate” (VaSm 20.42) [90] iti | itthaṃ suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittam abhihitam | gurutalpagaprāyaścittaṃ daśamādhyāye “mātaraṃ yadi gacchet tu bhaginīm” (ParSm 10.8) ity atra prapañcitam ||
atha patitasaṃsargaprayaścittam |
yas tu patitair brahmahadibhiḥ saha saṃvatsaraṃ saṃsargaṃ kṛtvā svayam api patitas tasya prāyaścittaṃ manur āha |
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ |
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryat saṃsargasya viśuddhaye || iti | (MDh 11.181)
ācāryas tu kaliyuge saṃsargadoṣābhāvam abhipretya saṃsargaprāyaścittaṃ nābhyadhāt | ata eva smṛtyantare kalau varjyānām anukramaṇe “saṃsargadoṣaḥ pāpeṣu” (SaṃSm 1.53) ity uktam |
|| 12.77–78 ||
saṃsargadoṣasya pātityāpādakatvābhāve ‘pi pāpamātrāpādakatvam astīty āha |
āsanāt śayanād yānāt saṃbhāṣāt sahabhojanāt || 12.79 ||
saṃkrāmanti hi pāpāni tailabindur ivāmbhasi |
[91] iti | yathā tailabindur ambhasi prakṣiptaḥ sarvatra vyāpnoti tathā patitasaṃsargakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ saṃsargiṇaṃ puruṣaṃ vyāpnoti || 12.79 ||
saṃsargadoṣavyājena pūrvam anuktaprāyaścittānāṃ sarveṣāṃ sādhāraṇāni prāyaścittāny āha |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ yavakaṃ ca tulāpuruṣa eva ca || 12.80 ||
gavāṃ caivānugamanaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam | iti |
tulāpuruṣaḥ kṛcchraviśeṣaḥ | tatsvarūpam upariṣṭād vakṣyāmaḥ | yatra yatra ca pratipadoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nopalabhyate tatra sarvatra pāpagauravalāghavānusāreṇa cāndrāyaṇādīny āvṛttāny anāvṛttāni vānuṣṭheyāni | tataḥ sarvāṇi pāpāni naśyantīti siddham || 12.80 ||
itthaṃ prāyaścittakāṇḍe navabhir adhyāyaiḥ prakīrṇakādīnāṃ mahāpātakāntānāṃ pāpānāṃ yāni prāyaścittāny ācāryeṇābhihitāni tāni vyācakṣāṇair asmābhis tatprasaṅgāpatitāni smṛtyantarāṇy udāhṛtya vyavasthāpitāni |
anudāhṛtasmṛtivacanavyavasthā |
[92]
atha yāni pūrvam anudāhṛtāni tāny udāhṛtya vyavasthāpayāmaḥ |
tatrātipātakasvarūpam |
tatra viṣṇuproktāṃ pāpānukramaṇikām āśritya tadānupūrvyāt prāyaścittavacanāny udāhriyante | tatra viṣṇur ādāv atipātakam anukramya tatsvarūpam evaṃ nirdideśa: “mātṛgamanaṃ duhitṛgamanaṃ snuṣāgamanam ity atipātakāni” iti (ViDh 34.1) | teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ daśamādhyāye “mātaraṃ yadi gacchet tu” (ParSm 10.8) ity asmin prakaraṇe prāyaścittāni nirṇītāni |
mahāpātakasvarūpam |
atipātakānantaraṃ mahāpātakam anukramya tatsvarūpanirdeśa evaṃ krtaḥ: “brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇaṃ gurudāragamanam iti mahāpātakāni | tatsaṃyogaś ca” iti (ViSm 35.1–2) | tatra gurudāragamanavyatiriktānāṃ caturṇāṃ dvādaśādhyāye “caturvidye” (ParSm 12.64) [93] ityādinā, “sarvapāpapraṇāśanam” (ParSm 12.81) ityantena granthena prāyaścittāny uktāni | gurudāragamanasya tu daśamādhyāye “pitṛdārān samāruhya” (ParSm 10.11) ity atra prāyaścittam abhihitam |
anupātakasvarūpam |
mahāpātakānantaram anupātakam anukramya tatsvarūpam evaṃ nirdiṣṭam:
yāgasthasya kṣatriyasya vadhaḥ vaiśyasya rajasvalāyāś cāntarvatnyāś cātigorāyā avijñātasya garbhasya śaraṇāgatasya ca ghātanaṃ brahmahatyāsamānīti | kūṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛvadha ity etau surāpānasamau | brāhmaṇabhūmyapaharaṇaṃ suvarṇasteyasamam | pitṛvyamātāmahamātulaśvaśuranṛpapatnyabhigamanaṃ gurudārābhigamanasadṛśam | pitṛmātṛsvasṛgamanaṃ ca śrotriyartvigupādhyāya-mitrapatnyabhigamanaṃ ca | [94] svasuḥ sakhyāḥ sagotrāyā uttamavarṇāyāḥ kumāryā rajasvalāyāḥ śaraṇāgatāyāḥ pravrajitāyā nikṣiptāyāś ca | (ViDh 36.1–7)
iti |
ath anupātakaprāyaścittam |
tatra prāyaścittaṃ viṣṇur evāha |
anupātakinas tv ete mahāpātakino yathā |
aśvamedhena śuddhyanti tīrthānusaraṇena vā || iti | (ViDh 36.8)
tatrāśvamedhaḥ sārvabhaumarājaviṣayaḥ, “rājā sārvabhaumo ‘śvamedhena yajeta” iti śruteḥ | tīrthasnānam itaraviṣayam | tac ca sādhāraṇaprāyaścittam | yatra pratipadoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nāsti nopalakṣyate vā tatredaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | yāgasthakṣatravadhasya tādṛśavaiśyavadhasya ca ṣaṣṭhādhyāye “vaiśyaṃ vā kṣatriyaṃ vāpi” (ParSm 6.17) ity etasya vyākhyānaprasaṅge prāyaścittaṃ darśitam | tathā [95] cāntarvatnyā atrigotrāyāś ca vadhe tatraiva prāyaścittam abhihitam | avijñātagarbhavadhe prāyaścittaṃ caturthādhyāye “garbhapātaṃ ca yā kuryāt” (ParSm 4.17) ity asmin prasaṅge varṇitam | śaraṇāgatāvadhaḥ pariśiṣyate | manus tv anyāny api brahmahatyāsamāny āha |
anṛtaṃ ca samutkarṣe rājagāmi ca paiśunam |
guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā || iti | (MDh 11.55)
yājñavalkyaś cāparāṇy āha |
gurūṇām adhyadhikṣepo vedanindā suhṛdvadhaḥ |
brahmahatyāsamaṃ jñeyam adhītasya ca nāśanam || (YDh 3.228)
[96] iti | atra śaraṇāgatavadhādiṣu pādanyūnaṃ brahmahatyāvratam avagantavyam | samaśabdasya “rājasamo mantrī” ityādāv īṣannūne prayogadarśanāt | manus tv adhītatyāgādīnāṃ surāpānasamatvam āha |
brahmojjhaṃ vedanindā ca kūṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || iti | (MDh 11.56)
tatra brahmojjhasya prāyaścittaṃ vasiṣṭha āha: “brahmojjhaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punar upayuñjīta vedam ācāryāt” iti (VaDh 20.12) | idaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ prāmādikādhyayana-tyāgaviṣayam | nāstikatayā tattyāge pādanyūnaṃ surāpānavratam iti manor matam | yady api vasiṣṭhenoktam: [97] “guror alīkanirbandhe kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā sacailaṃ snātvā guruprasādāt pūto bhavati” (VaDh 21.28) iti, tad etad amatipūrve sakṛdanuṣṭhāne veditavyam | viṣṇunānukrāntasya kauṭasākṣyasya dvādaśavārṣikaṃ prāyaścittam | tathā ca tadvratānuvṛttau śaṅkha āha |
kauṭasākṣyaṃ tathā kṛtvā nikṣepam apahṛtya ca |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryāt tyaktvā ca śaraṇāgatam || iti | (ŚaṅSm 17.5)
yatra sākṣyanṛtavadanena varṇino vadhaprāptiḥ tadviṣayam idam | aparam api brahmahatyāsamaṃ viṣṇur darśayati |
ākruṣṭas tāḍito vāpi dhanair vā viprayojitaḥ |
yam uddiśya tyajet prāṇāṃs tam āhur brahmaghātakam || (ViDh 3.46)
[98] iti | ākrośanādinimittābhāve mṛtasyaiva hatyādoṣaḥ, na tūddeśyasya | tathā ca smṛtyantaram|
akāraṇaṃ tu yaḥ kaścid dvijaḥ prāṇān parityajet |
tasyaiva tatra doṣaḥ syān na tu yaṃ parikīrtayet || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi surāpānasamāny udājahāra |
niṣiddhabhakṣaṇaṃ jaihmyam utkarṣe ca vaco ‘nṛtam |
rajasvalāmukhāsvādaḥ surāpānasamāni tu || iti | (YDh 3.229)
evamādiṣu sarvatra yena sāmyam ucyate tadīyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kiṃcin nyūnam anuṣṭheyam | sā ca nyūnatā nimittagauravalāghavānusāriṇī kalpanīyā | suvarṇasteyasame tu brāhmaṇabhūmyapaharaṇe maraṇāntikaprayaścitteṣu nyūnatvakalpanāsaṃbhavāt dvādaśavārṣikādivratāni yathāyogaṃ kalpanīyāni | yājñavalkyo ‘nyāni suvarṇasteyasamāny āha |
aśvaratnamanuṣastrībhūdhenuharaṇaṃ tathā |
nikṣepasya ca sarvaṃ hi suvarṇasteyasaṃmitam || (YDh 3.230)
[99] iti | manur api |
nikṣepasyāpaharaṇaṃ narāśvarajatasya ca |
bhūmivajramaṇīnāṃ ca rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam || iti | (MDh 11.57)
pitṛvyapatnyādigamanānāṃ gurutalpasamānānāṃ daśamādhyāye “mātṛṣvasṛgame caivam” (ParSm 10.9) ity asmin prakaraṇe prapañcitam |
upapātakasvarūpam |
anupātakānantaraṃ viṣṇur upapātakam anukramya tatsvarūpam evaṃ nirdideśa |
anṛtavacanam utkarṣe | rājagāmi ca paiśunam | guroś cālīkanirbandhaḥ | vedanindā | adhītasya ca tyāgaḥ | agnimātṛpitṛsutadārāṇāṃ ca | abhojyānnābhakṣyabhakṣaṇam | parasvaharaṇam | paradārābhigamanam | ayājyayājanam | vikarmaṇā jīvanam | asatpratigrahaḥ | kṣatriyaviṭśūdragovadhaḥ | avikreyavikrayaḥ | [100] parivittitānujena jyeṣṭhasya | parivedanaṃ ca | tasya ca kanyādānam | yājanaṃ ca | vrātyatā | bhṛtakādhyapanam | bhṛtakādhyayanādānam | sarvākareṣv adhīkāraḥ | mahāyantrapravartanam | drumagulmalatauṣadhīnāṃ hiṃsā | striyā jīvanam | abhicāraḥ | mūlakarmasu ca pravṛttiḥ | ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambhaḥ | anāhitāgnitā | steyam | devarṣipitṝṇām ṛṇasyānapakriyā | asacchāstradhigamanam | nāstikatā | kuśīlatā | [101] madyapastrīniṣevaṇam | ity upapātakāni | (ViDh 37.1–36)
iti | yājñavalkyas tu viṣṇunānuktāny api kānicid upapātakāni ṛṇānapakriyādīny udājahāra |
govadho vrātyatā stainyam ṛṇānāṃ cānapakriyā |
anāhitāgnitāpaṇyavikrayaḥ parivedanam ||
bhṛtād adhyayanādānaṃ bhṛtakādhyāpanaṃ tathā |
pāradāryaṃ pārivittyaṃ vārdhuṣyaṃ lavaṇakriyā ||
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho ninditārthopajīvanam |
[102]        nāstikyaṃ vratalopaṃ ca sutānāṃ caiva vikrayaḥ ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyam ayājyānāṃ ca yājanam |
pitṛmātṛsutatyāgas taṭākārāmavikrayaḥ ||
kanyāsaṃdūṣaṇaṃ caiva parivedakayājanam |
kanyāpradānaṃ tasyaiva kauṭilyaṃ vratalopanam ||
[103]        ātmano ‘rthe kriyārambho madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
svādhyāyāgnisutatyāgo bāndhavatyāga eva ca ||
indhanārthaṃ drumacchedaḥ strīhiṃsauṣadhajīvanam |
hiṃsrayantravidhānaṃ ca vyasanāny ātmavikrayaḥ ||
śūdrapreṣyaṃ hīnasakhyaṃ hīnayoniniṣevaṇam |
tathaivānāśrame vāsaḥ parānnaparipuṣṭatā ||
asacchāstrādhigamanam ākareṣv adhikāritā |
bhāryāyā vikrayaś caiṣām ekaikam upapātakam || iti | (YDh 3.234–42)
yady api “anṛtaṃ ca samutkarṣe” (MDh 11.55) ity etan manunā brahmahatyāsameṣu paṭhitaṃ yājñavalkyena surāpānasameṣu viṣṇunā upapātakeṣu tathāpi viṣayabhedena traividhyaṃ vaktuṃ śakyatvān na kiṃcid api sāṅkaryam | viṣayabhedas tūcyate | dveṣyaṃ puruṣaṃ rājabhṛtyādibhir mārayituṃ tasminn avidyamānam api mahāntam [104] aparādham āropyānṛtaṃ ced brūyāt tad brahmahatyāsamam, vadhaparyavasāyitvāt | yas tu lābhapūjākhātikāmo rājasabhādau svasmin avidyamānam api caturvedābhijñatvaṃ prakaṭayitum anṛtaṃ brūte tat surāpānasamam, atigarhitatvāt | yas tu sukhagoṣṭhyādau paropakāram antareṇa vṛthānṛtaṃ brūte tasyaitad upapātakam | tatrādyayoḥ prāyaścittaṃ pūrvam evoktam | tṛtīye tu kāmakṛte yājñavalkya āha |
mayi teja iti chāyāṃ svāṃ dṛṣṭvāmbugatāṃ japet |
sāvitrīm aśucau dṛṣṭe cāpalye cānṛte ‘pi ca || iti | (YDh 3.279)
akāmakṛte tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
suptvā kṣutvā ca bhuktvā ca niṣṭhīvyoktvānṛtāni ca |
pītvāpo ‘dhyeṣyamāṇaś ca ācāmet prayato ‘pi san || iti | (MDh 5.145)
rājño ‘gre sākṣāt paramparayā vā paradoṣakathanaṃ rājagāmi paiśunyam | gurāv alīkanirbandho dvividhaḥ | tatra rājagṛhādau svayaṃ samarthaḥ san asamarthasya guror dravyalābhādyuparodhakāraṇaṃ nirbandhaṃ karoti cet soyam alīkanirbandho brahmahatyāsamatvena manunoktaḥ | yatra vākpāruṣyādimātreṇa guror apriyam upajāyate [105] so ‘yam alīkanirbandha upapātakam | vedanindā trividhā | buddhārhatādiśāstrāṇy abhyasya vedānām aprāmāṇyapratipādane nirbandha ekā nindā | seyaṃ yājñavalkyena brahmahatyāsameṣūpavarṇitā | satyām api vedaprāmānyabuddhau jalpavitaṇḍādihetuśāstra-vyasanitayā śrutismṛtyuktasyānuṣṭhānasyāvajñā dvitīyā nindā | etad evābhipretya manur āha |
yo ‘vamanyeta te tūbhe hetuśāstrāśrayāt dvijaḥ |
sa sādhubhir bahiṣkāryo nāśtiko vedanindakaḥ || iti | (MDh 2.11)
seyaṃ nindā surāpānasameṣu manunodāhṛtā | yas tu vaidikaṃ karmānutiṣṭhann api nātyantaṃ tatphale śraddhatte “kim anena bhaviṣyati janāpavādabhayād eva kevalam anutiṣṭhāmi” ity evaṃ nindanti seyaṃ nindopapātakeṣu paṭhyate |
evam adhyayanatyāgāder ekaikasya bahuvidhasyānukrāntasya gauravalāghave paryālocyāvāntarabhedaḥ kalpanīyaḥ | “agnimātṛpitṛsutadārāṇām” (ViDh 37.6) ity atrāpi tyāga iti padam anveti | anukrāntānām upapātakānāṃ pūrvoktebhyo ‘tipātakamahāpātakānupātakebhyo ‘lpatvād upapātakatvam | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
[106]        mahāpātakatulyāni pāpāny uktāni yāni tu |
tāni pātakasaṃjñāni tannyūnam upapātakam || iti |
atha upapātakasya sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittam |
tasya copapātakajātasya sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viṣṇur āha |
upapātakinas tv ete kuryuś cāndrāyaṇaṃ narāḥ |
parākam atha vā kuryur yajeyur gosavena ca || iti | (ViDh 37.35)
atraiteṣām āvṛttānāṃ saṅghāte gosavo draṣṭavyaḥ | ekaikasya ca śaktaṃ prati parākaḥ aśaktaṃ prati cāndrāyaṇam | yājñavalkyo ‘pi sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
upapātakaśuddhiḥ syād evaṃ cāndrāyaṇena vā |
payasā vāpi māsena parākeṇātha vā punaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.265)
evam ity anena padena prakṛtaṃ govadhavratam atidiśyate, atideśasyopadeśān nyūnaviṣayatvāt | govratāṅgāni gocarmādīni kānicid viṣayāntareṣu nivartante | etac ca vratacatuṣṭayam akāmakāre śaktyapekṣayā vikalpitaṃ draṣṭavyam | kāmakāre tu,
[107]        etad eva vrataṃ kuryur upapātakino dvijāḥ |
avakīrṇivarjyaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || (MDh 11.117)
iti manunoktaṃ traimāsikaṃ draṣṭavyam | etac ca sādhāraṇaprāyaścittaṃ pratipadoktaprāyaścittābhāve tadanupalambhe vā draṣṭavyam |
pratipadoktaprāyaścittāni |
pratipadoktāni prāyaścittāni yathāsaṃbhavam udāharāmaḥ | tatra guror alīkanirbandhopapātakasya prāyaścittaṃ viṣnur āha: “samutkarṣe ‘nṛte guror alīkanirbandhe tadākṣepaṇe ca māsaṃ payasā varteta” iti (ViDh 54.54) | agnityāge sa evāha: “vedāgnyutsādī triṣavaṇasnāyy adhaḥśāyī saṃvatsaraṃ sakṛdbhaikṣeṇa varteta” iti (ViDh 54.13) | vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “yo ‘gnīn apavidhyet sa kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā punarādhānaṃ kārayet” (ViDh 21.27) [108] iti | manur api |
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caren māsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat ||
agnihotry apavidhyāgnīn māsād ūrdhvaṃ tu kāmataḥ |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva kuryād evāvicārayan || iti | (MDh 11.129–30)
hārītaḥ: “saṃvatsarotsanne ‘gnihotre cāndrāyaṇaṃ kṛtvā punar ādadhyāt | dvivarṣotsanne sāntapanaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca kuryāt | trivarṣotsanne saṃvatsaraṃ kṛcchram abhyasya punar ādadhyat” iti (HārSm 3.19–21) | śaṅkho ‘pi: “abhyutsādī sāṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caret gāṃ ca dadyāt” iti (ŚaṅSm 11.23) | bharadvājo: “dvādaśāhātikrame tryaham upavāsaḥ | māsātikrame dvādaśāham upavāsaḥ | saṃvatsarātikrame māsopavāsaḥ | payobhakṣaṇaṃ ca” [109] iti | etat sarvam ālasyād agnityāgaviṣayatayā yathāyogam ūhanīyam | yat tu bharadvājagṛhye ‘bhihitam: “prāṇāyāmaśatam ā daśarātraṃ kuryāt | upavāsaḥ syād ā viṃśatirātram | ata ūrdhvam ā ṣaṣṭirātraṃ tisro rātrīr upavaset | ata ūrdhvam ā saṃvatsarāt prājāpatyaṃ vrataṃ caret | ata ūrdhvaṃ kālabahutve doṣabahutvam” iti, tat pramādād agnityāgaviṣayam | nāstikyāt tyāge tu vyāghra āha |
yo ‘gniṃ tyajati nāstikyāt prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
anyatra punarādhānaṃ dānam eva tathaiva ca || iti |
prājāpatyasyopalakṣaṇatvāt tyāgakālasya gauravalāghavānusāreṇa vratāntarāṇy ūhanīyāni | vratāntarakartavyam āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
atītakālaṃ juhuyād agnau vipro yavān ayam |
naṣṭe ‘gnau vidhivad dadyāt kṛtvādhānaṃ punar dvijaḥ || iti |
etac caupāsanāgniviṣayam | dāratyāge śātātapa āha: [110] “kaumāradāratyāgī māsaṃ payobhakṣaḥ śudhyati” iti (ŚāSm 1.27) | abhojyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittaṃ tv ekādaśādhyāye prapañcitam |
atha parasvaharaṇaprāyaścittam |
tatra parasvaharaṇe manur āha |
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇi kṛtvā kāmād dvijottamaḥ |
svajātīyagṛhād eva kṛcchrābdena viśudhyati ||
manuṣyāṇāṃtu haraṇe strīṇāṃ kṣetragṛhasya ca |
kūpavāpījalānāṃ ca śuddhiś cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
dravyāṇām alpasārāṇāṃ steyaṃ kṛtvānyaveśmanaḥ |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tan niryāty ātmaśuddhaye ||
bhakṣyabhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya ca |
puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ ca śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca |
[111]        cailacarmāmiṣāṇāṃ ca trirātraṃ syād abhojanam ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca |
ayaḥ kāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānnatā ||
kārpāsakīṭajorṇānāṃ dviśaphaikaśaphasya ca |
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ ca rajjvāś caiva tryahaṃ payaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.162–68)
eteṣāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ madhye dhānyasya parimāṇaviśeṣeṇa vrataviśeṣaḥ smṛtyantare ‘bhihitaḥ |
dhānyaṃ daśabhyaḥ kumbhebhyo harato ‘bhyadhike vadhaḥ | iti |
yat tu jābālinoktam,
tiladhānyānnavastrāṇāṃ śayyānām āmiṣasya ca |
saṃvatsarārdhaṃ kurvīta vratam etat samāhitaḥ ||
iti, tad etad akāmakṛtaviṣayam, alpatvāt | bhakṣyasya tu sakṛdbhojanaparyāptikasyāpahāre paiṭhīnasir āha: “bhakṣyabhojyānnasyodarapūraṇamātraharaṇe trirātraṃ pañcagavyāhāraḥ” iti | yāni tu manūkteṣu viṣayeṣu vratāntarāṇy anyair munibhir [112] darśitāni teṣu vrateṣu yāni samāni na tatra vivādaḥ | nyūnānām akāmakāraviṣayatvam adhikānāṃ tu kāmakṛtābhyāsaviṣayatvam iti vivekaḥ | tatra vacanāni:
dravyāṇām alpasārāṇāṃ sāntapanam | bhakṣyabhojyayānaśayyāsanamūlapuṣpa-phalānāṃ haraṇe pañcagavyapānam | tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumaśuṣkānnaguḍavastra-carmāmiṣāṇāṃ trirātram upavaset | maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrarajatāyaḥkāṃsyānāṃ dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇān aśnīyāt | kārpāsakīṭa-jorṇādyapaharaṇe trirātraṃ payasā varteta | dviśaphaikaśaphaharaṇe trirātram upavaset | pakṣigandhauṣadhirajjuvaidalānām apaharaṇe dinam upavaset |
datvaivāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dhanikasyābhyupāyataḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kuryāt kalmaṣasyāpanuttaye || iti | (ViDh 52.7–14)
jābāliḥ |
aśvagobhūmikanyāś ca hṛtvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
apahṛtya paśūn kṣudrān prājāpatyaṃ samācaret ||
guḍakārpāsadhānyāni sarpir lavanam eva ca |
pakvānnam auṣadhaṃ tailaṃ śayyāṃ vāsa upānahau ||
[113]        kāṃsyāpastāmrasīsaṃ vā kṛcchraṃ kṛcchrārdham eva ca |
udake phalamūleṣu puṣpaparṇasugandhiṣu ||
mṛdbhāṇḍamadhumāṃseṣu kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate |
vyāpādanāpahāreṣu santoṣya svāminaṃ tataḥ ||
pāpaṃ nivedya viprebhyaḥ prāyaścittena yujyate |
apahṛtya tu varṇānāṃ dhanaṃ vipraḥ pramādataḥ ||
prāyaścittaṃ ca yat proktaṃ brāhmaṇānumate caret |
rājasvasyāpaharaṇe ramaṇīnāṃ janasya ca ||
dhanāpaharaṇe caiva kuryāt saṃvatsaravratam || iti |
paradāragamanasya tu prāyaścittaṃ daśamādhyāye ‘bhihitam |
