ayājyayājanaprāyaścittam |
ayājyayājane manur āha |
[114]        vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣām antyakarma ca |
abhicāram ahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrair vyapohati || iti | (MDh 11.197)
yat tu pracetasā śūdrayājakādīn anukramyoktam: “ete pañcatapo’bhrāvakāśajalaśayanādy anuṣṭheyuḥ krameṇa grīṣmavarṣāhemanteṣu | māsaṃ gomūtrayāvakam aśnīyuḥ” iti, tat kāmakṛtābhyāsaviṣayam | yat tu yamenoktam,
purodhāḥ śūdravarṇasya brahmaṇo yaḥ pravartate |
snehād arthaprasaṅgād vā tasya kṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || (YaSm 6.9)
iti, tad aśaktaviṣayam | yat tu gautamenoktam: “niṣiddhamantraprayoge sahasrānuvākaṃ japet” (GDh 22.33) [115] iti, tat kāmato ‘bhyāsaviṣayam | yat tu paiṭhīnasinoktam: “śūdrayājakaḥ sarvadravyaparityāgāt pūto bhavati | prāṇāyāmasahasreṣu daśakṛtvo ‘bhyasteṣu” iti, tad akāmato ‘bhyāsaviṣayam | asatpratigrahe yājñavalkya āha |
goṣṭhe vasan brahmacārī māsam ekaṃ payovratam |
gāyatrījāpyanirataḥ śudhyate ‘satpratigrahāt || iti | (YDh 3.290)
atra japasaṃkhyā manunā darśitā |
japitvā trīṇi sāvitryāḥ sahasrāṇi samāhitaḥ |
māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā mucyate ‘satpratigrahāt || iti | (MDh 11.194)
etac ca dātṛdravyayor ubhayoḥ sattve veditavyam | anyatarasyāsattve tu ṣaṭtriṃśanmate
darśitam |
pavitreṣṭyā viśudhyanti sarve ghorāḥ pratigrahāḥ |
aindavena mṛgāreṣṭyā kayācin mitarvindayā ||
devyā lakṣajapenaiva śudhyante ‘satpratigrahāt | iti |
yat tu vṛddhahārītavacanam,
rājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kṛtvā māsam apsu sadā vaset |
ṣaṣṭhe kāle payobhakṣaḥ pūrṇe māse pramucyate ||
tarpayitvā dvijān kāmaiḥ satataṃ niyatavrataḥ || (VṛHārSm 11.71–72)
iti, tad āpadi kurukṣetroparāgādau kṛṣṇājinādipratigrahaviṣayam | alpadravyaparigrahe hārītaḥ: “maṇivāsogavādīnāṃ pratigrahe sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet” iti (HārSm 21.3) |
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘pi |
bhikṣāmātre gṛhīte tu puṇyaṃ mantram udīrayet | iti |
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
pratiṣiddheṣu sarveṣu ṣaṣṭhāṃśaṃ parikalpayet | iti |
dānārtham iti śeṣaḥ | vikarmaṇā jīvane tu cāndrāyaṇam | tathā ca naṭādīn vikarmaṇā jīvino ‘nukramya yama āha |
eteṣām eva sarveṣāṃ pratyāpattis tu mṛgyatām |
bhakṣyānnam upayuñjāno dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (YaSm 6.13)
nārado ‘pi |
[117]        karmaṇā garhitenaiva yad vittaṃ samupārjitam |
tasya tyāgena śuddhyanti dharmasyānveṣaṇena vā ||
apsu prāsyeta tad dravyam anyāyena yad āgatam |
upayuktānuśiṣṭaṃ ca deyaṃ vā brahmavādine || iti | (NSm 14.7–8)
kṣatriyādivadhasya prāyaścittaṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāye varṇitam | govadhasya tv aṣṭamanavamādhyāyayos tat prapañcitam |
avikreyavikrayaprāyaścittam |
avikreyavikrayasya caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
surāyā vikrayaṃ kṛtvā caret saumyacatuṣṭayam |
lākṣālavaṇamāṃsānāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
madhvājyatailasomānāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
payaḥ pāyasāpūpānāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
dadhyājyekṣurasānāṃ ca guḍakhaṇḍādivikraye |
sarveṣāṃ snehapakvānāṃ parākaṃ tu samācaret ||
[118]        siddhānnavikraye vipraḥ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
upavāsaṃ tu takrasya naktaṃ kāñjikavikraye ||
pūgīphalāni mañjiṣṭhā drākṣā kharjūram eva ca |
eteṣāṃ vikraye kṛcchraṃ panasasya dinatrayam ||
kadalī nārikelaṃ ca nāgaraṃ bījapūrakam |
eteṣāṃ pādakṛcchraḥ syāj jambīrādes tathaiva ca ||
kastūrikādigandhānāṃ vikraye kṛcchram ācaret |
karpūrādes tadardhaṃ syād dinaṃ hiṃgvādivikraye ||
tilānāṃ vikrayaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
yajñārthaṃ kṛṣijātāṃś ca dānalabdhāṃś ca vikrayet ||
raktapītāni vastrāṇi kṛṣṇājinam athāpi vā |
eteṣāṃ vikraye kṛcchraṃ gargasya vacanaṃyathā ||
govikrayaṃ dvijaḥ kuryāl lābhārthaṃ dhanamohitaḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ prakurvīta gajānām aindavaṃ smṛtam ||
kharāśvājāvikānāṃ ca karabhāṇāṃ ca vikraye |
[119]        parākaṃ tatra kurvīta nṛṇāṃ dviguṇam ācaret ||
nārīṇāṃ vikrayaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
dviguṇaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ ca vratam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ prakurvīta ekāhaṃ vedavikraye |
aṅgānāṃ tu parākaṃ syāt smṛtīnāṃ kṛcchram ācaret ||
itihāsapurāṇānāṃ caret sāntapanaṃ dvijaḥ |
rahasyapañcarātrāṇāṃ kṛcchraṃ tatra samācaret ||
gāthānāṃ nītiśāstrāṇāṃ prākṛtānāṃ tathaiva ca |
sarvāsām eva vidyānāṃ pādakṛcchraṃ samācaret || iti |
hārīto ‘pi:
guḍatilapuṣpamūlaphalapakvānnavikraye saumyāyanam | lākṣālavaṇamadhu-māṃsatailakṣīradadhitakragandhacarmavāsasām anyatamavikraye [120] cāndrāyaṇam | tathorṇākeśakesarabhūdhenuveśmaśastravikraye ca | matsyamāṃsasnāyvasthiśṛṅganakhaśaṅkhaśuktivikraye taptakṛcchram | hiṅgugugguluharitālamanaḥśilāñjanagaurikakṣāralavaṇamaṇimuktāpravāla-vaiṇavamṛnmayeṣu ca | ārāmataṭākodapānapuṣkariṇīsukṛtavikraye triṣavaṇa-snāyy adhaḥśāyī caturthakālāhāro daśasahasraṃ japed gāyatrīṃ saṃvatsareṇa pūto bhavati | hīnamānonmāpanasaṅkīrṇavikraye ca | iti | (HārSm 4.4–10)
īdṛśeṣv ekaviṣayabahuvrateṣu asakṛtpūrvamuktayā nītyā yathāyogaṃ vyavasthā kalpanīyā |
parivittyādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ caturthādhyāye varṇitam |
atha vrātyaprāyaścittam
vrātyo nāma sāvitrīpatitaḥ | tasya prāyaścittaṃ manur āha |
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi |
tāṃś cārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhy upanāyayet || (MDh 11.191)
[121] iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “patitasāvitrika uddālavrataṃ caret | dvau māsau yāvakena vartayet māsaṃ payasārdhamāsam āmikṣayā aṣṭarātraṃ ghṛtena ṣaḍrātram ayācitaṃ haviṣyaṃ bhuñjīta trirātram abbhakṣo ‘horātram upavaset | aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet | vrātyastomena vā yajeta” iti (VaDh 11.76–79) | tatra mānavam āpadviṣayam | uddālakavrataṃ tv anāpadviṣayam | yat tu yamenoktam,
sāvitrī patitā yasya daśavarṣāṇi pañca ca |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ ||
ekaviṃśatirātraṃ ca pibet prasṛtiyāvakam |
haviṣā bhojayec caiva brāhmaṇān sapta pañca ca ||
tato yāvakaśūddhasya tasyopanayanaṃ smṛtam | (YaSm 5.27–29)
iti, tan manusamānaviṣayam | yasya pitrādayo ‘py anupanītāḥ tasya āpastamboktaṃ draṣṭavyam:
atha yasya pitā pitāmaha ity anupetau syātāṃ te brahmahasaṃstutāḥ | teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ [122] vivāham iti ca varjayet | teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittam | yathā prathame ‘tikrame ṛtur evaṃ saṃvatsaraḥ | athopanayanam | tataḥ saṃvatsaram udakopasparśanam | pratipūruṣaṃ saṃkhyāya saṃvatsarān yāvanto ‘nupetāḥ syuḥ | saptabhiḥ pāvamānībhiḥ ‘yad anti yac ca dūraka’ ity etābhir yajuḥpavitreṇa sāmapavitreṇāṅgiraseneti | atha vā vyāhṛtibhir eva | athādhyāpyaḥ | atha yasya prapitāmahāder nānusmaryata upanayanaṃ te śmaśānasaṃstutāḥ | teṣām abhyāgamanaṃ bhojanaṃ vivāham iti ca varjayet | teṣām icchatāṃ prāyaścittam | [123] dvādaśavarṣāṇi traividyakaṃ brahmacaryaṃ cared athopanayanaṃ tata udakopasparśanam | pāvamānyādibhiḥ |
iti | (ĀpDh 1.1.1.32–2.8)
bhṛtakādhyayanādhyāpanaprāyaścittam |
bhṛtakādhyayanādhyāpanayor hārīta āha |
bhṛtakādhyāpanaṃ kurvan bhṛtakādhyāpitaś ca yaḥ |
anuyogapradānena trīn pakṣāṃs tu payaḥ pibet || iti |
drumādihiṃsāyāṃ manur āha |
phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyam ṛkśatam |
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām ||
kṛṣṭajānām auṣadhīnāṃ jātānāṃ ca svayaṃ vane |
vṛthālambhe ‘nugacched gāṃ dinam ekaṃ payovrataḥ || iti | (MDh 11.142–44)
etac ca yajñārthavṛkṣacchedanādivyatiriktaviṣayam | ata eva yājñavalkyo ‘pi vṛtheti viśinaṣṭi |
vṛkṣagulmalathāvīrucchedane japyam ṛkśatam |
syād oṣadhivṛthācchedī kṣīrāśī go’nuto dinam || (YDh 3.276)
[124] iti | yat tu hārītenoktam: “sthāvarasrīsṛpādīnāṃ vadhe yasyedaṃ prāṇam ity etayājyaṃ hutvā tilapātraṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt” (HārSm 21.45) iti, tad aśvatthādipuṇyasthāvaraviṣayam | mahāphalapradasya panasanārīkelādivṛkṣajātasya chednāvṛttau śaṅkha śaṅkha āha |
saṃvatsaravrataṃ kuryāc chitvā vṛkṣaṃ phalapradam | iti | (ŚaṃSm 17.49)
dṛṣṭārthatve na doṣaḥ, “phalapuṣpopagān pādapān na hiṃsyāt | karṣaṇakāryārtham upahanyāt” iti smaraṇāt |
atha nāstikyaprāyaścittam
nāstikyaṃ tu vācikaṃ cet tad upapātakam | yathāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ |
nāstikās trividhāḥ proktā dharmajñais tattvadarśibhiḥ |
kriyāduṣṭo manoduṣṭo vāgduṣṭaś ca tathaiva ca ||
[125]        upapātakas kriyāduṣṭo vāgduṣṭaḥ pātakaḥ smṛtaḥ |
abhyāsāt tu kriyāduṣṭo mahāpātaka iṣyate || iti |
tatropapātakanāstikye vasiṣṭha āha: “nāstikaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā viramen nāstikyāt” iti (VaDh 21.29) | yat tu śaṅkhenoktam: “nāstiko nāstikavṛttiḥ kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī mithyābhiśaṃsīty ete pañca saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛhe bhaikṣaṃ careyuḥ” iti, tad etat mahāpātakanāstikyaviṣayam |
vratalopaprāyaścittam
vratalopino ‘vakīrṇivrataṃ yājñavalkya āha |
avakīrṇī bhaved gatvā brahmacārī tu yoṣitam |
gardabhaṃ paśum ālabhya nairṛtaṃ sa viśudhyati | iti | YDh 3.280)
manur api |
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe |
sthālīpākavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi ||
[126]        hutvāgniṃ vidhivad dhomam antataś ca saṃ mety ṛcā |
vātendraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ ||
etasminn enasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam |
saptāgārān cared bhaikṣyaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan ||
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayann ekakālikam |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam abdena sa viśudhyati || iti | (MDh 11.118–23)
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “brahmacārī cet striyam upeyād araṇye catuṣpathe laukike ‘gnau rakṣodaivataṃ gardabhaṃ paśum ālabhate | nairṛtaṃ vā caruṃ nirvapet | [127] tasya juhuyāt – kāmāya svāhā, kāmakāmāya svāhā, nirṛtaye svāhā, rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāhā” iti (VaDh 23.1–3) | tatra śrotriyasya paśur aśrotriyasya carur iti draṣtavyam | śaṅkhas tu varṇabhedena vrataviśeṣam āha: “guptāyāṃ vaiśyāyām avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ triṣavaṇam anutiṣṭhet kṣatriyāyāṃ dve varṣe | brāhmaṇyāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇi” iti | yad apy aṅgirasoktam,
avakīrṇinimittaṃ tu brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret |
cīravāsās tu ṣaṇmāsān tathā mucyeta kilbiṣāt ||
iti, tad īṣadvyabhicāriṇīviṣayam | atyantavyabhicāriṇīṣu punaḥ śaṅkha āha: [128] “svairiṇyāṃ brāhmaṇyām avakīrṇaḥ ṣaḍrātram upoṣito gāṃ dadyāt | kṣatriyāyām upoṣitas trirātraṃ ghṛtapātraṃ dadyāt | vaiśyāyāṃ caturthakālāhāro brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā yavasabhāraṃ ca gobhyo dadyāt | vṛṣalām avakīrṇaḥ sacailasnāta udakumbhaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt | goṣv avakīrṇaḥ prājāpatyaṃ caret | raṇḍāyām avakīrṇaḥ palālabhāraṃ sīsamāṣaṃ ca dadyāt” iti | atra ca “brāhmaṇasyārdhaṃ kṣatriyaḥ” iti nyāyo nāsti kiṃ tu samānam eva trayāṇāṃ varṇānām | tad āha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
avakīrṇī dvijo rājā vaiśyaś cāpi khareṇa tu |
iṣṭvā bhaikṣāśino nityaṃ śudhyanty abdāt samāhitāḥ || iti |
abhyāse kaṇva āha |
prathame divase rātrāv avakīrṇī gardabhena tu |
yajed evaṃ yathābhyāsam abdenaikena śudhyati ||
[129] iti | tatra viśeṣam āha gautamaḥ: “tasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya lohitapātraḥ sapta gṛhān bhaikṣaṃ caret karmācakṣāṇaḥ” iti (GDh 24.93) | avakīrṇilakṣaṇam āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
skanditaṃ vratinā reto yena syād brahmacāriṇā |
kāmato ‘kāmataḥ prāhur avakīrṇīti taṃ budhāḥ || iti |
yativanasthayos tv adhikaṃ vratam āha śāṇḍilyaḥ |
vānaprastho yatiś caiva skandane sati kāmataḥ |
parākatrayasaṃyuktam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti |
yateḥ punar gārhasthyasvīkāre saṃvarta āha |
saṃnyasya durmatiḥ kaścit pratyāpattiṃ vrajed yati |
sa kuryāt kṛcchram aśrāntaḥ ṣaṇmāsān pratyanantaram || (SaṃSm 159)
[130] iti | vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi |
yaḥ pratyavasito vipraḥ pravrajyāto vinirgataḥ |
anāśakanivṛttaś ca gārhasthyaṃ ca cikīrṣati ||
caret ṣāṇmāsikaṃ kṛcchraṃ niyatātmā samāhitaḥ |
caret trīṇi ca kṛcchrāṇi trīṇi cāndrāyaṇāni ca ||
jātakarmādibhiḥ sarvaiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ śuddhim āpnuyāt || iti |
tatra brāhmaṇasy ṣāṇmāsikakṛcchraḥ kṣatriyasya cāndrāyaṇatrayam, vaiśyasya kṛcchratrayam iti vyavasthā | atra ṣāṇmāsikādivratatrayaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiva sakṛ(da)bhyāsādyapekṣayā vyavasthāpanīyam ity anye | vratāntaralope ‘py avakīrṇivratam atidiśati manuḥ |
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || (MDh 2.187)
[131] iti | yat tu yājñavalkya āha |
bhaikṣāgnikārye tyaktvā tu saptarātram anāturaḥ |
kāmāvakīrṇa ity ābhyāṃ juhuyād āhutidvayam ||
upasthānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt saṃ mā siñcatv anena tu | iti | (YDh 3.281–82)
homamantrau tu: “kāmāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāmakāmāya svāhā | kāmāvapanno ‘smy avapanno ‘smi kāmakāmāya svāheti” (GDh 25.9–10) iti | etac ca guruśuśrūṣādikāryavyagratayākaraṇe draṣṭavyam |
sutādivikrayaprāyaścittam
sutādivikraye upapātakasādhāraṇoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ yojyam | yat tu śaṅkhenoktam: “devagṛha-pratiśrayodyānārāmasabhāprapātaṭākapuṇyasetusutavikrayaṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchraṃ caret” [132] iti, yac ca vṛddhaparāśareṇoktam,
vikrīya kanyakāṃ gāṃ ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
iti, tad āpady akāmato draṣṭavyam | yat tu caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam,
nārīṇāṃ vikrayaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
dviguṇaṃ puruṣasyaiva vratam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
iti, tat tatraiva kāmato draṣṭavyam | yat tu paiṭhīnasir āha: “ārāmataṭākodapānapuṣkariṇīsukṛta-sutavikraye triṣavaṇasnāyy adhaḥśāyī brahmacārī caturthakālāhāraḥ samvatsareṇa pūto bhavati” iti, tad abhyāsaviṣayam ||
kanyādūṣaṇaprāyaścittam
kanyādūṣaṇe upapātakasādhāranaprāyaścittaṃ vijñeyam | yat tu śaṅkha āha: “kanyādūṣī somavikrayī ca kṛcchrābdaṃ careyātām” iti, yac ca hārītaḥ:
kanyādūṣī somvikrayī vṛṣalīpatiḥ kaumāradāratyāgī surāmadyapaḥ śūdrayājako guroḥ pratihantā nāstiko nāstikavṛttiḥ kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī brahmaghno mitraghno mithyābhiśaṃsī patitasaṃvyavahārī mitradhruk śaraṇāgataghātī pratirūpakavṛttir ity ete pañcatapo ‘bhrāvakāśajalaśayanādy anutiṣṭheyur grīṣmavarṣāhemanteṣu | māsaṃ gomūtrayāvakam aśnīyuḥ | (HārSm 2.4–5)
iti, tat prātilaumyena kanyādūṣaṇe draṣṭavyam ||
atha dyūtādivyasanaprāyaścittam
dyūtādivyasaneṣv apy upapātakasādhāraṇoktaṃ prayaścittaṃ yojyam | yat tu baudhāyanaḥ saṃvatsaravratam āha |
athāśucikarāṇi | dyūtam abhicāro ‘nāhitāgner uñchavṛttitā samāvṛttasya bhaikṣacaryā tasya caiva gurukule vāsa ūrdhvaṃ caturbhyo [134] māsebhyo yaś ca tam adhyāpayati | nakṣatranirdeśanaṃ ceti | teṣāṃ tu nirveśaḥ | dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān dvādaśa dvādaśāhān dvādaśaṣaḍahān dvādaśa tryahān dvādaśāhaṃ ṣaḍahaṃ tryaham ekāham iti aśucikarnirveśaḥ | (BDh 2.1.2.15–17)
iti, tad abhyāsaviṣayam | yad apy āha pracetāḥ |
anṛtavāk taskaro rājabhṛtyo vṛkṣāropakavṛttir garado ‘gnidaś ca rathagajārohaṇavṛttiḥ raṅgopajīvī kūṭapaṇikaḥ śūdropādhyāyo vṛṣalīpatir bhāṇḍiko nakṣatropajīvī śvavṛttir brahmajīvī cikitsako devalakaḥ purohitaḥ kitavo madyapaḥ kūṭakārako ‘patyavikrayī manuṣyapaśuvikretā ceti | tān uddharet sametya nyāyato brāhmaṇo vyavasthayā sarvadravyatyāge caturthakālāhārāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ triṣavaṇam [135] upaspṛsyuḥ | tasyānte devapitṛtarpaṇaṃ gavāhnikaṃ cety evaṃ vyavahāryāḥ |
iti, tad abhyāsaviṣayam ||
anāśramavāse prāyaścittam
anāśramavāse hārītena prāyaścittam uktam: “anāśramī saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ caritvāśramam upeyāt | dvitīye ‘tikṛcchraṃ tṛtīye kṛcchrātikṛcchram ata ūrdhvaṃ cāndrāyaṇam” iti |
śūdrasevāprāyaścittam
śūdrasevāyāṃ baudhāyana āha: “samudrasaṃyānam | brāhmaṇanyāsāpaharaṇam | sarvāpaṇyair vyavaharaṇam | bhūmyanṛtam | śūdrasevanaṃ ca | śūdrābhijananam | tadapatyatvaṃ ca | teṣāṃ ca nirveśaḥ | [136] caturthakāle mitabhojinaḥ syur apo ‘bhyupeyuḥ savanānukalpam | sthānāsanābyāṃ viharanta ete tribhir varṣais tad apahanti pāpam” (BDh 2.1.2.2–10) iti, tad bahukālābhyāsaviṣayam |
jātibhraṃśakarapāpaprāyaścittam
upapātakānantaraṃ jātibhraṃśakarāṇi viṣṇunānukrāntāni: “brāhmaṇasya rujaḥkaraṇam | aghreyamadhyayor ghrātiḥ | jyaihmyaṃ paśuṣu maithunācaraṇaṃ puṃsi ca | iti jātibhraṃśakarāṇi” iti | teṣu sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ manu-viṣṇū āhatuḥ |
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamam icchayā |
caret sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam anicchayā || iti |
(MDh 11.124; VDh 37.7)
tatra brāhmaṇasya rujaḥkaraṇe prāyaścittam ekādaśādhyāye [137] ‘bhihitam | paśumaithune tu daśamādhyāye ‘bhihitam |
saṅkarīkaraṇapāpaprāyaścittam
jātibhraṃśakarānantaraṃ saṅkarīkaraṇāny anukrāntavān viṣṇuḥ: “grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ hiṃsā saṅkarīkaraṇam” iti (ViSm 39.1) | tatra sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sa evāha |
saṅkarīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā māsam aśnīta yāvakam |
kṛcchrātikṛccchram atha vā prāyaścittaṃ tu kārayet || iti | (ViDh 39.2)
pratipadaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhādhyāye ‘bhihitam ||
apātrīkaraṇapāpaprāyaścittam
saṅkarīkaraṇānantaram apātrīkaraṇam anukrāntavān viṣṇuḥ: “ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ kusīdajīvanam asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ śūdrasevanam ity apātrīkaraṇam” iti (ViSm 40.1) | tasya ca sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ manur āha |
apātrīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati |
śītakṛcchreṇa vā śuddhir mahāsāntapanena vā || (ViDh 40.2)
[138] iti ||
malinīkaraṇapāpaprāyaścittam
apātrīkaraṇānantaraṃ malinīkaraṇam anukrāntavān viṣṇuḥ: “pakṣiṇāṃ jalacarāṇāṃ jalajānāṃ ca ghātanam | kṛmikīṭānāṃ ca | madyānugatabhojanam | iti malāvahāni” iti (ViDh 41.1–4) | tatra sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sa evāha |
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam |
kṛcchrātikṛcchram atha vā prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam || iti | (ViDh 41.5)
prāyaścittaviśeṣaḥ
atha prakīrṇakaprāyaścittaviśeṣo ‘bhidhīyate | tatra śaraṇāgatatyāge manur āha |
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya ca dvijaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhāras tat pāpam apasedhati || iti | (MDh 11.198)
patitādisaṃnidhāv adhyayane vasiṣṭha āha: “patitacaṇḍālārāvaśravaṇe trirātraṃ vāgyatā [139] anaśnanta āsīran | sahasraparamāṃ vācam abhyasya tataḥ pūtā bhavantīti vijñāyate” iti (ViDh 23.34–35) | etad buddhipūrvakaviṣayam | abuddhipūrvake tu ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘bhihitam: “caṇḍālaśrotrāvakāśe śrutismṛtipāṭhe ekarātram abhojanam” iti | sarpādyantarāgamane tu yama āha |
sarpasya nakulasyātha ajamārjārayos tathā |
mūṣikasya tathoṣṭrasya maṇḍūkasya ca yoṣitaḥ ||
puruṣasyaiḍakasyāpi śuno ‘śvasya kharasya ca |
antarāgamane sadyaḥ prāyaścittam idaṃ śṛṇu ||
trirātram upavāsaś ca trirahaś cābhiṣecanam |
grāmāntaraṃ vā gantavyaṃ jānubhyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti | (YaSm 6.48–50)
kharoṣṭrayānārohaṇādau prāyaścittam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[140]        prāṇāyāmī jale snātvā kharayānoṣṭrayānagaḥ |
nagnaḥ snātvā ca bhuktvā ca gatvā caiva divā striyam || iti | (YDh 3.291)
idaṃ cākāmakāraviṣayam | kāmakāre tu yama āha |
kharoṣṭraviṣamā nārī samāruhyātha vā pumān |
yajane yājane kṛcchram agniś caiva vinaśyati ||
uṣṭrayānaṃ samāruhya kharayānaṃ ca kāmataḥ |
savāsā jalam āplutya prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | (= MDh 11.201)
mūtrapurīṣaprāyaścittam
apsu mūtrapurīṣādikaraṇe manur āha |
vinādbhir apsu vāpy ārtaḥ śārīraṃ saṃniveśya tu |
sacailo bahir āplutya gām ālabhya viśudhyati || iti | (MDh 11.203)
idam akāmakāraviṣayam | kāmakāre tu yama āha |
[141]        āpadgato vinā toyaṃ śārīraṃ yo niṣevate |
ekāhaṃ kṣapaṇaṃ kṛtvā sacailaṃ snānam ācaret || iti | (YaSm 5.26)
yac cāha sumantuḥ: “apsv agnau vā mehatas taptakṛcchraṃ kāyaviśodhanam” iti, tad anārtaparatayā yojyam ||
karmalopaprāyaścittam
śrautasmārtakarmādilope manur āha |
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittam abhojanam || iti | (MDh 11.203)
atropavāsasya pratipadokteṣṭyādinā samuccayaḥ | pañcamahāyajñādyakaraṇe bṛhaspatir āha |
anirvartya mahāyajñān yo bhuṅkte pratyahaṃ gṛhī |
anāturaḥ sati dhane kṛcchrārdhena viśudhyati ||
āhitāgnir upasthānaṃ na kuryād yajñakarmaṇi |
ṛtau na gacched bhāryāṃ vā so ‘pi kṛcchrārdham ācaret || iti | (BSm 2.13–14)
vaiṣamyadānaprāyaścittam
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ vaiṣamyeṇa dānādau yamaḥ |
[142]        na paṅktyāṃ viṣamaṃ dadyān na yācen na ca dāpayet |
prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa mucyate karmaṇas tataḥ || iti |
patitādisaṃbhāṣaṇe tu gautamaḥ: “na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta | saṃbhāṣya puṇyakṛto manasā dhyāyet | brāhmaṇena vā saha saṃbhāṣeta” iti (GDh 9.19–21) | aśucet bhojane śātātapa āha |
mūtroccāraṃ dvijaḥ kṛtvā akṛtvā śaucam ātmanaḥ |
mohād bhuktvā trirātraṃ syād buddhvā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti | (ŚāSm 2.64)
vṛddhaśātātapo ‘pi |
mūtrayitvā vrajan mārgaṃ smṛtibhraṃśāj jalaṃ pibet |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā juhuyāt sarpiṣāhutīḥ || (VṛŚāSm 13–14)
[143] iti | vyāghro ‘pi |
aspṛśyasparśanaṃ kṛtvā yadā bhuṅkte gṛhāśramī |
akāmatas trirātraṃ syāt ṣaḍrātraṃ kāmataś caret ||
yatiś caiva vanasthaś ca kuryātāṃ tau tad eva tu || iti |
uśanā api |
caṇḍālśvapacaiḥ spṛṣṭo viṇmūtrocchiṣṭa eva ca |
trirātreṇa viśuddhaḥ syād bhuktocchiṣṭaḥ ṣaḍ ācaret || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
asnātvā tu yadā bhuṅkte piṇḍaṃ datvā pitur vratī |
spṛṣṭvā śavam udakyāṃ vā caṇḍālaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā ||
akāmatas trirātraṃ syād buddhyā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti |
brahmasūtraṃ vinā mūtrapurīṣādikaraṇe smṛtyantare prāyaścittam uktam |
vinā yajñopavītena yady ucchiṣṭo bhaved dvijaḥ |
prāyaścittam ahorātraṃ gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ tu vā || iti |
idaṃ ca kāmakāraviṣayam | akāmatas tu |
pibato mehataś caiva bhuñjato ‘nupavītinaḥ |
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ naktaṃ ca tritayaṃ kramāt ||
[144] iti | bhojanāntaram ācamanam akṛtvāsanād utthāne prāyaścittam āśvamedhike ‘bhihitam |
yady uttiṣṭhaty anācānto bhuktavān āsanāt tataḥ |
sadyaḥsnānaṃ prakurvīta so ‘nyathāprayato bhavet || iti |
apāṅkteyapaṅktau bhojane mārkaṇḍeya āha |
apāṅkteyasya yaḥ kaścit paṅktau bhuṅkte dvijottamaḥ |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || iti |
nīlīraktavastradhāraṇe tv āpastamba āha |
nīlīraktaṃ yadā vastraṃ brāhmaṇo ‘ṅgeṣu dhārayet |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
romakūpair yadāgacched raso nīlyās tu kasyacit |
[145]        trivarṇeṣu ca sāmānyaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
pālanaṃ vikrayaś caiva tadvṛttyā tūpajīvanam |
patitas tu bhaved vipraḥ tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśudhyati ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam |
vṛthā tasya mahāyajñā nīlīsūtrasya dhāraṇāt ||
nīlīmadhye tu gacched vā pramādād brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
nīlīdāru yadā bhindyād brāhmaṇasya śarīrakam |
śoṇitaṃ dṛśyate tatra dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
strīṇāṃ krīḍārthasaṃbhoge śayanīye na duṣyati ||
nīlīraktena vastreṇa bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam || (ĀpSm 6.1–8)
[146] bhṛgur api |
strīdhṛtā śayane nīlī brāhmaṇasya na duṣyati |
nṛpasya vṛddhavaiśyasya parvavarjyaṃ hi dhāraṇāt ||
kambale paṭṭasūtre ca nīlīrāgo na duṣyati | iti |
brahmatarunirmitakhaṭvādyārohaṇe śaṅkhaḥ |
adhyāsya śayanaṃ yānam āsanaṃ pāduke tathā |
dvijaḥ palāśavṛkṣasya trirātraṃ tu vratī bhavet ||
kṣatriyas tu raṇe ‘datvā pṛṣṭhaṃ prāṇaparāyaṇaḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ vrataṃ kuryāc chittvā pippalapādapam ||
dvau viprau brāhmaṇāgnī vā dampatī godvijottamau |
antareṇa yadā gacchet kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret ||
homakāle tathā dohe svādhyāye dārasaṃgrahe |
antareṇa yadā gacchet dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || (ŚaṃSm 17.48–51)
[147] iti | duḥsvapnāriṣṭadarśanādau sa evāha |
duḥsvapnāriṣtadarśanādau ghṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca dadyāt | iti |
tīrthayātrām antareṇa deśāntaragamane devalaḥ prāyaścittam āha |
sindhusaurāṣṭrasauvīrān tathā pratyantavāsinaḥ |
aṅgavaṅgakaliṅgāś ca gatvā saṃskāram arhati || iti | (DevSm 61)
sūryodayādikālaśayane prāyaścittam āha yamaḥ |
sūryodaye tu yaḥ śete sūryābhyudita ucyate |
astaṃ gate tu yaḥ śete sūryanirmukta ucyate ||
brahmastenāv ubhau samyag ahorātroṣitau śucī |
gāyatryā daśasāhasraṃ kuryād ekāhnikaṃ pṛthak || iti |
manur api |
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmakārataḥ |
nimloced vāpy avijñānāt japann upavased dinam || (MDh 2.220)
[148] iti | gautamas tu brahmacāriṇo viśeṣam āha: “sūryābhyudito brahmacārī tiṣṭhed ahar abhuñjāno ‘bhyastam itaś ca rātriṃ japet sāvitrīm” iti (GDh 23.21) | yativanasthayor viśeṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
vanasthaś ca yatiś caiva sūryenābhyuditau yadi |
brahmakūrcāśinau bhūtvā japetāṃ praṇavaṃ tv ahaḥ || iti | (VaDh 28.41)
daṇḍakamaṇḍalvādināśe prāyaścittam āha paiṭhīnasiḥ: “naṣṭe daṇḍakāṣṭhe bhakṣaṃ datvā brāhmaṇāya tadahr upavaset | anyaṃ prayacched guruḥ | kamaṇḍalāv apy evam eva | naṣṭāyāṃ mekhalāyāṃ trivṛtam ācaret” iti | saṃdhyāgnikāryalope saṃvartaḥ |
saṃdhyām āpadi nopāste agnikāryaṃ yathāvidhi |
sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japet snātvā samāhitaḥ || iti | (SaṃSm 2.27)
svaśakṛn nirīkṣaṇe prāyaścittam āha yamaḥ |
[149]        praty ādityaṃ na meheta na paśyed ātmanaḥ śakṛt |
dṛṣṭvā sūryaṃ nirīkṣeta gām agniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tathā || iti | (YaSm 5.13)
bhojanakāle ‘śucitve vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
yadā bhojanakāle tu aśucir bhavati dvijaḥ |
bhūmau nikṣipya taṃ grāmsṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati ||
bhakṣayitvā tu taṃ grāsam ahorātreṇa śudhyati |
aśitvā sarvam evānnaṃ trirātreṇ viśudhyati || iti | (VṛŚāSm 16–17)
śrāddhe nimantritasya kālātikrame yama āha |
ketanaṃ kārayitvā tu yo ‘tipātayate dvijaḥ |
brahmahatyām avāpnoti śūdrayonau prajāyate ||
etasminn enasi prāpte brāhmaṇo niyatavrataḥ |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ cīrtvā tataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (YaSm 5.14–15)
kṣatriyādyupasaṃgrahaṇe hārīta āha: “kṣatriyābhivādane ‘horātram upavaset | vaiśyasyāpi | śūdrasyābhivādane trirātram upavāsaḥ | tathā śayyārūḍha-[150]-pādukopāndāropita-pādocchiṣṭāndhakārasthaśrāddhakṛjjapadevatāpūjābhiratābhivādane trirātram upavāsaḥ syāt | anyatra nimantritenānyatra bhojane ‘pi trirātram upavāsaḥ” iti (HārSm 21.7–12) ||
abhiśaṃsanaprāyaścittam
mithyābhiśaṃsane prāyaścittam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
mahāpāpopapāpābhyāṃ yo ‘bhiśaṃsen mṛṣā param |
abbhakṣo māsam āsīta sa jāpī niyatendriyaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.285)
japaś ca śuddhavatīnāṃ kāryaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhmaṇam anṛtenābhiśaṃsya patanīyenopapatanīyena vā māsam abbhakṣaḥ śuddhavatīr āvartayet | aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet” (VaDh 23.39) [151] iti | hārīto ‘pi |
anṛtābhiśaṃsanākrośe gurūṇāṃ paiśuneṣu ca |
ekaviṃśatirātraṃ tu pibed brāhmīṃ suvarcalām || iti | (HārSm 21.3)
śaṅkhalikhitau: “nāstikavṛttiḥ kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī brāhmaṇavṛttighno mithyābhiśṃsana ity ete pañcasaṃvatsarān brāhmaṇagṛhe bhaikṣyaṃ careyuḥ | saṃvatsaraṃ dhautaṃ bhaikṣyam aśnīyuḥ | ṣaṇmāsaṃ vā gā anugaccheyuḥ” iti | eteṣāṃ vacanānāṃ yathāyogaṃ vyavasthā draṣṭavyā ||
abhiśastaprāyaścittam
abhiśastaprāyaścittam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
abhiśasto mṛṣā kṛcchraṃ cared āgneyam eva vā |
nirvapet tu puroṣāśaṃ vāyavyaṃ paśum eva vā || (YDh 3.287)
[152] iti | yat tu vasiṣṭhavacanam: “etenaivābhiśasto vyākhyātaḥ” (VaDh 23.37) iti, tat tadaivākṛtaprāyaścittasya draṣṭavyam | yat tu paiṭhīnasinoktam: “anṛtenābhiśastaḥ kṛcchraṃ caret | māsaṃ pātakeṣu | mahāpātakeṣu dvimāsam” iti, tad api vasiṣṭhena samānaviṣayam |
tad evaṃ viṣṇunānukrāntānām atipātakādīnāṃ prakīrṇakāntānāṃ daśividhānāṃ pāpānāṃ sāmānyato viśeṣataś ca prāyaścittāni pratipāditāni ||
atha rahasyaprāyaścittāny abhidhīyante
yat pāpaṃ kartṛvyatiriktenānyena kenacid api na jñātaṃ tad rahasyam | tasya prāyaścittam api rahasy eva kartavyam | tathā ca hārītaḥ: [153] “atha brāhmaṇasya śrutadharmaśāstrasya rahasyam anukramiṣyāmaḥ | rahasye rahasyaṃ prakāśe prakāśam” iti (HārSm 30.1–2) | yamaḥ: “rahasye rahasyaṃ prakāśe prakāśam | rahasyakṛte rahasyam eva kartavyam | prakāśakṛte prakāśam eva kartavyam” iti | rahasyatvād nāsti pariṣadanumatyapekṣā | tad āhatur bṛhaspati-yājñavalkyau |
vikhyātadoṣaḥ kurvīta parṣado ‘numataṃ vratam |
anabhikhyātadoṣas tu rahasyavratam ācaret || (YDh 3.301)
[154] iti | na ca – vināpariṣadaṃ vratajñānābhāva – iti śaṅkanīyam, śāstrajñasya tadvijñānasaṃbhavāt | itareṇāpi buddhimatā vidvadgoṣṭhyāṃ kenacid vyājenāvagantuṃ śakyatvāt | yadi tadabhijñānāya pariṣado ‘gre pāpaṃ prakaṭīkuryāt tadā prāyaścittaṃ bahulaṃ bhavati | dvividhā hy aśuddhiḥ pāpena janyate | aihikyāmuṣmikī ca | tayor aihikī śiṣṭaiḥ saha vyavahāraṃ vārayati | āmuṣmikī tu narakaṃ prāpayati | tatra prakaṭapāpeṣu dvividhāśuddhisadbhāvāt tannivṛttaye prāyaścittabāhulyam apekṣitam | rahasye tv aikikāśuddhyabhāvād āmuṣmikamātrāśuddhiḥ svalpenāpi japahomādinā nivartate | ata eva manuḥ prājāpatyādivratānāṃ japādīnāṃ ca vyavasthām āha |
etair dvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratair āviṣkṛtainasaḥ |
anāviṣkṛtapāpāṃs tu mantrair homaiś ca śodhayet || iti | (MDh 11.226)
tatra rahasyānāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sa evāha |
vedābhyāso ‘nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā |
nāśayanty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api ||
[155]        yathaidhas tejasā vahniḥ prāptaṃ nirdahati kṣaṇāt |
tathājñānakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kṛtsnaṃ dahati vedavit ||
savyāhṛtipraṇavakāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api bhrūṇahaṇaṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ || iti | (MDh 11.245–48)
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yady akāryaśataṃ sāgraṃ kṛtaṃ vedaś ca dhāryate |
sarvaṃ tat tasya vedāgnir dahaty agnir ivendhanam || iti | (VaDh 27.1)
caturviṃśatimate |
kṛtaiś ca kriyamāṇaiś ca kariṣyadbhir na saṃśayaḥ |
brahma pratiṣṭhety etāvad anuvākau japed dvijaḥ ||
[156] iti | yamaḥ |
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |
gāyatrīṃ sa japen nityaṃ mahāpātakanāśinīm ||
virājṃ dviguṇaṃ japtvā tadahnaiva viśudhyati |
vāmadevyaṃ trir āvartya tadahnaiva viśudhyati ||
pauruṣaṃ sūktam āvartya mucyate sarvakilbiṣāt |
vṛṣabhaṃ śataśo japtvā tadahnaiva viśudhyati ||
vedam ekaguṇaṃ kṛtvā tadahnaiva viśudhyati |
rudraikādaśakaṃ japtvā tadahnaiva viśudhyati || iti |
viṣṇuḥ:
athātaḥ sarvavedapavitrāṇi bhavanti | yeṣāṃ japaiś ca homaiś ca dvijātayaḥ pāpebhyaḥ pūyante | [157] aghamarṣaṇam | devakṛtam | śuddhavatyaḥ | taratsamandīyam | kūṣmāṇḍyaḥ | pāvamānyaḥ | durgāḥ sāvitri | abhiṣaṅgāḥ | padastomāḥ | sāmāni | vyāhṛtayo bhāruṇḍāni | candrasāma | puruṣavrate sāmanī | abliṅgam | bārhaspatyam | gosūktam | vāksūktam | aśvasūktam | madhvarcaṃ sāmanī cendraśuddhe | śatarudrīyam | atharvaśiraḥ | trisuparṇam | mahāvratam | nārāyaṇīyam | puruṣasūktaṃ ca |
trīṇy ājyadohāni rathantaraṃ ca
agner vrataṃ vāmadevyaṃ bṛhac ca |
[158]        etāni japtāni punanti jantūn
jātismaratvaṃ labhate yadīcchet || (ViSm 56.1–27)
[159] iti | caturviṃśatimate |
pāvamānīs tathā kautsaṃ pauruṣaṃ sūktam eva ca |
japtvā pāpaiḥ pramucyeta pavitraṃ madhucchandasam ||
maṇḍalabrāhmaṇaṃ rudraṃ śukriyo mokṣakas tathā |
vāmadevyaṃ bṛhatsāma japtvā pāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
yajñāyajñīyam ādityaṃ jyeṣṭhamāsa ca rājanam |
pārucchepaṃ ca sāmāni japtvā mucyet kilbiṣāt ||
atharvaśirasaṃ caiva pauruṣaṃ sūktam eva ca |
nīlarudraṃ tathaivaindraṃ japtvā pāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
ātharvaṇāś ca ye kecit mantrāḥ kāmavivarjitāḥ |
te sarve pāpahantāro yājñavalkyavaco yathā ||
siṃhe me manyur ity etad anuvākaṃ japed dvijaḥ |
japtvā pāpaiḥ pramucyeta baudhāyanavaco yathā ||
ṛgvedam abhyased yas tu yajuḥśākhām athāpi vā |
sāmāni sarahasyāni atharvāṅgirasas tathā ||
brāhmaṇāni ca kalpāṃś ca ṣaḍaṅgāni tathaiva ca |
[160]        ākhyānāni tathānyāni japtvā pāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
itihāsapurāṇāni devatāstavanāni ca |
japtvā pāpaiḥ pramucyeta dharmasthānais tathā paraiḥ || iti |
baudhāyanaḥ |
vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍācaret |
yad upasthakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ padbhyāṃ vā yat kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
bāhubhyāṃ manasā vācā śrotratvakghrāṇacakṣuṣā |
sāyaṃsaṃdhyām upasthāya tena tasmāt pramucyate || iti | (BDh 2.4.7.18)
manuḥ |
mahāpātakinaś caiva śeṣāś cākāryakāriṇaḥ |
tapasaiva sutaptena mucyante kilbiṣāt tataḥ ||
yat kiṃcid enaḥ kurvanti manovākkarmabhir janāḥ |
tat sarvaṃ nirṇudanty āśu tapasaiva tapodhanāḥ || (MDh 11.239–41)
[161] iti | kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
japas tapas tīrthasevā devabrāhmaṇapūjanam |
grahaṇādiṣu kāleṣu mahāpātakaśodhanam ||
puṇyakṣetrābhigamanaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
devatābhyarcanaṃ nṝṇām aśeṣāghavināśanam ||
amāvāsyāṃ tithiṃ prāpya yaḥ samārādhayed bhavam |
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā ca sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ mahādevīṃ tathā kṛṣṇacaturdaśīm |
saṃpūjya brāhmaṇamukhe sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
trayodaśyāṃ tathā rātrau sopahāraṃ trilocanam |
iṣṭveśaṃ prathame yāme mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ ||
ekādaśyāṃ nirāhāraḥ samabhyarcya janārdanam |
dvādaśyāṃ śuklapakṣasya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
upoṣitaś caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe samāhitaḥ |
yamāya dharmarājāya mṛtyave cāntakāya ca ||
vaivasvatāya kālāya sarvabhūtakṣayāya ca |
pratyekaṃ tilasaṃuktān dadyāt saptodakāñjalīn ||
[162]        snātvā nadyāṃ tu pūrvāhṇe mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.33.97–108)
paiṭḥīnasiḥ |
sarvapāpaprasakto ‘pi dhyāyan nimiṣam acyutam |
punas tapasvī bhavati paṅktipāvanapāvanaḥ || iti |
caturviṃśatimate |
mṛgāreṣṭiḥ pavitreṣṭis trihaviḥ pāvamāny api |
iṣṭayaḥ pāpanāśinyo vaiśvānaryā samanvitāḥ || iti |
kūrmapurāṇe |
asmin kaliyuge ghore lokāḥ pāpānuvartinaḥ |
bhaviṣyanti mahābāho varṇāśramavivarjitāḥ ||
nānyat paśyāmi jantūnāṃ muktvā vārāṇasīṃ purīm |
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kalau yuge || iti | (KūPu 1.1.28.9–10)
iti sādhāraṇaprāyaścittāni |
atha pratipadoktāni prāyaścittāni
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
[163]        trirātropoṣito japtvā brahmahā tv aghamarṣaṇam |
antarjale viśudhyeta gāṃ datvā ca payasvinīm || iti | (YDh 3.302)
śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “brahmahā trirātropoṣito ‘ntarjale ‘ghamarṣaṇaṃ trir āvartayet” iti | etac ca kāmakāraviṣayam | akāmakāraviṣaye tu yamaḥ: “evaṃ brahmahatyāsurāpānasuvarṇasteya-gurutalpeṣu prāṇāyāmaiḥ śrānto ‘ghamarṣaṇaṃ japet” iti | baudhāyanaḥ |
grāmāt prācīm udīcīṃ vā diśam upaniṣkramya snātaḥ śucir udakānte sthaṇḍilam upalipya sakṛt klinnavāsāḥ sakṛt pūtena pāṇinā ādityābhimukho ‘ghamarṣaṇasvādhyāyam adhīyīta | prātaḥ śataṃ mādhyāhne śatam aparāhṇe śatam aparimitaṃ vā | uditeṣu nakṣatreṣu prasṛtiyāvakaṃ prāśnīyāt | jñānakṛtebhyo ‘jñānakṛtebhyaś copapātakebhyaḥ saptarātrāt pramucyate | [164] dvādaśarātrād bhrūṇahananaṃ gurutalpagamanaṃ suvarṇastainyaṃ surāpānam iti ca varjayitvā | ekaviṃśatirātrāt tāny apy atitarati | iti | (BDh 3.5.2–7)
manur api |
araṇye vā trir abhyasya prayato vedasaṃhitām |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ parākaiḥ śodhitas tribhiḥ || iti | (MDh 11.258)
bṛhadviṣṇur api: “brahmahatyāṃ kṛtvā prācīm udīcīṃ vā diśam upaprabhūtenendhanenāgniṃ prajvālyāghamarṣaṇenāṣṭasahasram ājyāhutīr juhuyāt tad etasmāt pūto bhavati” iti | etāni kāmākāmakārādibhedena vyavasthāpanīyāni | surāpāne yama āha: [165] “surāpaḥ kaṇṭhamātram udakam avatīrya sutasomāt prabhṛtim ādāyoṅkāreṇābhimantrya pibet | tato ‘psu nimagno mānastokīyaṃ japet” iti | yājñavalkyaḥ |
trirātropoṣito hutvā kūṣmāṇḍībhir ghṛtaṃ śuciḥ | iti | (YDh 3.304)
manuḥ |
kautsaṃ japtvāpa ity etad vāsiṣṭhaṃ ca pratīty ṛcam |
māhitraṃ śuddhavatyaś ca surāpo ‘pi viśudhyati ||
mantraiḥ śākalahomīyair abdaṃ hutvā ghṛtaṃ dvijaḥ |
sugurv apy apahanty eno japtvā vā nama ity ṛcam ||
mahāpātakasaṃyukto ‘nugacched gāḥ samāhitaḥ |
abhyasyābdaṃ pāvamānīr bhaikṣāhāro viśudhyati || (MDh 11.249, 256–57)
[166] iti | vasiṣṭhaḥ |
surāṃ pītvāpsu nirmajya triḥ paṭhed aghamarṣaṇam |
yathāśvamedhāvabhṛthas tādṛśaṃ manur abravīt || iti | 9VaDh 26.8)
atra jñānājñānābhyāsānabhyāsair vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | suvarṇasteye śātātapaḥ |
madyaṃ pītvā gurudārāṃś ca gatvā
steyaṃ kṛtvā brahmahatyāṃ ca kṛtvā |
bhasmacchanno bhasmaśayyāṃ śayāno
rudrādhyāyī mucyate sarvapāpaiḥ || iti | (ŚāSm 1.71)
yamaḥ: “brāhmaṇaḥ suvarṇasteyaṃ kṛtvā hiraṇyaṃ śālāyāṃ prakṣipyāpsu snāto grīvāmātram udakaṃ hiraṇyavarṇābhiś catasṛbhir ātmānam abhyukṣya trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati” [167] iti | bṛhaspatiḥ |
sakṛj japtvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpam eva vā |
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ vā kṣaṇād bhavati nirmalaḥ || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ svarṇahārī tu rudrajāpī jale sthitaḥ | iti | (YDh 3.304)
japaś caikādaśakṛtvā āryaḥ | tad āha atriḥ |
ekādaśa guṇān vāpi rudrān āvartya dharmavit |
mahāpāpair api spṛṣṭo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
gurutalpe yājñavalkyaḥ |
sahasraśīrṣājāpi ca mucyate gurutalpagaḥ |
gaur deyā karmaṇo ‘syānte pṛthag ebhiḥ payasvinī || iti | (YDh 3.305)
yamo ‘pi: “atha gurutalpagamanaṃ kṛtvāghamarṣaṇam antarjale trir āvartya tad etasmāt pāpāt pūto bhavati” [168] iti | manur api |
haviṣyāntīyam abhyasya na tamaṃ ha itīti ca |
japtvā ca pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.251)
saṃsargī yena saha saṃsargaṃ karoti sa tadīyam eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt, “sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāt” iti pūrvam evoktatvāt ||
athopapātakarahasyaprāyaścittāni
tatra yamaḥ |
daśapraṇavasaṃyuktaiḥ prāṇāyāmaiś catuḥśataiḥ |
mucyate brahmahatyāyāḥ kiṃ punaḥ śeṣapātakaiḥ || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kāryaṃ sarvapāpāpanuttaye |
upapātakajātānām anādiṣṭasya caiva hi || (YDh 3.306)
[169] iti | manur api |
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣann apanodanam |
avety ṛcaṃ japed abdaṃ yat kiṃcedam itīti ca || iti | (MDh 11.252)
atra sakṛdabhyāsādibhedena vyavasthā draṣṭavyā ||
atha vratalakṣaṇāni
tatra sarvavratasya sādhāraṇāny aṅgāni yājñavalkya āha |
brahmacaryaṃ dayā kṣāntir dānaṃ satyam akalkatā |
ahiṃsāsteyamādhurye damaś ceti yamāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
snānamaunopavādejyāsvādhyāyopasthanigrahāḥ |
niyamā guruśuśrūṣāśaucākrodhāpramāditāḥ || iti | (YDh 3.313–14)
atra brahmacaryaṃ sarvendriyanigrahaḥ | upasthanigraho liṅganirodhaḥ | tayor gobalīvardanyāyena punaruktiḥ | akalkatvam akuṭilatvam | [170] iti | manur api |
ahiṃsāṃ satyam akrodham ārjavaṃ ca samācaret |
trir ahni trir niṣāyāṃ ca savāsā jalam āviśet |
strīśūdrapatitāṃś caiva nābhibhāṣeta karhicit ||
sthānāsanābhyāṃ vihared aśakto ‘dhaḥ śayīta vā |
brahmacārī vratī ca syād gurudevadvijārcakaḥ ||
sāvitrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ |
sarveṣv eva vrateṣv evaṃ prāyaścittārtham ādṛtaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.222–25)
tatra prājāptayavratalakṣaṇam āha hārītaḥ |
tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryahaṃ prātas tryaham adhyād ayācitam |
paraṃ tryahaṃ vāyubhakṣaḥ eṣa kṛcchraḥ prajāpateḥ || iti |
manur api |
tryahaṃ prātas tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryaham adyād ayācitam |
tryahaṃ paraṃ tu nāśnīyāt prājāpatyaṃ caran dvijaḥ || (MDh 11.211)
[171] iti | asyaivādhikāribhedena prayogāntaram āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ahaḥ prātar ahar naktam ahar ekam ayācitam |
ahaḥ parākaṃ tatraikam evaṃ caturahau parau ||
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ manur dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca śiśukṛccham uvāca ha || iti | (VaDh 23.42–43)
etad ubhayam api prājāpatyakṛcchraṃ darśayati yājñavalkyaḥ |
ekabhaktena naktena tathaivāyācitena ca |
upavāsena caikena pādakṛcchraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
yathā kathaṃcit triguṇaḥ prājāpatyo ‘yam ucyate || (YDh 3.319–20)
[172] iti | ekabhaktena divaiva sakṛd bhojanena | naktena rātrau sakṛdbhojanena | ayācitena na vidyate yācitaṃ yasmin bhojane tad ayācitam tena | atra kālaviśeṣāpratīter divā rātrau vā sakṛdbhojanena | upavāsena caikena ekasmin dine ‘naśanena ca pādakṛcchro bhavati | ayam eva pādakṛcchraḥ svasthānavivṛddhyā vā daṇḍakalitavad āvṛttyā vā yathā kathaṃcit triguṇaḥ trir abhyastaḥ prājāpatya ity ucyate ity arthaḥ | ekabhaktādiṣu grāsasaṃkhyā āpastambena darśitā |
sāyaṃ dvāviṃśatir grāsāḥ prātaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ |
caturviṃśatir ayācyāḥ paraṃ niraśanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tatparimāṇam api tenaivoktam |
kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇaṃ tu yathā vāsyaṃ viśet sukham | iti |
caturviṃśatimate tu prakārāntareṇa saṃkhyā śrūyate |
prātas tu dvādaśa grāsāḥ sāyaṃ pañcadaśaiva tu |
ayācite ca dvāv aṣṭau paraṃ vai mārutāśanaḥ || iti |
atra śaktyapekṣayā vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | āpastambas tu caturaḥ pādakṛcchrān kṛtvā teṣāṃ varṇabhedena vyavasthām āha |
[173]        tryahaṃ niraśanaṃ pādaḥ pādaś cāyācitaṃ tryaham |
sāyaṃ tryahaṃ tathā pādaḥ pādaḥ prātas tathā tryaham ||
prātaḥ pādaṃ carec chūdraḥ sāyaṃ vaiśyasya dāpayet |
ayācitaṃ tu rājanye trirātraṃ bṛhmaṇe smṛtam || iti |
ardhakṛcchrapādonakṛcchrayor api svarūpaṃ sa evāha |
sāyaṃ prātar dinārdhaṃ syāt pādonaṃ naktavarjitam | iti |
ayam arthaḥ | ayācitopavāsatryahadvayānuṣṭhāne nārdhakṛcchro bhavati | nakatryahavyatiriktatryahatrayānuṣṭhānena pādonakṛcchro bhavati | ardhakṛcchraprakāro ‘pi tenaivoktaḥ |
sāyaṃ prātas tathaivoktaṃ dinadvayam ayācitam |
dinadvayaṃ tu nāśnīyāt kṛcchrārdhaṃ tad vidhīyate ||
[174] iti | atrāpi śaktyapekṣayā vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | yat tu japahomādibahvaṅgasahitaṃ prājāpatyakṛcchraṃ gautamenābhihitam
haviṣyān prātarāśān bhuktvā tisro rātrīr nāśnīyāt | athāparaṃ tryahaṃ naktaṃ bhuñjīta | athāparaṃ tryahaṃ na kaṃcana yāceta | athāparaṃ tryaham upavaset | tiṣṭhed ahani, rātrāv āsīta | kṣiprakāmaḥ | satyaṃ vadet | anāryair na saṃbhāṣeta | rauravayaudhājape nityaṃ prayuñjīta | anusavanam udakopasparśanam āpohiṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ pavitravatībhir mārjayīta | hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā ity aṣṭābhiḥ | athodakatarpaṇam | oṃ namo hamāya mohamāya saṃhamāya dhunvate tāpamāya punarvasave | namo namo [175] mauñjāyormyāya vasuvindāya sarvavindāya namo namaḥ pārāya supārāya mahāpārāya pāradāya pārayiṣṇave | namo namo rudrāya paśupataye mahate devāya tryambakāyaikacarāyādhipataye harāya śarvāyeśānāyogrāya vajriṇe ghṛṇine kapardine | namo namaḥ sūryāyādityāya | namo namo nīlagrīvāya śitikaṇṭhāya | namo namaḥ kṛṣṇāya piṅgalāya | namo namo jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya vṛddhāyendrāya harikeśāyordhvaretase | namo namaḥ satyāya pāvakāya pāvakavarṇāya kāmāya kāmarūpāya | namo namo dīptāya dīptarūpiṇe | namo namas tīkṣṇāya tīkṣṇarūpiṇe | namo namaḥ saumyāya supuruṣāya mahāpuruṣāya madhyamapuruṣāya uttamapuruṣāya brahmacāriṇe | [176] namo namaś candralalāṭāya kṛttivāsase pinākahastāya namo nama iti | etad evādityopasthānam | etā evājyāhutayaḥ | dvādaśarātrasyānte caruṃ śrapayitvaitābhyo devatābhyo juhuyāt | agnaye svāhā somāya svāhā agnīṣomābhyām indrāgnibhyām indrāya viśvebhyo devebhyo brahmaṇe prajāpataye agnaye sviṣṭakṛte iti | ante brāhmaṇabhojanam || iti | (GDh 27.2–29)
haviṣyānityādy upavased ityantena prājāpatyasya svarūpam uktam | tiṣṭhed ityādinā tasyaivetikartavyatocyate | ahani tiṣṭhed ityantena bhojanādyaviruddhakāleṣu ahar utthita eva syāt | rātrāv āsītetyantena nidrāyā āvaśyakatvāt tām āsīna eva sevetety uktaṃ bhavati | kṣiprakāmo ‘pi bhavet | śīghraṃ śuddhaḥ syām iti saṃjātakāmo ‘pi bhaved ity arthaḥ | atha vā śīghraṃ śuddhikāmo ‘hani tiṣṭhed rātrāv āsītety arthaḥ |
[177]        evaṃ satyaṃ vaded ityādyaṅgakalāpe kṣiprakāma iti adhikāriviśeṣaṇam anuṣañjanīyam | anena yaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ śuddho bhavāmīti manyate tasya nāyaṃ niyama iti avagamyate | rauravayaudhājape sāmanī | namo hamāyetyādayas trayodaś mantrās tarpaṇasūryopasthānājyahomeṣu draṣṭavyāḥ | atha vā saṃpradānavibhaktyantāḥ ṣaṭpañcāśan mantrāḥ | tarpaṇam anusavanaṃ kartavyam, snānāṅgatvāt tarpaṇasya | tathā ca bhṛguḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikṃ kāmyaṃ tridhaṃ snānam ucyate |
tarpaṇaṃ trividhasyāṅgam eṣa eva vidhiḥ sadā || iti |
ādityopasthānājyahomau tu pratidinaṃ sakṛt sakṛt kāryau, “athodakatarpaṇam ādityopasthānam ājyahomaś ca” it vaktavye, “etad evāditypasthānam | etā evājyāhutayaḥ” iti pṛthagyogakaraṇāt | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam | etad hārītādyukta-[178]-japahomādyaṅgarahitaprājāpatyadvayasthāne veditavyam, bahuprayāsasādhyatvenātigurutvāt | atha vā yeṣāṃ śūdrādīnāṃ mantrādhikāro nāsti teṣāṃ japahomādirahitaṃ prājāpatyaṃ veditavyam | ata evoktam aṅgirasā |
tasmāc chūdraṃ samāsādya sadā dharmapathe sthitam |
prāyaścittaṃ pradātavyaṃ japahomavivarjitam || iti |
yeṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ japahomādiṣv adhikāras teṣāṃ gautamoktaṃ veditavyam ||
athātikṛcchraḥ
tasya lakṣaṇam uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt tryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat |
tryahaṃ copavasen nityam atikṛcchraṃ caran dvijaḥ || iti |
ekabhaktanaktāyācitadivaseṣu navasv ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyāt tryahaṃ copavaset | evaṃ cātikṛcchro bhavatīty arthaḥ | yat tu yājñavalkyenoktam,
ayam evātikṛcchraḥ syāt pāṇipūrānnabhojanaḥ | (YDh 3.320)
[179] iti – ayam eva prājāpatyakṛcchraḥ ekabhakanaktāyācitadivaseṣu navasu pāṇipūrānnabhojanayukto ‘tikṛcchro bhavatīty arthaḥ – tad etad aśaktaviṣayam | pāṇipūrānnasya grāsaparimitād annād adhikaparimāṇatvāt ||
atha kṛcchrātikṛcchaḥ
tasya lakṣaṇaṃ yājñavalkyenoktam |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ payasā divasān ekaviṃśatim | iti | (YDh 3.321)
viṣṇur api: “kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ payasā divasān ekaviṃśatikṣapaṇam” iti (ViDh 46.13) | yat tu gautamaḥ: “abbhakṣas tṛtīyaḥ sa kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ” (GDh 27.32) iti, –ekabhaktanaktāyācitadivaseṣu yo bhojanakālaḥ tasminn eva kāle kevalam udakena vartanaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchro bhavatīti – tad etac chaktaviṣayam | atikṛcchrakṛcchrātikṛcchayor api prājāpatyakṛcchroktetikartavyatā draṣṭavyā, [180] “etenaiva kṛcchrātikṛcchrau vyākhyātau” (GDh 27.31) iti gautamasmaraṇāt | parākalakṣaṇam āha yamaḥ |
dvādaśāhaṃ nirāhāro yathātmā saṃśitavrataḥ |
kṛcchraḥ prākamāmaiṣa sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ || iti |
parṇakṛcchralakṣaṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
parṇōdumbarajājīvabilvapatrakośodakaiḥ |
pratyekaṃ paryahaṃ pītaiḥ parṇakṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ || (YDh 3.317)
[181] iti | palāśodumbarāravindabilvaparṇānām ekaikena kvathitam udakaṃ pratyahaṃ pibet | kuśodakaṃ caikasminn ahanīti | parṇakṛcchrasya lakṣaṇāntaram āha yamaḥ |
etāny eva samastāni trirātropoṣitaḥ śuciḥ |
kvāthayitvā pibed adbhiḥ parṇakṛcchro vidhīyate || iti |
yadā palāśādipatrāṇy ekīkṛtyāmbhasā kvāthiyitvā trirātropavāsānte kvathitaṃ tat pibed tadā parṇakṛcchro bhavatīty arthaḥ | phalakṛcchrādīnāṃ svarūpam āha mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
phalair māsena kathitaḥ phalakṛcchro manīṣibhiḥ |
śrīkṛcchraḥ śrīphalaiḥ proktaḥ padākṣarair aparas tathā ||
māsenāmalakair evaṃ śrīkṛcchram aparaṃ smṛtam |
patrair mataḥ patrakṛcchraḥ puṣpair tatkṛcchra ucyate ||
mūlakṛcchraḥ smṛto mūlais toyakṛccho jalena tu || iti |
yadā bilvādiphalāny ambhasā kvāthayitvā māsam ekaṃ tad ambhaḥ pibati tadā phalakṛcchro bhavati | yadā bilvapadmākṣamūlānām anyatamasya kvāthaṃ māsaṃ pibet tadā patrapuṣpamūlakṛcchrāṇi bhavanti | [182] vāruṇastrīkṛcchrayor lakṣaṇam āha yamaḥ |
brahmacārī jitakrodho māse ‘py udakasaktukān |
pibec ca niyatāhāraḥ kṛcchraṃ vāruṇam ucyate ||
tryahaṃ pibet tu gomūtraṃ tryahaṃ vai gomayaṃ pibet |
tryahaṃ vai yāvakenaiva strīkṛcchraṃ hy etad ucyate || iti | (YaSm 5.31)
saumyakṛcchrasvarūpam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
piṇyākācāmatakrāmbysaktūnāṃ prativāsaram |
ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraḥ saumyo ‘yam ucyate || iti | (YDh 3.322)
ācām odananiḥsrāvaḥ | piṇyākādīnāṃ pañcānām ekaikaṃ pratidinam upabhujya ṣaṣṭhe ‘hany upavaset | eṣa saumyakṛcchraḥ | yat tu jābālena caturahavyāpī saumyakṛcchra ity uktam,
[183]        piṇyākaṃ saktavas takraṃ codakasaktavaḥ |
trirātram upavāsaś ca tulāpuruṣa ucyate || iti |
so ‘yam aṣṭadivasasādhyaḥ | yājñavalkyas tu pañcadaśadivasasādhyam āha |
eṣāṃ trirātram abhyāsād ekaikasya yathākramam |
tulāpuruṣa ity eva jñeyaḥ pañcadaśāhikaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.323)
eṣāṃ prakṛtānāṃ piṇyākādīnāṃ pañcadravyāṇām | yamas tv ekaviṃśatidivasasādhyam āha |
ācāmam atha piṇyākaṃ takraṃ codakasaktukān |
[184]        tryahaṃ tryahaṃ prabhuñjāno vāyubhakṣas tryahadvayam ||
ekaviṃśatirātras tu tulāpuruṣa ucyate ||
iti, tad etat trayaṃ pāpatāratamyaviṣayatayā vyavasthāpanīyam | atretikartavyatām āha hārītaḥ |
athātas triṇayanoktasya tulāpuruṣasya kalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
ayājyayājanaṃ kṛtvā prāśya mūtrapurīṣayoḥ |
apratigrāhyam ādāya yājayitvā vininditān ||
vināyakopasṛṣṭasya mahāvyādhihatasya ca |
etat kṛcchraṃ tulāpūrṣaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam ||
svargadvāram idaṃ puṇyaṃ mahādevena nirmitam |
ācāmam atha piṇyākaṃ takraṃ codakasaktukān ||
tryahaṃ tryahaṃ prayuñjāno vāyubhakṣas tryahadvayam |
vācā kāyena manasā kṛtāni vividhāni ca ||
[185]                ācāmas tāni nirhanti navaśrāddhaṃ ca sūtakam |
taikṣṇyaṃ ca dāruṇaṃ caitat keśakīṭahataṃ ca yat ||
ākhor mūtrapurīṣaṃ ca brahmahaspṛṣṭam eva ca |
piṇyākas tāni nirhanti asthi bhittvā tu yat sthitam ||
āhāreṣu ca ye doṣāḥ snehadoṣāś ca ye kvacit |
kharoṣṭramukhasaṃspṛṣṭam ugrasaṃspṛṣṭam eva ca ||
takreṇa tāni nirhanti yac cānyat śvāvalokitam |
kānakāśvatilāgāvo bhūmir ācchādanaṃ striyaḥ ||
sarvaṃ punāti dharmātmā gūḍhaṃ codakasaktubhiḥ ||
brahmahatyāṃ bhrūṇahatyāṃ svarṇasteyaṃ surāpānaṃ gurudārābhigamanaṃ kanyādūṣaṇaṃ klībābhigamanam udakyābhigamanaṃ vāyubhakṣaḥ punāti |
[186]                gandhośīramayau kāryau puruṣau palasaṃmitau |
nadīmṛttikayā vāpi puruṣau dvau ca kārayet ||
mahāpaśur yena śareṇa viddho
yad vā śastram ubhayato yac ca lakṣyam |
tenāyasīṃ sukṛtāṃ kārayitvā
prādesamātrām ubhayato jātaśikyām ||
sauvarṇīṃ rājatīṃ vāpi tulāṃ devīṃ tu kārayet |
tasyālābhe tulā kāryā yathā vāpy upapadyate ||
yaḥ puruṣaḥ piṅgalo babhrur halamusalacakrapāṇistamaha (?) piṅgalaṃ cakrahalamusalaśūlavajrapāṇim āvāhayāmi | svata ehi sūta ehi svayāma ehi nāga ehi kṛṣṇājina ehi | [187] svāgatam anu svāgatam | bhagavate tulāpuruṣāya mahādevāyedam āsanam idaṃ pādyam idam ācamanīyam idaṃ gandhamālyadhūpadīpanaivedyaṃ pratigṛhāṇa prasīda deva tubhyaṃ namaḥ | akruddhaḥ sumanā bhava | atha tulām abhimantrayet | ṛṣisatyaṃ brahmasatyaṃ devasatyaṃ antaḥsatyaṃ tena satyena satyavādini |
deśānāṃ saṃpadaṃ brūhi kaṃ deśaṃ vardhayiṣyasi |
bījānāṃ saṃpadaṃ brūhi kiṃ bījaṃ vardhayiṣyasi |
rājñāṃ ca saṃpadaṃ brūhi kaṃ nṛpaṃ vardhayiṣyati ||
asmākaṃ saṃpadaṃ brūhi kim asmākaṃ bhaviṣyati |
yadā tu gurum ātmānam asakṛt tulayā dhṛtam |
paraṃ caiva laghuṃ manyet tadā karma samārabhet ||
agnaye pṛthivyadhipataye svāhā | vāyave ‘ntarikṣādhipataye svāhā | [188] sūryāya divo’dhipataye svāhā | somāya nakṣatrādhipataye svāhā | vāsukaye nāgādhipataye svāhā | kuberāya yakṣādhipataye svāhā | indrāya devādhipataye svāhā | brahmaṇe trailokyādhipataye svāhā | devyai bhagādhipataye svāhā | sālakaṭaṅkaṭāya svāhā | kūṣmāṇḍarājaputrāya svāhā | vināyakāya svāhā | mahāvināyakāya svāhā | mahāmahāvināyakāya svāhā | vakratuṇḍāya gāṇānāṃ pataye svāhā | dharmāya svāhā | adharmāya svāhā | tulāyai svāhā | [189] tulāpuruṣāya svāhā | devyai punarāgamanāya svāhā | sakṛc caritivā śuciḥ putryaḥ karmaṇyo bhavati | dvitīyaṃ caritvā gāṇapatyaṃ prāpnoti | triś caritvā mahādevasyāvasathaṃ prāpnoti | āṣāḍhyāṃ kārttikyāṃ vā phālgunyāṃ vā puṇye nakṣatre eṣā vihito dharmaḥ || iti |
(HārSm 28.61–115)
atha kṛcchrāḥ
vāruṇakṛcchraḥ
tatra vāruṇakṛcchram āha yamaḥ |
brahmacārī jitakrodho māse ‘py udakasaktukān |
pibec ca niyatāhāraḥ kṛcchraṃ vāruṇam ucyate || iti |
athāghamarṣanakṛcchraḥ
aghamarṣaṇakṛcchram āha viṣṇuḥ: “atha kṛcchrāṇi bhavanti | tryahaṃ nāśnīyāt | [190] pratyahaṃ ca triṣavaṇaṃ snānam ācaret | magnas trir aghamarṣaṇaṃ japet | divā sthitas tiṣṭhet | rātrāv āsīta | karmaṇo ‘nte payasvinīṃ dadyāt | ity aghamarṣaṇam” iti (ViDh 46.1–9) | śaṅkhas tu prakārāntareṇāghamarṣaṇakṛcchram āha |
tryahaṃ triṣavaṇasnāyī maunī syād aghamarṣaṇam |
manasā triḥ paṭhed apsu na bhuñjīta dinatrayam ||
vīrāsanaṃ sadā tiṣṭhet gā ca dadyāt payasvinīm |
aghamarṣaṇam ity etad vrataṃ sarvāghasūdanam || iti | (ŚaṅSm 18.1–2)
atha yajñakṛcchraḥ
tatrāha aṅgirāḥ |
yuktas triṣavaṇasnāyī saṃyato maunam āsthitaḥ |
prātaḥsnānasmārambhaṃ kuryāj japyaṃ ca nityaśaḥ ||
[191]        sāvitrīṃ vyāhṛtīś caiva japed aṣṭasahasrakam |
oṃkāram āditaḥ kṛtvā rūpe rūpe tathāntataḥ ||
bhūmau vīrāsane yuktaḥ kuryāj japyaṃ susaṃyataḥ |
āsīnaś ca sthito vāpi pibed gavyaṃ payaḥ sakṛt ||
gavyasya payaso ‘lābhe gavyam eva bhaved dadhi |
dadhno ‘bhāve bhavet takraṃ takrābhāve tu yāvakam ||
eṣām anyatamaṃ yad yad upapadyeta tat pibet |
gomūtreṇa samāyuktaṃ yāvakaṃ copayojayet ||
ekāhena tu kṛcchro ‘yam uktas tv aṅgirasā svayam |
sarvapāpaharo divyo nāmnā yajña iti smṛtaḥ||
etat pātakayuktānāṃ tathā cāpy upapātakaiḥ |
mahadbhiś cāpi yuktānāṃ prāyaścittam idaṃ śubham || iti |
atha devakṛcchraḥ
devakṛtaṃ kṛcchraṃ darśayati yamaḥ |
yavāgūṃ yāvakaṃ śākaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tathā |
tryahaṃ tryahaṃ tu prāśnīyād vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
[192]        kṛcchraṃ devakṛtaṃ nāma sarvakalmaṣanāśanam |
marudbhir vasubhī rudrair ādityaiś caritaṃ vratam ||
vratasyāsya prabhāvena virajaskā hi te ‘bhavan || iti |
atha yāvakakṛcchraḥ
yāvakakṛcchram āha śaṅkhaḥ |
gopurīṣayavābhyāso māsam ekaṃ samāhitaḥ |
vrataṃ tu yāvakaṃ kuryāt sarvapāpanuttaye || iti | (ŚaṅSm 18.10)
devalo ‘pi: “yavānām apsu sādhitānāṃ saptarātraṃ pakṣaṃ māsaṃ vā pāśanaṃ yāvakaḥ | etena yāvakapāyasaudakāni vyākhyātāni | yāvakapāyasaudakāni prājāpatyavidhānena vyākhyātāni bhavanti || iti |
atha prasṛtiyāvakakṛcchraḥ
atha prasṛtiyāvakaḥ | tatra hārītaḥ:
ya ātmakṛtaiḥ karmabhir gurum ātmānaṃ paśyet ātmārthaṃ prasṛtiyāvakaṃ śrapayet | tato ‘gnau [193] juhuyāt | tad eva balikarma | śṛtaṃ cābhimantrayet |
yavo ‘si dhānyarājo vā vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitram ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtam ||
ghṛtaṃ yavā madhu yavā āpohiṣṭhāmṛtaṃ yavāḥ |
sarvaṃ punantu me pāpaṃ yan mayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam ||
vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā durvicintitam |
alakṣmīṃ kālakarṇīṃ ca sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
mātāpitror aśuśrūṣāṃ yauvane kāritāṃ tathā |
śvasūkarāvalīḍhaṃ ca ucchiṣṭopahataṃ ca yat ||
suvarṇasteyaṃ vrātyatvaṃ bālatvād ātmanas tathā |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ sarvaṃ punīta me yavāḥ ||
śrapyamāṇe rakṣāṃ kuryāt | namo rudrāya bhūtādhpataye dyauḥ sāvitrī mānasoketi pātre trir niṣicya “ye devā manojātā manoyujaḥ sudakṣā dakṣapitaras te naḥ pāntu te no ‘vantu tebhyo namas tebhyaḥ [194] svāhā” ity ātmani juhuyāt | trirātram evārthārthī | pāpakṛt ṣaḍrātraṃ pītvā pūto bhavati | saptarātraṃ mahāpātakī | dvādaśarātraṃ pītvā sarvaṃ puruṣakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ nirdahati | niḥsṛtānāṃ yavānām ekaviṃśatirātraṃ pītvā gaṇān paśyati | gaṇādhipatiṃ paśyati | vidyāṃ paśyati | vidyādhipatiṃ paśyati | yo ‘śnīyād yāvakaṃ pakvaṃ gomūtre sakṛd dadhikṣīrasarpiḥ pramucyate so ‘haṃsaḥ kṣaṇād ity āha bhagavān maitrāvaruṇiḥ | iti | (HārSm 28.32–48)
itthaṃ prājāpatyādivratāni nirūpitāni |
atha vratagrahaṇaprakāraḥ
tatra viṣṇuḥ |
sarvapāpeṣu sarveṣāṃ vratānāṃ vidhipūrvakam |
grahaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi prāyaścitte cikīrṣite ||
[195]        dinānte nakharomādīn pravāpya snānam ācaret |
bhasmagomayamṛdvāripañcagavyādikalpitaiḥ ||
malāpakarṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ bāhyaśaucopasiddhaye |
dantadhāvanapūrveṇa pañcagavyena saṃyutam ||
vrataṃ niśāmukhe grāhyaṃ bahistārakadarśane |
ācamyātaḥ paraṃ maunī dyāyan duṣkṛtam ātmanaḥ ||
manaḥsaṃtāpanaṃ tīvram udvahec chokam antataḥ | iti | (ViDh 3.21–25)
bahiḥ grāmān niṣkramyety arthaḥ | pūrvedyur niśāmukhe vrataṃ pariṣaduddiṣṭaprakāreṇa saṃkalpya paredyus triṣavaṇasnānaprātarhomādyaṅgasahitaṃ vratam anuṣṭhātum ārabhet | prātarhomādyaṅgāni ca jābālena darśitāni |
ārambhe sarvakṛcchrāṇāṃ samāptau ca viśeṣataḥ |
ājyenaiva hi śālāgnau juhuyād āhutīḥ pṛthak ||
śrāddhaṃ kuryād vratānte ca gohiraṇyādidakṣiṇām || iti |
yamo ‘pi |
paścāttāpo nirvṛttiś ca snānaṃ cāṅgatayoditam |
naimittikānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vratānāṃ vidhipūrvakam ||
[196]        gātrābhyaṅgaṃ śiro ‘bhyaṅgaṃ tāmbūlam anulepanam |
vratastho varjayet sarvaṃ yac cānyad balarāgakṛt || iti | (YaSm 6.14)
gṛhītasya vratasyāsamāpane pratyavāyam āha chāgaleyaḥ |
pūrvaṃ vrataṃ gṛhītvā tu nācaret kāmato hi yaḥ |
jīvan bhavati cāṇḍālo mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate || iti ||
atha pratyāmnāyāḥ
atha pūrvoktānāṃ vratānāṃ kenacin nimittenānuṣṭhānāśaktau yathāyogaṃ pratyāmnāyā ucyante | tatra prājāpatyasya pratyāmnāyāś caturviṃśatimate darśitāḥ |
kṛcchro devyayutaṃ caiva prāṇāyāmaśatadvayam |
tilahomasahasraṃ tu vedapārāyaṇaṃ tathā ||
viprā dvādaśa vā bhojyāḥ pāvakeṣṭis tathaiva ca |
atha vā pāvamāneṣṭiḥ samāny āhur manīṣiṇaḥ || iti |
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
[197]        kṛcchro ‘yutaṃ tu gāyatryā udavāsas tathaiva ca |
dhenupradānaṃ viprāya samam etac catuṣṭayam || iti |
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
prājāpatyakriyāśaktau dhenuṃ dadyād dvijottamaḥ |
dhenor abhāve dātavyaṃ mūlyaṃ tulyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
mūlyaṃ ca yathāśakti deyam | ata eva brahmapurāṇe |
gavām abhāve niṣkaṃ syāt tadardhaṃ pādam eva vā | iti |
yatra yāvatsaṅkhyayā prājāpatyāny āvartanīyāni bhavanti tatra tāvatsaṃkhyayā godānāny āvartanīyāni | tad api caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
janmaprabhṛti pāpāni bahūni vividhāni ca |
arvāk tu bhrūṇahatyāyāḥ ṣaḍabdaṃ kṛcchram ācaret ||
pratyāmnāye gavāṃ deyaṃ sāśīti dhaninā śatam |
tathāṣṭādaśalakṣāṇi gāyatryā vā japed budhaḥ || iti |
nanu – uktarītyā dvādaśavārṣikasya prājāpatyasya [198] pratyāmnāyatvena ṣaṭtriśallakṣasaṃkhyāko gāyatrījapaḥ prāpnoti | koṭisaṃkhyākas tu caturviṃśatimata eva smaryate |
gāyatryās tu japan koṭiṃ brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati |
lakṣāśītiṃ japed yas tu surāpānād vimucyate ||
punāti hemakartāraṃ gāyatrīlakṣasaptatiḥ |
gāyatryāḥ ṣaṣṭibhir lakṣair mucyate gurutalpagaḥ || iti |
tathā ca sati parasparavirodhaḥ syāt – iti cet |
evaṃ tarhy anayaivānupapattyā koṭijapo ‘pi na pratyāmnāyaḥ | kiṃ tu dvādaśavārṣikavratasamānaviṣayatayā vikalpyate iti kalpanīyam | cāndrāyaṇādīnāṃ pratyāmnāyās tatraiva darśitāḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ mṛgāreṣṭiḥ pavitreṣṭis tathaiva ca |
mitravindā paśuś caiva kṛcchraṃ māsatrayaṃ tathā ||
tilahomāyutaṃ caiva parākadvayam eva ca |
gāyatryā lakṣajapyaṃ ca samāny āha bṛhaspatiḥ ||
nityanaimittikānāṃ ca kāmyānāṃ caiva karmaṇām |
iṣṭīnāṃ paśubandhānām abhāve caravaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
parākataptakṛcchrāṇāṃ sthāne kṛcchratrayaṃ caret |
vratahomādikaṃ vāpi kalpayet pūrvakalpavat ||
[199] iti | yas tu tapasy asamarthaḥ dhānyasamṛddhaś ca sa kṛcchrādivratāni brāhmaṇabhojanena saṃpādayet | tad api tatraivoktam |
kṛcchre pañcātikṛcchre triguṇam ahar ahas triṃśad evām tṛtīye
catvāriṃśac ca tapte triguṇanaguṇitā viṃśatiḥ syāt parāke |
kṛcchre santāpanākhye bhavati ṣaḍadhikā viṃśatiḥ saiva hīnā
dvābhyāṃ cāndrāyaṇe syāt tapasi kṛśabalo bhojayed vipramukhyān || iti |
ahar ahar iti sarvatra saṃbadhyate | tṛtīyaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | yat tu prājāpatyapratyāmnāyatvena “viprā dvādaśa vā bhojyāḥ” iti pūrvam udāhṛtam, tan nirdhanaviṣayam, dhanikasya pratidinaṃ pañca pañceti dvādaśasu divaseṣu ṣaṣṭibrāhmaṇabhojanavidhānāt | ata eva smṛtyantare |
prājāpatyaṃ cared vipro yady aśaktaṅ kathaṃcana |
ahāni pañca viprāgryān bhojayet samyag īpsitān || iti |
sāntapanādīnāṃ godānapratyāmnāyas tatraiva caturviṃśatimate darśitaḥ |
[200]        prājāpatye tu gām ekāṃ dadyāt sāntapane dvayam |
parāke taptakṛcchre ca tisras tisras tu gās tathā ||
aṣṭau cāndrāyaṇe deyāḥ pratyāmnāyavidhau sadā |
yathāvibhavasāreṇa dānaṃ dadyād viśuddhaye || iti |
yat tu smṛtyantare cāndrāyaṇasya godānatrayam abhihitam,
prājāpatye tu gām ekām atikṛcchre dvayaṃ smṛtam |
cāndrāyaṇe parāke ca tisras tā dakṣīnās tathā ||
iti, tan nirdhanaviṣayam | yatoktagodānādāv aśakto gobhyas tṛṇaṃ dadyāt | tathāha kaṇvaḥ |
ekam adhyayanaṃ kuryāt prājāpatyam athāpi vā |
dadyād vā daśasāhastraṃ gavāṃ muṣṭiṃ vicankṣaṇaḥ || iti |
adhyayanajapādīnāṃ puruṣaviśeṣeṇa vyavasthā caturviṃśatimate darśitā |
dharmaniṣṭhās taponiṣṭhāḥ kadācit pāpam āgatāḥ |
japahomādikaṃ tebhyo viśeṣeṇa vidhīyate ||
[201]         nāmadhārakaviprā ye mūrkhā dharmavivarjitāḥ |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādīni tebhyo dadyād viśeṣataḥ ||
dhaninā dakṣiṇā deyā prayatnavihitā tu yā |
evaṃ naraviśeṣeṇa prāyaścittāni dāpayet || iti |
anayaiva diśā smṛtyantare prasiddhās tīrthayātrādipratyāmnāyā yathāyogam unneyāḥ | ata eva devalaḥ |
abhigamya ca tīrthāni puṇyāny āyatanāni ca |
naraḥ pāpāt pramucyeta brāhmaṇānāṃ tapasvinām ||
sarvāḥ samudragāḥ puṇyāḥ sarve puṇyā nagottamāḥ |
sarvam āyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ sarve puṇyā vanāśrayāḥ ||
atha gaṅgā-sarasvatī-yamunā-narmadā-vipāśā-vitastā–kauśikī–nandā–virajā–candrabhāgā–sarayūḥ-utpathavartī-sindhuḥ-kṛṣṇā-veṇī-śoṇā-tāpī-pārāvatī–pāṣāṇakī-gomatī-gaṇḍakī-bāhudā-pampā-devikā-kāverī-tāmraparṇī-carmaṇvati-vetravatī-godāvarī-tuṅgabhadrā-sucakṣuraruṇā-[202] ceti mahānadyaḥ puṇyatamāḥ | gaṅgādvāraṃ kanakhalaṃ śakrāvatāraṃ saukaraṃ prayāgo gaṅgāsāgaram iti etāni gaṅgāyās tīrthāni | plākṣaṃ prasravaṇaṃ vṛddhakanyakaṃ sārasvatam ādityatīrthaṃ badarīpāñcālaṃ vaijayantaṃ pṛthūdakaṃ naimiṣaṃ vinaśanaṃ camasodbhedaṃ prabhāsa iti sārasvatatīrthāni | kāverīsaṅgamaṃ sarasvatīsaṅgamaṃ gandhamādanam iti sāmudrākhatīrthāni puṇyatamānīti | puṣkaraṃ mūrdhakaṃ gayāśiraḥ kurukṣetram iti puṇyakṣetrāṇi | vārāṇasī mahābhairavaṃ devadāruvanaṃ prācīnabarhiḥ kedāraṃ madhyamamahālayaṃ kumāramahālayaṃ rudrāvatāraṃ vaṅgalaṃ viṣṇuśivaṃ mahākālaṃ puruṣottamaṃ jambumārgaṃ cety etāny āyatanāni | himavān udayo mahendro viduraḥ śataśṛṅgaḥ śrīparvato devaparvato nīlaparvato vindhyaparvataḥ pāriyātraś ceti parvatāḥ puṇyatamāḥ pāpaṃ nāśayanti | anyāni kṣetrāyatana-parvata-āśrama-tīrthāni puṇyatamāni bhavanti | [203] iti | viśvāmitraḥ |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādīni śuddhyabhyudayakāraṇam |
prakāśe ca rahasye cāpy anukte saṃśaye ‘sphuṭe ||
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ śiśukṛcchraḥ parākakaḥ || iti |
jamadagniḥ |
tīrthe tu pādaḥ kṛcchraṃ syān nadyām ardhaphalaṃ labhet |
dviguṇaṃ tu mahāndyāṃ saṅgame triguṇaṃ bhavet || iti |
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
atikṛcchraḥ parṇakṛcchraḥ saumyaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrakaḥ |
mahāsāntapanaṃ śodhye taptakṛcchras tu pāvanaḥ |
japopavāsakṛcchras tu brahmakūrcas tu śodhanaḥ ||
ete vyastāḥ samastā vā pratyekaṃ dvyekato ‘pi vā |
pātakādiṣu sarveṣu upapātakeṣu ca yatnataḥ ||
[204]        kāryāś cāndrāyaṇayutā kevalā vā viśuddhaye |
upavāsas trirātraṃ vā māsaḥ pakṣas tadardhakam ||
ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhāni kāryā śuddhiḥ phalair vinā |
upapātakayuktānām anādiṣṭeṣu caiva hi ||
śaktijātiguṇān dṛṣṭvā sakṛd buddhikṛtaṃ laghu |
anubandhādikaṃ dṛṣṭvā kalpyaṃ sarvaṃ yathāgamam ||
prakāśa uktaṃ yat kiṃcid viṃśo bhāgo rahasyake |
triṃśadbhāgaḥ ṣaṣṭibhāgaḥ kalpyo jātyādyapekṣayā || iti | (NSm 13.21–27)
iti kṛcchrapratyāmnāyā nirūpitāḥ
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartaka-
śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyaduhrandharasya
mādhavāmātyasya kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ
dvādaśādhyāye prāyaścitaprakaraṇam ||
[205]
atha karmavipāko nirūpyate
yady api loke karmaśabdaḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛtayoḥ sādhāraṇaḥ tathāpy atra prāyaścittasya prakṛtatvāt tannivartyatayā duṣkṛtasyaiva buddhisthatvāt tatraiva paryavasyati | veśeṣeṇa pacanaṃ vipākaḥ phaladaśā | karmaṇāṃ vipākaḥ karmavipākaḥ | sa ca vipākas trividhaḥ janma cāyur bogaś ceti | etat sarvaṃ patañjalir yogaśāstre sūtrayāmāsa: “sati mūle tadvipāko jātyāurbhogāḥ” iti (YogaSū 2.13) | asya sūtrasyāyam arthaḥ — avidyāsmitārāgadveṣābhiniveśātmakāḥ pañca kleśāḥ  karmaṇāṃ mūlam, “kleśamūlaḥ karmāśayaḥ” (YogSū 2.12) it sutritatvāt | sati tasmin pañcakleśātmake mūle tāni karmāṇi vipacyante | dagdhakleśabījasya tu jīvanmuktasya na saṃcitāni karmāṇi phalam ārabhante, jñānāgninā bhasmasātkṛtatvāt | yathā vituṣo vrīhir na prarohati tadvat | tasya tuṣasthānīyaṃ kleśapañcakam upajīvyaiva karmāṇi janmāditrarūpeṇa vipacyante | tatra janma kṛmyādi,
[206]        patitaṃ yājayitvā tu krimiyonau prajāyate | (BrPu 217.40)
ityādismaraṇāt | āyuḥ pañcadaśavarṣādikam,
tatra jīvati varṣāṇi daśa vā pañca vā dvijaḥ | (BrPu 217.40)
ity abhidhānāt | bhogo yamayātanādiḥ,
yamaloke tu te ghorāṃ labhante pariyātanām | (BrPu 125.137)
ityādismaraṇāt |
tam etaṃ jātyāyurbhogalakṣaṇaṃ vipākaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣaśāstrodāharaṇena bahudhā prapañcayiṣyāmaḥ |
nanu – nāyaṃ vipāko vidhātuṃ pratiṣeddhuṃ vā śakyate ananuṣṭheyatvād avarjanīyatvāc ca | ata evaitasmin vidhipratiṣedhapare śāstre tannirūpaṇam anyuktam |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, prāyaścittarthavādatvena tadupayogāt | arthavādas tu vihite pravartakatvena pratiṣiddhān nivartakatvena ca vidhipratiṣedhaikavākyatāṃ bhajate – iti nyāyavidāṃ maryādā |
atra ca prājāpatyādyanuṣṭhānarūpasya prāyaścittasya kleśātmakatvāt [207] rāgataḥ pravṛttir na saṃbhavatīti tatra pravṛttisiddhaye prarocanā kartavyā |
nanu – vihiteṣv agniṣṭomādiṣu svargādivipākopavarṇanena prarocayituṃ śakyate, svargādīnām anukūlaphalatvāt | kṣayarogādīnāṃ tu pratikūlaphalatvena tadupavarṇanaṃ na prarocanāyopayuktam |
viṣama upanyāsaḥ | tatra hi svargādisādhane prarocanam utpādanīyam | na tv atra kṣayarogādisādhane prarocanam utpadyate | kiṃ tarhi kṣayarogādinivartake prāyaścitte | tannivṛttiś cānukūlaṃ phalam |
nanu – evam api kṣayarogādinivṛttirūpa eva prāyaścittātmakasya karmaṇo vipākaḥ prarocakatvena vaktavyaḥ | na tu brahmahatyādivipākaḥ, tasyāprarocakatvāt |
maivam | dvividhā hi brahmahatyādayaḥ | saṃcitā saṃceṣyamāṇāś ca | tatra saṃceṣyamāṇeṣu pratiṣedhaviṣayeṣu brahmahatyādiṣu kṣayarogādyupanyāsaḥ brāhmaṇāvagoraṇādau śatayātanādivat tannivartakārthavādatvenānveti | saṃciteṣu pratikūlaṃ phalaṃ śrutvā tata udvijamānas tannivṛttyupāyam ākāṅkṣati | ata udvegaprayojakatayā prāyaścitte ‘pi pravartaktvaṃ bhavatīti pravartakanivartakadvividhārthavādatvena ayaṃ karmavipākopanyāsa upayujyate | [208]
tad evaṃ saṃcitasaṃceṣyamāṇayor upayogaḥ | ārabdhaphaleṣu pratikūlaphalasya pratyakṣadarśanād evodvegaḥ sidhyati | pravartakaṃ tu vyādhyādinivṛttirūpasya prāyaścittavipākasyopavarṇanam | ataḥ kṣayarogādirūpas tannivṛttirūpaś ca vipāko ‘tra nirūpyate | tatra sāmānyena pāpakarmavipākaḥ śivadharmottare ‘bhihitaḥ |
athātaḥ patatāṃ puṃsām adharmaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
narakārṇave mahāghore pātanāt pāpam ucyate || iti |
viṣṇudharmottare ‘pi |
ativāhikasaṃjñas tu deho bhavati bhārgava |
kevalaṃ tan manuṣyāṇāṃ mṛtyukāla upasthite ||
yāmyair narair manuṣyāṇāṃ taccharīraṃ bhṛgūttama |
nīyate yāmyamārgeṇa nānyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ dvija ||
manuṣyāḥ pratipadyante svargaṃ narakam eva vā |
naivānye prāṇinaḥ kecit sarve te phalabhojinaḥ ||
śubhānām aśubhānāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ bhṛgunandana |
saṃcayaḥ kriyate loke manuṣyair eva kevalam ||
[209]        tasmān manuṣyas tu mṛto yamalokaṃ prapadyate |
nānyaḥ prāṇī mahābhāga phalayonau vyavasthitaḥ ||
yāmyalokaṃ prapannasya puruṣasya tathā yamaḥ |
yonīś ca narakāś caiva nirūpayati karmaṇā || iti | (ViDhU 2.102.2–7)
brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
karmaṇā manasā vacā ye dharmavimukhā narāḥ |
yamaloke tu te ghorāṃ labhante pariyātanām ||
ihālpam api kṛtvā tu naraḥ karmāśubhātmakam |
prāpnoti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yamalokeṣu yātanām || iti | (BrPu 125.136–42)
viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
pāpakṛd yāti narakaṃ prāyaścittaprāṅmukhaḥ |
pāpānām anurūpāṇi prāyaścittāni yad yathā ||
tathā tathaiva saṃsmṛtya proktāni paramarṣibhiḥ || iti | (ViPu 4.5.21–22)
viṣṇudharmottare |
prāyaścittaiḥ kṣayaṃ yāti pāpaṃ kṛtam asaṃśayam |
[210]        prāyaścittavihīnā ye rājabhiś cāpy adaṇḍitāḥ |
narakaṃ pratipadyante tiryagyoniṃ tathaiva ca ||
manuṣyam api cāsādya bhavantīha tathāṅkitāḥ |
prāyaścittam ataḥ kāryaṃ kalmaṣasyāpanuttaye || iti | (ViDhU 2.70.3–5)
bhaviṣyottare ‘pi |
devatiryaṅmanuṣyāṇām adharmaniratātmanām |
dharmarājaḥ smṛtaḥ śāstā sughorair vividhair vadhaiḥ ||
niyamācārasaktānāṃ pramādāt skhalitātmanām |
prāyaścittair guruḥ śāstā na tu tair iṣyatte yamaḥ ||
pāradārikacaurāṇām anyāyavyavahāriṇām |
nṛpatiḥ śāsakaḥ proktaḥ pracchannānāṃ ca dharmarāṭ ||
tasmāt kṛtasya pāpasya prāyaścittaṃ samācaret | iti |
brahmapurāṇe |
tataś ca yātanādehaṃ kleśena pratipadyate |
tatkarmajaṃ yātanārtham amātāpitṛsaṃbhavam ||
[211]        tatpramāṇavayovasthāsaṃsthānaiḥ prāktanaṃ yathā |
vihāya sumahāguptaṃ śarīraṃ pāñcabhautikam ||
anyat śarīram ādatte yātanārthaṃ svakarmajam |
tanmātraguṇasaṃyuktaṃ nānātmīyaṃ svakarmajam ||
dṛḍhaṃ śarīram āpnoti sukhaduḥkhopabhuktaye |
tena bhuktvā svakarmāṇi pāpakartā naro bhṛśam ||
sukhāni dhārmiko ‘duṣṭa ihānīto yamakṣayāt || iti | (BrPu 214.28–32)
manus tu mānasādikarmaviśeṣeṇa vipākaviśeṣam āha |
cāturvarṇyasya kṛtsno ‘yam ukto dharmas tvayānagha |
karmaṇāṃ phalanirvṛttiṃ śaṃsa nas tattvataḥ parām ||
sa tān uvāca dharmātmā maharṣīn mānavo bhṛguḥ |
asya sarvasya śṛṇuta karmayogasya nirṇayam ||
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ karma manovāgdehasaṃbhavam |
karmajā gatayo nṝṇām uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ ||
[212]        tasyeha trividhasyāpi tryadhiṣṭhānasya dehinaḥ |
daśalakṣaṇayuktasya mano vidyāt pravartakam ||
paradraveṣv abhidhyānaṃ manasāniṣṭacintanam |
vitathābhiniveśaś ca trividhaṃ karma mānasam ||
pāruṣyam anṛtaṃ caiva pauśunyaṃ cāpi sarvaśaḥ |
asaṃbaddhapralāpaś ca vāṅmayaṃ syāc caturvidham ||
adattānām upādānaṃ hiṃsā caivāvidhanataḥ |
paradāropasevā ca śārīraṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam ||
mānasaṃ manasaivāyam upabhuṅkte śubhāśubham |
vācaiva vākkṛtaṃ karma kāyenaiva tu kāyikam ||
śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣair yāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ |
vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasair antyajātitām || iti | (MDh 12.1–9)
hārīto ‘pi |
sarvābhakṣyabhakṣaṇam abhojyabhojanam apeyapānam agamyāgamanam ayājyayājanam asatpratigrahaṇaṃ paradārābhigamanaṃ dravyāharaṇaṃ prāṇihiṃsā ceti śārīrāṇi | pāruṣyam anṛtaṃ vivādaḥ śrutivikrayaś ceti [213] vācikāni | paropatāpanaṃ parābhidrohaḥ krodho lobho moho ‘haṅkāraś ceti mānasāni | tad etāny aṣṭādaśa nairayāṇi karmāṇi yasmin yasmin vayasi karoti yaḥ sa tasmin tasmin vayasi śārīravācikamānasāni prāpnoti | evaṃ hy āha |
yasyāṃ yasyām avasthāyāṃ yat karoti śubhāśubham |
tasyāṃ tasyām avasthāyāṃ tat tat phalam avāpnuyāt ||
śarīreṇaiva śārīraṃ vāṅmayena tu vācikam |
mānasaṃ manasaivāyam upabhukte śubhāśubham || iti |
(HārSm 32.13–18)
ātmana īśvararūpatvaṃ pratipādyāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yady evaṃ sa kathaṃ brahman pāpayoniṣu jāyate |
īśvaraḥ sa kathaṃ bhāvair aniṣṭaiḥ saṃprayujyate ||
karaṇair anvitasyāpi pūrvajñānaṃ kathaṃ ca na |
vetti sarvagatāṃ kasmāt sarvago ‘pi na vedanām ||
antyapakṣisthāvaratāṃ manovākkāyakarmajaiḥ |
doṣaiḥ prayāti jīvo ‘yaṃ bhavayoniśateṣu ca ||
[214]        anantāś ca yathā bhāvāḥ śrīreṣu śarīriṇām |
rūpāṇy api tathaiveha sarvayoniṣu dehinām ||
vipākaḥ karmaṇāṃ pretya keṣāṃcid iha jāyate |
iha cāmutra caikeṣāṃ bhāvas tatra prayojanam ||
pradravyāṇy abhidhyāyaṃs tathāniṣṭāni cintayan |
vitathābhiniveśī ca jāyate ‘ntyāsu yoniṣu ||
puruṣo ‘nṛtavādī ca piśunaḥ paruṣas tathā |
saṃbaddhapralāpī ca mṛgapakṣiṣu jāyate ||
adattānānanirataḥ paradāropasevakaḥ |
hiṃsakaś cāvidhānena sthāvareṣv abhijāyate || iti | (YDh 3.129–36)
sattvādiguṇaviśeṣeṇa vipākaviśeṣaṃ sa evāha |
ātmajñaḥ śaucavān dāntas tapasvī vijitendriyaḥ |
dharmakṛd vedavid yāti sāttviko devayonitām ||
asatkāryarato dhīra ārambhī viṣayī ca yaḥ |
sa rājaso manuṣyeṣu mrto janmādhighacchati ||
[215]        nidrāluḥ krūrakṛl lubdho nāstiko yācakas tathā |
pramādavān bhinnavṛtto bhavet tiryakṣu tāmasaḥ ||
rajasā tamasā caivaṃ samāviṣṭo bhramann iha |
bhāvair aniṣṭaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ saṃsāraṃ praipadyate || iti | (YDh 3.137–40)
manur api |
sattvaṃ rajas tamaś ceti trīn vidyād ātmano guṇān |
yair vyāpyemān sthito bhāvān mahān sarvān aśeṣataḥ ||
yo yadaiṣāṃ guṇo dehe sākalyenātiricyate |
sa tadā tadguṇaprāyaṃ taṃ karoti śarīriṇam ||
sattvaṃ jñānaṃ tamo ‘jñānaṃ rāgadveṣau rajaḥ smṛtam |
etad vyāptimad eteṣāṃ sarvabhūtāśritaṃ vapuḥ ||
tatra yat prītisaṃyuktaṃ kiṃcid ātmani lakṣayet |
praśāntam iva śuddhābhaṃ sattvaṃ tad upadhārayet ||
[216]        yat tu duḥkhasamāyuktam aprītikaram ātmanaḥ |
tad rajo ‘pratipaṃ vidyāt satataṃ hāri dehinām ||
yat tu syān mohasaṃyuktam avyaktaṃ viṣayātmakam |
apratarkyam avijñeyaṃ tamas tad upadhārayet ||
trayāṇām api caiteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ yaḥ phalodayaḥ |
agryo madho jaghanyaś ca tam pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
vedādbhyāsas tatpo jñānaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
dharmakriyātmacintā ca sāttvikaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam ||
ārambharucitā dhairyam asatkāryaparigrahaḥ |
dharmakriyātmacintā ca sāttvikaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam ||
ārambharucitā dharyam asatkāryaparigrahaḥ |
viṣayopasevā cājasraṃ rājasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam ||
lobhaḥ svapno ‘dhṛtiḥ krauryaṃ nāstikyaṃ bhinnavṛttitā |
yāciṣṇutā pramādaś ca tāmasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam ||
trayāṇām api caiteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ triṣu tiṣṭhatām |
idaṃ sāmāsikaṃ jñeyaṃ kramaśo guṇalakṣaṇam ||
[217]        yat karma kṛtvā kurvaṃś ca kariṣyaṃś caiva lajjate |
taj jñeyaṃ viduṣā sarvaṃ tāmasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam ||
yenāsmin karmaṇā loke khyātim icchati puṣkalām |
na ca śocaty asampattau tad vijñeya tu rājasam ||
yat sarveṇecchati jñātuṃ yan na lajjati cācaran |
yena tuṣyati cātmāsya tat sattvaguṇalakṣaṇam ||
tamaso lakṣaṇaṃ kāmo rajasas tv artha ucyate |
sattvasya lakṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ śraiṣṭhyam eṣāṃ yathākramam ||
yena yāṃs tu guṇenaiṣāṃ saṃsārān pratipadyate |
tān samāsena vakṣyāmi sarvasyāsya yathākramam ||
devatvaṃ sāttvikā yānti samuṣyatvaṃ hi rājasāḥ |
tiryaktvaṃ tāmasā nityam ity eṣā trividhā gatiḥ ||
trividhā trividhaiṣā tu vijñeyā gauṇikī gatiḥ |
adhamā madhyamāgryā ca karmavidyāviśeṣataḥ ||
sthāvarāḥ kṛmikīṭāś ca matsyāḥ sarpāḥ sarīsṛpāḥ |
paśavaś ca śṛgālāś ca jaghanyā tāmasī gatiḥ ||
[218]        hastinaś ca turaṅgāś ca śūdrā mlecchāś ca garhitāḥ |
siṃhā vyāghrā varāhāś ca madhyamā tāmasī gatiḥ ||
cāraṇāś ca suparṇāś ca puruṣāś caiva dāmbhikāḥ |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāś ca tāmaseṣūttamā gatiḥ ||
jhallā mallā naṭāś caiva puruṣāḥ śastravṛttayaḥ |
dyūtapānaprasaktāś ca jaghanyā rājasī gatiḥ ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāś caiva rājñāṃ caiva purohitāḥ |
vādayuddhapradhānāś ca madhyamā rājasī gatiḥ ||
gandharvā guhyakā yakṣā vibudhānucarāś ca ye |
tathaivāpsarasaḥ sarvā rājaseṣūttamā gatiḥ ||
tāpasā yatayo viprā ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ |
nakṣatrāṇi ca daityāś ca prathamā sāttvikī gatiḥ ||
tāpasā yatayo viprā ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ |
nakṣatrāṇi ca daityāś ca prathamā sāttvikī gatiḥ ||
yajvāna ṛṣayo vedā devā jyotīṃṣi vatsarāḥ |
pitaraś caiva sādhyāś ca dvitīyā sāttvikī gatiḥ ||
[219]        brahmā viśvasṛjo dharmo mahān avyaktam eva ca |
uttamāṃ sāttvikīm etāṃ gatim āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
eṣa sarvaḥ samuddiṣṭas triprakārasya karmaṇaḥ |
trividhas trividhaḥ kṛtsnaḥ saṃsāraḥ sārvabhautikaḥ || iti | (MDh 12.24–51)
atha pretatvādihetubhyo nānāvidhebhyaḥ pāpaviśeṣebhyo yoniviśeṣā abhihitā viṣṇudharmottare |
asahiṣṇutayānyasya guṇānāṃ kāraṇaṃ vinā |
mahat pāpaṃ samutpannaṃ tad asya pretakāraṇam ||
brahmasvahāriṇaś caite pāpāḥ pretatvam āgatāḥ |
paradāraratāḥ kecit svāmidroharatāḥ pare ||
mitradroharatāḥ kecid deśe ‘smin bhṛśadāruṇe |
svakarmabiś cyutāḥ sarve jāyante pretayoniṣu || iti | (ViDhU 2.70.23–25)
padmapurāṇe |
ayājyayājakaś caiva yājyānāṃ ca vivarjakaḥ |
[220]        virato viṣṇuvidyāsu sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
sāmānyadakṣiṇāṃ labdhvā gṛhṇāty eko vimohitaḥ |
nāstikyabhāvanirataḥ sa preto jāyate naraḥ || iti | (PadPu 45.50–53)
skānde camatkārakhaṇḍe |
yo bhaven mānavaḥ kṣudras tathā paiśunyakārakaḥ |
brāhmaṇānvayasaṃbhūto vṛthāmāṃsāśanaś ca yaḥ ||
prāṇināṃ hiṃsako nityaṃ sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
akṛtvā devakāryaṃ yas tathā ca pitṛtarpaṇam |
yo ‘śnāti bhṛtyavargebhyo ‘datvā pretaḥ sa jāyate ||
paradārarataś caiva paravittāpahārakaḥ |
parāpavādasaṃtuṣṭaḥ sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
kanyāṃ yacchati vṛddhāya nīcāya dhanalipsayā |
kurūpāya kuśīlāya sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
devastrīguruvittāni gṛhītvā na prayacchati |
[221]        viśeṣād brāhmaṇendrāṇāṃ sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
dīyamānasya vittasya brāhmaṇebhyas tu pāpakṛt |
vighnam ācarate yas tu sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
śūdrānnenodrasthena brāhmaṇo mriyate yadi |
trikālajño vedavedī dharmajño ‘tha ṣaḍaṅgavit ||
caturvedy api yo vipraḥ sa preto jāyate naraḥ |
śāstrakartā granthakartā yady api syāt ṣaḍaṅgavit ||
kuladeśocitaṃ karma yas tyaktvānyat samācaret |
kāmād vā yadi vā mohāt sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
ucchiṣṭasya viśeṣeṇa antarikṣamṛtasya ca ||
viṣāgnijalamṛtyūnāṃ tathā caivātmaghātināṃ |
pretatvaṃ jāyate nūnaṃ satyam etan na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
agnipurāṇe |
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vṛddhaṃ jñātiṃ sādhujanaṃ tathā |
lobhāt tyajati yas tv etān sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
kujyoniḥ kutsito vaidyaḥ kujñānī ca kudeśikaḥ |
śūdrānugrahakartā ca sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
nindako dvijadevānāṃ gītavādyarataḥ sadā |
vṛddhaṃ bālaṃ guruṃ viprān yo ‘vamatya bhunakti vai ||
kanyāṃ dadāti śulkena sa preto jāyate naraḥ |
[222]        nyāsāpahartā mitradhruk parapākarataḥ sadā ||
nirdoṣaṃ suhṛdaṃ bhāryāṃ ṛtukāle na yāti yaḥ |
na saheta yajñas teṣāṃ sa preto jāyate naraḥ ||
hastyaśvarathayānāni mṛtaśayyāsanāni ca |
kṛṣṇājinaṃ tu gṛhṇāti anāpadi tu yo dvijaḥ ||
tathobhayamukhīṃ dolāṃ saśailāṃ medinīṃ dvijaḥ |
kurukṣetre ca yad dānaṃ cāṇḍālāt patitāt tathā ||
māsike ca navaśrāddhe bhuñjan pretānnam eva ca |
bhūmikanyāpahartā ca sa preto jāyate naraḥ || iti | (AgPu 201.41–48)
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
apahṛtya ca sarvasvaṃ devasvaṃ ca tathā param |
dattapahāradānāc ca paiśācyaṃ yāti vai naraḥ ||
yonikāryaṃ ca yo devi āhitena tu kārayet |
so ‘pi yāti piśācatvaṃ svāmidroheṇa mānavaḥ ||
[223]        svāmidravyaṃ gṛhītvā tu na yajen na dadāti ca |
ātmanaḥ poṣako mūḍhaḥ piśācatvaṃ sa gacchati ||
maṭhādhipatyaṃ yo devi kurute madamohitaḥ |
vināśayen maṭhadravyaṃ nirmālyaṃ yaś ca bhakṣati ||
parasya yoṣitaṃ hṛtvā brahmasvam apahṛtya ca |
araṇye nirjale deśe bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ ||
adharmaṃ dharmam ity āhur ye tu mohavaśaṃ gatāḥ |
hartāraḥ paradārāṇāṃ cauryeṇa balato ‘pi vā ||
araṇye nirjale deśe bhavanti brahmarākṣasāḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
saṃyogaṃ patitair gatvā parasyaiva ca yoṣitam |
apahṛtya ca viprasvaṃ bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ || (MDh 12.60)
guruṃ tvaṃkṛtya huṃkṛtya vipraṃ nirjitya vādataḥ |
araṇye nirjale deśe bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ ||
[224]        ye brāhmaṇān pradviṣanti garhitvā te hi rākṣasāḥ |
ācāryam ṛtvijaś caiva guruṃ caiva tapasvinam ||
munīṃś cāpy avamanyante te bhavantīha rākṣasāḥ || iti |
vāmanapurāṇe |
caṇḍālād antyajād vāpi pratigṛhṇāti dakṣiṇām |
yājako yājyamānas tu so ‘śmāntaḥ sthalakīṭakaḥ || (VāPu 61.27)
skānde revākhaṇḍe |
bubhukṣiteṣu bhṛtyeṣu ya eko ‘śnāti mānavaḥ |
śvayoniṃ sa samāsādya caṇḍālo jāyate naraḥ ||
vedoktaṃ yaḥ parityajya dharmam anyaṃ samācaret |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi śvayonau jāyate dhruvam ||
vedārthanindakā ye ca ye ca brāhmaṇanindakāḥ |
iha janmani śūdrās te mṛtāḥ śvāno bhavanti hi || iti | (SkPu Rev 30.37–39)
brahmapurāṇe |
yadi putrasamaṃ śiṣyaṃ gurur jahyād akāraṇam |
ātmanaḥ kāmakāreṇa so ‘pi hiṃsraḥ prajāyate ||
[225]        pitaraṃ mātaraṃ caiva yas tu putro ‘vamanyate |
so ‘pi vipro mṛto jantuḥ pūrvaṃ jāyeta gardabhaḥ ||
gardabhatvaṃ tu saṃprāpya daśa varṣāṇi jīvati |
saṃvatsaraṃ tu kumbhīras tato jāyeta mānavaḥ ||
jyeṣṭhaṃ pitṛsamaṃ cāpi bhrātaraṃ yo ‘vamanyate ||
so ‘pi mṛtyum avāpnoti krauñcayonau ca jāyate |
āśāpahartā tu naraḥ kṛmiyonau prajāyate ||
viśvāsahartā tu naro mīno jāyeta durmatiḥ |
upasthite vivāhe tu yajñe dāne mahotsave ||
mohāt karoti yo vighnaṃ sa mṛto jāyate kṛmiḥ |
kṛtaghnaḥ sa mṛto vipro yamasya viṣayaṃ gataḥ ||
yamasya vinayaiḥ krūrair vadhaṃ prāpnoti dāruṇam |
yātanāḥ prāpya tatrasthas tato bahvīḥ samā dvijāḥ ||
tato daṃśaḥ krtaghnaś ca saṃsāraṃ pratipadyate |
jalapravarṣaṇaṃ yas tu bhindyān matsyo bhavet tu saḥ ||
[226]        ayonigo vṛko hi syād ulūkaḥ krayavañcanāt |
pratiśrutya dvijebhyaś cāpy adatvā madhuko bhavet ||
labhed devalako rājan yoniṃ caṇḍālasaṃjñitām |
mṛtasyaikadaśāhe tu bhuñjan śvā cābhijāyate || iti | (BrPu 218.48–83)
garuḍapurāṇe |
kauṭsākṣyapradāḥ pāpā amedhyakṛmayaś ciram |
bhūtvā bhavanti sarpās te piśācās tadanantaram ||
gurumitradruhaḥ pāpā ye ca svāmidruho janāḥ |
dvijaśiṣyadruhaś caiva kṛtaghnā nāstikās tathā ||
tyāgino bāndhavānāṃ ca tyāginaḥ śaraṇārthinām |
amedhyakṛmayaḥ pāpā mṛgavyādhā bhavanti ca ||
ururogagaṇopetā jāyante śambukā jale |
candrārkagrahaṇe bhuktvā jāyate kuñjaro naraḥ || (GaPu 281.5–9)
[227] iti | śivadharmottare |
vipraḥ pañcamahāyajñān akṛtvā yo ‘nnam aśnute |
viḍvarāho bhaven nityaṃ kravyāśī vyādha eva ca ||
dvijas tv ekaśaphānāṃ ca vikrayāj jāyate mṛgaḥ |
matsarī viṭkṛmiś caiva avajñākāry atha kṛmiḥ || iti |
vāyusaṃhitāyām |
kharo vai bahuyājitvāt kāko ‘mantritabhojanāt |
aparīkṣitabhojī syād vānaro vijane vane ||
avidyāṃ yaḥ prayaccheta balīvardo bhavet tu saḥ |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ vipre datvā vai kubjatāṃ vrajet || iti |
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
agnihotraṃ bhaved yasya na kuryāt tasya rakṣaṇam |
parvātikramakārī tu mṛgatvaṃ yāti brāhmaṇaḥ ||
[228]        vedopajīvikāḥ pāpāḥ strīlolāḥ satataṃ ca ye |
bhavanti ceha kākāḥ syuś ciraṃ viḍbhuja eva ca ||
pāyasaṃ kṛsaraṃ caiva havīṃṣi madhurāṇi ca |
adatvā cāgniviprebhyaḥ prāsaṇād bhujago bhavet || iti |
nāradapurāṇe |
bhartuḥ saukhyena yā nārī saukhyayuktā na jāyate |
sā śyenī jāyate rājan trīṇi varṣāṇi pañca ca ||
vācā cākrośikā yā tu saṃnirudhya patiṃ hriyā |
akāryaṃ kārayet pāpā sā nārī valgulī bhavet ||
bhartur arthe hi yā vittaṃ vidyamānaṃ na yacchati |
jīvitaṃ vā varārohe viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ ||
kṛmiyonivinirmuktā kāṣṭhakīṭā bhavet tu sā |
sa sādhayanti kāryāṇi prabhūṇāṃ ye vicetasaḥ ||
bhūtvā vedanabhoktāro jāyante mūlatāṃ hi te || iti | (NāPu 31.9–14)
vṛddhagautamaḥ|
maulyād vedopadeṣṭā ca vyāghro bhavati mānavaḥ |
jambuko jāyate jantur ananujñātapāṭhakaḥ ||
śūdrāt paṭhati cāṇḍālo brahmadravyopajīvakaḥ ||
[229] iti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
paradravyāṇy abhidhyāyaṃs tathāniṣṭāni cintayan |
vitathābhiniveśī ca jāyate ‘ntyāsu yoniṣu ||
puruṣo ‘nṛtavādī ca piśunaḥ puruṣas tathā |
anibaddhapralāpī ca mṛgapakṣiṣu jāyate ||
adattādānanirataḥ paradāropasevakaḥ |
hiṃsakaś cāvidhānena sthāvareṣu prajāyate || iti |
atha nānāvidhaniṣiddhakarmaśeṣaphalāni
tatra umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
śvapākapulkasādīnāṃ kutsitānāṃ acetasām |
kuleṣu te ‘bhijāyante guruvṛddhāvamāninaḥ ||
guruṇā hy ananujñāto yo vedābhyāsam ācaret |
sa tu jātivihīneṣu jāyate bhuvi mānavaḥ |
adharmaṃ dharmam ity āhur ye tu mohavaśaṃgatāḥ ||
te ca kālakṛtodyogāḥ saṃbhavantīha mānuṣe |
nirhomā nirvaṣaṭkārās te bhavanti narādhamāḥ ||
[230] iti | skānde nāgarakhaṇḍe |
prāpnuvanti kudeśe ca janma ye hetuvādakāḥ |
svāmidroharatā ye ca kukule janma cāpnuyuḥ ||
adatvā ye tathāśnanti pitṛdevadvijātiṣu |
durbhikṣe janatāpe ca kudeśe janma cāpnuyuḥ ||
ye ca kurvanti dampatyor vivādaṃ sānurāgayoḥ |
virūpā bhramamāṇāś ca sarvalokavivarjitāḥ ||
daridrā jāyayā bhraṣṭā bhavanti vigatāyuṣaḥ |
kanyādāne ca vighnaṃ yo vikrayaṃ yaḥ karoti vā ||
sa kanyāḥ kevalāḥ sūte na putraṃ kevalaṃ kvacit |
jāyante tāś ca bandhakyo vidhavā durbhagās tathā || iti |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe ‘pi |
yair na dattam nariḥ kiṃcit teṣāṃ cihnaṃ daridratā |
jāyante rogiṇo raudrā hiṃsrāḥ kubjāś ca vāmanāḥ ||
aputrāḥ kalahākrāntāḥ paradāropasevakāḥ |
mahārajanakarmāṇaḥ kucelāḥ kuśrīriṇaḥ ||
[231]        kudambhāś capalā lubdhā mūrkhā niṣṭhurabhāṣiṇaḥ |
dānahīnā bhavanty ete devabrahmasvabhakṣakāḥ || iti |
skānde revākhaṇḍe |
pitā mātā gurur bhrātā anyo vā vikalendriyaḥ |
bhavanti nādṛtā yais tu te bhavanti narādhamāḥ ||
iha mānuṣaloke ‘smin dīnāndhās te bhavanti ca |
ye tyajanti svakāṃ bhāryāṃ mūḍhāḥ paṇḍitamāninaḥ ||
te yānti narakaṃ ghoraṃ tāmisraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
tatra varṣaśatasyānte iha mānuṣatāṃ gatāḥ ||
[232]        duścarmā durbhagaś caiva daridraḥ saṃprajāyate |
jāyate gṛdhrasīyuktaḥ parabhāryopasevakaḥ ||
gadgado ‘nṛtavādī syāt mūkaś caiva gavām ṛte |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ vipre dadan vai kubjatāṃ vrajet ||
mātsaryād api jātyandho dveṣāc ca badhiro bhavet |
adatvā bhakṣyam aśnāti anapatyo bhavec ca saḥ || iti |
gaūḍapurāṇe |
avamatya ca ye yānti bhagavatkīrtanaṃ narāḥ |
bādhiryam upayānty āśu te vai janmani janmani ||
paśyanto bhagavaddvāraṃ tāmasās tatparicchadam |
akṛtvā tatpraṇāmādi ye yānti puruṣādhamāḥ ||
jatyandhād te ‘bhijāyante ‘py aṅgahīnāparigrahāḥ |
paṅgavaḥ pādahīnāś ca kuṣṭhino bhuvi ye narāḥ ||
viṣṇucchāyā dhruvaṃ tais tu laṅghitā syān na saṃśayaḥ |
tathā ca bhagavaddhāmnaś chāyām ākramate tu yaḥ ||
[233]        araṇye jāyate paṅguḥ sa vai rogādipīḍitaḥ || iti | (GarPu 281.13–17)
viṣṇudharmottare ‘pi |
piśunaḥ pūtināsaḥ syāt sūcakaḥ pūtivaktrakaḥ |
khalvāṭo matsarī rāma nāstiko vedanindakaḥ ||
bhūya eva samāpnoti tathā nāstikatāṃ dvijaḥ |
ghūrṇikas tailikaś caiva andho bhavati mānavaḥ ||
pratikūlaṃ guror yas tu so ‘pasmārī ca jāyate |
miṣṭāśy eko mahābhāga vātagulmī prajāyate ||
bhagabhakṣas tu kuṇḍāśī dīrgharogī ca pīḍakaḥ |
gurusvāmidvikākrośaṃ vedanindāṃ tathaiva ca |
śrutvā bhavati durbuddhir badhiro mānvādhamaḥ ||
devadattena dīppena kṛtvā karma sudurmatiḥ |
jāyate budbudākṣo vā netrarogayuto ‘pi vā ||
tathaiva dīnarūpaś ca tena pāpena karmaṇā |
