[234]        hīnavarṇas tadā loke bhavaty atyantadurmatiḥ ||
virūpa eva bhavati tathāvikreyavikrayī |
vināparādhena tathā kṛtvā patnyadhivedanam ||
sa rājabhayam āpnoti vyavahāre tathaiva ca |
mūlakarma tathā kṛtvā vaśīkaraṇakāraṇāt ||
dveṣyo bhavati loke ‘smin jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
parivettā tathā loke sarvavyādhisamanvitaḥ ||
ayājyayākaś caiva jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ |
vedavikrayiko mūrkhaḥ kitavaś caiva jāyate ||
nāstikas tu daridraḥ syāt sarvakarmavivarjitaḥ |
atimānapravṛttas tu jāyate kutsite kule ||
śvapākapulkasādīnāṃ kutsitānām acetasām |
kuleṣu te ‘bhijāyante guruvṛddhāpavādakāḥ ||
śvabhre tu maraṇaṃ like viṣaiḥ sthāvarajāṅgamaiḥ |
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca nikhibhyaś ca caṇḍālād brāhmaṇāt tathā ||
[235]        gṛhādhaḥpatanāc caiva drumādhaḥpatanāt tathā |
gobhyaś ca maraṇaṃ loke vijñeyaṃ pāpakarmaṇām ||
tāḍitas tāḍyate loke hārito hāryate tathā |
hatvā vadham āpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
yad yat parasyācarati tat tad asyopajāyate |
prāpnoti yādṛśīṃ bādhāṃ karmaṇā svakṛtena yaḥ ||
tasyāpi tādṛśī bādhā jñeyā janmani janmanit |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena prabādhāṃ vivarjayet || iti | (ViDhU 2.107.6–30)
mahābhārate ‘pi |
ye nāstikā niṣkriyāś ca guruśāstrātilaṅghinaḥ |
adharmajñā gatācārās te bhavanti gatāyuṣaḥ ||
kuśīlā bhinnamaryādā nityaṃ saṃkīrṇamaithunāḥ |
alpāyuṣo bavantīha narā nirayagāminaḥ ||
loṣṭamartī tṛṇacchedī nakhakhādī ca yo naraḥ |
nityocchiṣṭaḥ sūcakaś ca nehāyur vindate mahat ||
āyur asya nikṛntāmi prajñām asyādade tathā |
[236]        ya ucchiṣṭaḥ pravadati svādhyāyaṃ cādhigacchati |
yaś cānadhāyakāle ‘pi mohād abhyasate dvijaḥ ||
pratyādityaṃ pratyanalaṃ pratigāṃ ca pratidvijam |
ye mehayanti pathi ca te bhavanti gatāyuṣaḥ || iti |
śivadharmottare |
devadravyopajīvī ca sa bhaved avabāhukaḥ |
devadravyaṃ svayaṃ yena bhuktaṃ yasya sadā bhavet ||
rasajñāvidhuraḥ kubjaḥ kaṇḍūḥ sarvāṅgadukhitaḥ |
devasparśaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā parapratyayakāraṇāt ||
mithyācāraṃ caret paścād budbudāsyo naro bhavet |
devadāsodanaṃ bhuktvā bṛhadgulmī ca vai kramāt ||
sarvāṅgakuṣṭhī timirī naro bhavati pāpataḥ |
dīpaṃ devagṛhād dhṛtvā paṅguś cāndo bhaven naraḥ |
mūrcchī bhramī suradveṣṭā pakṣapātī jvarī bhavet ||
devāvamantā bhavati sarvadā maithune rataḥ |
sadāpahṛtasarvasvaḥ kutsitaḥ kāmamohitaḥ ||
[237] iti | vṛddhagautamaḥ |
devadravyopajīvī hṛdrogī | pratijñāya brāhmaṇasyādānād alpāyuḥ | patnībahutve saty ekārāmaḥ klībaḥ | svāminā dharme niyuktas tadanuṣṭhānāśakto jalodarī | durbalabādhe balavatām upekṣāyām aṅgahīnaḥ | vyavahārapakṣapātī jihvārogī | svayaṃ pravartitadharmānuṣṭhānacchedane pratipanneṣṭaviyogaḥ | svayam agrabhuk śūlarogī | parikṣīṇamitrabandhusvāmisvajanāvamantā paribhūtavṛttiḥ | atithiṃ paśyann aśnataḥ kapālapaṭṭikā | sūryāprakāśe pratiśrayādānād iṣṭaviyogaḥ | chadmanā gurusvāmimitram upacarataḥ prāptārthaparibhrāṃśaḥ | visrambhāpahārī sarvaduḥkhāśrayaḥ | goduḥkhakārī gonāsaḥ | gonirdayaś cipiṭanāsaḥ | pathanāśako nistejaskaḥ sabhāyām | galagaṇḍī mahadadhikṣepī | puṇyākṣepī [238] vakranāsaḥ | śiṣṭacīrṇadharmadūṣakaḥ kekarākṣaḥ | sādhujanadevatapasvidvijagurujñānayogadveṣṭā vakrāsyaḥ | taṭākārāmabhettā netrāṅgahīnaḥ | kṛtaghnaḥ sarvārambhavikalaḥ | parasvābhilāṣī kṣayarogī syāt | corarakakaḥ –
vyavahāreṣv amadhyasthaś cauravṛttiparaś ca yaḥ |
vāṇijyalābho no tasya deśāntaragatasya ca ||
vyādhiḥ syāt prāṇasaṃdeho mṛtis tatraiva pāpinaḥ || iti |
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
akṛtvā khalayajñaṃ tu yas tu dhānyaṃ praveśayet |
marudeśe bhaved vṛkṣaḥ phalapuṣpavivarjitaḥ ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu durātmā karṣako bhavet |
tasyānte mānuṣo bhūtvā kadācit kālaparyayāt ||
daridro vyādhito mūrkho hy akulīnas tu jāyate |
yas tu kṣudreṇa bhāvena snehavicchedakārakaḥ ||
so ‘napatyatvam āpnoti tasya pāpasya kāraṇāt |
na śṛṇvanti guror vākyaṃ vedavākyaṃ ca ye narāḥ ||
sarveṣāṃ caiva nāstikyaṃ tena te yānti mūkatām |
prārthayitvā tu yo gāṃ vai savatsāṃ kṣīrasaṃyutām ||
[239]        ātmanā pibati kṣīraṃ tenāsau yāti cāndhatām ||
prasravaṃ ṣṭhīvanaṃ vahnau kṛtvā sarvāṅgadāhayuk |
udare śūlasaṃyukto jayate manujādhamaḥ || iti |
śivadharmottare |
mukhakṛṣṇavraṇī śvitrī garado mattako bhavet |
jihvāvaktravraṇī hy anyaduḥkhakārī bhaven naraḥ ||
apakāraṃ tu yaḥ kuryād upakartur bhaven naraḥ |
śvāsakāsī jvarī sarvagātrarogī mṛtaprajaḥ ||
mṛtaputro ‘py abandhuḥ syād yo dveṣṭi parasantatim |
paravittavināśāya karoti sa visarpavān ||
akāraṇād dharmapatnītyāgī syāc ca jalodarī |
veśyāṃ kulavadhūṃ kṛtvā vartate yo narādhamaḥ ||
bhaktaṃ bhoktum aśaktaś ca maṇḍapānena jīvati |
pareṣāṃ cetasaḥ kleśakārī bhavati mānavaḥ ||
kṣayī jvarī pramehī ca gulmī dāhī bhagandarī |
rajasvalāntyacaṇḍālapāpiṣṭhapatitaiḥ saha ||
vyavahārī puṣpavatyāṃ bhuktvā kuṣṭhī jvarī bhavet |
anapatyo daridraś ca viśvastasya viṣapradaḥ ||
[240]        niṣiddhavastubhogena mohayed yaḥ parān pumān |
paropadiṣṭajñānena bhaved unmattako ‘pi vā ||
ṛṇaṃ datvādhikāṃ vṛddhiṃ yo gṛhṇātīha mānavaḥ |
sa bhūtvā dhanināṃ vaṃśe vrajed deśāntaraṃ tataḥ ||
vāṇijyalābhas tatrāpi nāsti tasya narasya vai |
amukhyamukhyakārī yaḥ sa jvarī śvāsakāsavān ||
sarvāṅgarogī pāpātmā dveṣṭā mṛtasamāṅgabhāk || iti |
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
mṛṣṭānnena ya ātmānaṃ poṣayanti narādhamāḥ |
bālānāṃ vīkṣyamāṇānām adadann atti nispṛhaḥ ||
na hutaṃ na tapas taptaṃ na dattaṃ kiṃ ca brāhmaṇe |
ātmaiva poṣito yena tat pāpaṃ kathayāmy aham ||
yatra deśe tv anāvṛṣṭir yatra vāyusamudbhavaḥ |
tasmin deśe kṣudhārttāś ca jāyante pāpakarmiṇaḥ || iti |
padmapurāṇe |
yas tu vipratvam utsṛjya kṣatradharmaṃ niṣevate |
brāhmaṇyāt sa paribhraṣṭaḥ kṣatrayonau prajāyate ||
[241]        vaiśyakarmā ca yo vipro lobhamohavyapāśrayaḥ |
sa dvijo vaiśyatām eti śūdrakarma karoti yaḥ ||
svadharmapracyuto vipraḥ sa hi śūdratvam āpnuyāt |
kṣatriyo vā mahābāho vaiśyo vā dharmacāriṇau ||
svāni karmāṇy apākṛtya śūdrakarma niṣevate |
svasthānāc ca paribhraṣṭo jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā yāti tādṛśaḥ |
śūdrānnenodarasthena mriyate yadi putraka ||
brāhmaṇaḥ śūdratām eti nāsti tatra vicāraṇā || iti | (PadPu 5.26.7–13)
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ svavarṇe pāṇisaṃgraham |
akṛtvā tv anyajāpāṇeḥ patanti nṛpa samgrahāt ||
yaś ca yasyās tu hīnāyāḥ kurute pāṇisaṃgraham |
akṛtvā varṇasaṃyogaṃ so ‘pi tadvarṇabhāg bhavet ||
[242] iti |
atha viśeṣeṇa karmavipākaḥ
tatra hiṃsāviśeṣād yoniviśeṣaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
yāṃ yāṃ yoniṃ ca jīvo ‘yaṃ yena yeneha karmaṇā |
kramaśo yāti loke ‘smiṃs tat tat sarvaṃ nibodhata ||
bahūn varṣagaṇān ghorān narakān prāpya tatkṣayāt |
saṃsārān pratipadyante mahāpātakinas tv imān ||
śvaśūkarakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ go’jāvimṛgapakṣiṇām |
caṇḍālapulkasānāṃ ca brahmahā yonim ṛcchati || iti | (MDh 12.53–55)
nandipurāṇe |
narakeṣu krameṇaiva brahmahā pātyate naraḥ |
kalpaṃ kalpaṃ tataś cānte sthāvaraś ca prajāyate ||
tṛṇagulmalatāvallīvīrddrumavibhedataḥ |
tṛṇabhedās tv agaṇitā gulmabhedās tathā matāḥ |
latābhedāś ca niḥsaṃkhyā vallībhedās tathaiva ca ||
vīrudbhedās tv asaṃkhyātā asaṃkhyā drumajātayaḥ |
teṣāṃ ca deśabhedena punaḥ kaṣṭatvam iṣyate ||
[243]        tataḥ kenāpi kālena nānāsthāvarayonitaḥ |
karma bhuktvā krameṇaiva bhavanti ca sarīsṛpāḥ ||
vṛkṣavalkalabhedena deśabhedena vāpy atha |
sarīsṛpādibhedena tatra bhuktvā tu pāpakam ||
mṛgeṣu jāyate martyo bhedair agaṇitais tataḥ |
tatrāpi bhuktvā svaṃ karma jāyate paśuyoniṣu ||
tatrāpi bhautikaṃ bhogaṃ bhuktvānte gotvam āpya tu |
mucyate paśujanmāc ca tato jāyeta mānuṣe ||
tatrāpi niyamo nāsti śvapākādyeṣu jāyate |
viprānteṣu manuṣyeṣu jāyate pātakī naraḥ || iti |
śivadharmottare ‘pi |
evaṃ pāpaviśuddhāś ca sāvaśeṣeṇa karmaṇā |
tataḥ kṣitiṃ samāsādya jāyante dehinaḥ punaḥ ||
tatrānubhūya duḥkhāni jāyante kīṭayoniṣu |
niṣkrāntāḥ kīṭayonibhyaḥ kramāj jāyanti pakṣiṇaḥ ||
saṃkliṣṭāḥ pakṣibhāvena jāyante mṛgayoniṣu |
mṛgaduḥkham atikramya jānyante paśuyoniṣu ||
[244]        paśuyoniṃ samāsādya punar jāyanti mānuṣāḥ |
evaṃ yoniṣu sarvāsu paribhrāmya krameṇa tu ||
kālāntaravaśād yānti mānuṣyam atidurlabham |
uttamaṃ cāpi mānuṣyaṃ prāpyante puṇyagauravāt ||
vicitrā gatayaḥ proktāḥ karmaṇāṃ gurulāghavāt |
tato mānuṣatāṃ prāpya vyādhibhis tatra pīḍyate ||
kuṣṭharogeṇa ghoreṇa brahmahatyāṃ prayojayan | iti |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
aśastraṃ puruṣaṃ hatvā naraḥ saṃjāyate kharaḥ |
kṛmiḥ strīvadhakartā ca bālahā copajāyate ||
vṛṣasya vṛṣaṇau chittvā ṣaṇḍhatvaṃ prāpnuyān naraḥ |
parihṛtya tato bhūyo janmanāmekaviṃśatim ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgeṣu sthitasteyacareṣu ca |
gotraṃ prāpya ca caṇḍālapulakasādi jugupsitam ||
[245]        paṅgvandho badhiraḥ kuṣṭhī yakṣmaṇā ca prapīḍitaḥ |
mukharogākṣirogair vā rogair anyaiś ca bādhyate ||
apasmārī ca bhavati śūdratvaṃ caiva gacchati |
eṣa eva kramo dṛṣṭo gosuvarṇādihāriṇām ||
vidyāpahāriṇāṃ caiva nideśabhrāṃśināṃ gurau || iti | (MārPu 15.18–36)
brahmapurāṇe |
yas tu raudrasamācāraḥ sarvabhūtabhayaṃkaraḥ |
hastābhyāṃ yadi padbhyāṃ vā rajjvā daṇḍena vā punaḥ ||
loṣṭaiḥ stambhair upāyair vā jantūṃs tāḍayate ‘niśam |
hiṃśārthī nikṛtaprajña udvejanakaraḥ sadā ||
[246]        evaṃ śīlasamācāro nirayaṃ pratipadyate |
sa cen manuṣyatāṃ gacched yadi kālaviparyayāt ||
vadhabandhaparikliṣṭaḥ kule jāyeta so ‘dhame |
lokadveṣṭādhamaḥ puṃsāṃ svayaṃ karmakṛtaiḥ phalaiḥ || iti |
atha hiṃsrakarmaśeṣaphalāni
tatra hiṃsrakarmaviśeṣaphalāni śaṅkhalikhitābhyāṃ darśitāni: “ata ūrdhvaṃ bahūni varṣasahasrāṇi yamaviṣaye kumbhīpākarauravādiṣu sthāneṣu duḥkhāny anubhūyeha jātānām imāni lakṣaṇāni bhavanti | tad yathā brahmahā kuṣṭhī goghnaś cāndho ‘nekarogiṇaś ca” iti |
yamaḥ |
aśuddhānāṃ tu pāpānāṃ patitānāṃ ca karmabhiḥ |
uttīrṇānāṃ ca tiryaktvād dehe bhavati lakṣaṇam ||
iha duścaritaiḥ kecit kecit pūrvakṛtais tathā |
prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam ||
dvijaghnaṃ kuṣṭhinaṃ vidyāt kunakhaṃ svarṇahārakam |
vyaṅgāś cādharmiṇaś caiva jāyante prāṇihiṃsakāḥ ||
[247] iti | hārītaḥ: “kunakhī bhrūṇahā | brahmaghnaḥ kuṣṭhī | paraduḥkhadāyino nityāndhāḥ | parārtiratā dīrgharogiṇaḥ | dṛṣṭyapahartāro ‘ndhāḥ | śrotrāpahartāro badhirāḥ | vācopahartāro mūkāḥ | evaṃ hy āha |
yad aṅgaṃ pīḍyate yena tena hīno ‘bhijāyate |
nākṛtvā labhate kiṃcit śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham” || iti | (HārSm 24.73–80)
kaśyapaḥ |
kṣayī syāt kṣetraharaṇe arśavān nṛvadhe ‘pi ca |
jalodarayutaḥ sa syāt svanārīṇāṃ ca hiṃsakaḥ ||
karṇabhettā karṇaśūlī oṣṭharogy oṣṭhanāśakaḥ |
dantapātanakartā ca dantarogī bhavet sadā ||
uraḥstambhād uraḥsthambhī akṣirogy akṣināśakaḥ |
padā tu yad tāḍayati visarpī sa bhaven naraḥ ||
[248]         pīḍām apriyakartā ca dhanurvyādhir dhanurgrahāt |
pādakīlī sa bhavati pādahantā ca yo bhavet ||
sa gadgadī dārayati yo vṛkṣān ativistṛtān |
śūlena śūlī bhavati yas tu hiṃsyād dṛḍhān paśūn |
naktāndhyaṃ jāyate tasya yo gavāṃ nayanadvaye ||
karoti śūlaprakṣepaṃ pūrvabhāvapracoditaḥ || iti | (KāśSm 3.40–46)
vyāso ‘pi |
pitṛhā yāti paiśācyaṃ jātyandho mātṛghātakaḥ |
prāṇyaṅgaṃ mṛgaśāvaṃ ca hatvā vandhyo mṛtaprajāḥ ||
asādhyarogī bālaghnaḥ kṣayī syāt sarvajīvahā |
gātrakṛcchrī vraṇī śvitrī visarpī paraghātakaḥ ||
netrarogī bhavet tasya yo ‘nyeṣāṃ dṛṣṭighātakaḥ |
pareṣāṃ rasanāchedī jihvāsya vraṇavān bhavet ||
[249]        kṛtaghnaḥ sphuṭitākṣo vā tṛtīyajvarasaṃyutaḥ |
cāturthakoṣṇaśītoṣṇajvarī syāt satataṃ jvarī ||
grāme vane vā jantūnāṃ śvabhir yo bhītim arpayet |
bhayodvegakaro ‘nyeṣāṃ pāṣāṇenātha ghātakaḥ ||
talaghātī ca saṃbhūtaḥ kuleṣu dhanināṃ naraḥ |
gatvā deśāntaraṃ teṣāṃ tatra rogo mahān bhavet || iti | (VyāSm 6.17–22)
vṛddhagautamaḥ |
vṛkṣacchettā vṛthā yas tu sa nāḍīvraṇavān bhavet | iti |
vāyupurāṇe |
bhavet sphuṭitapādas tu yo nihanyād vanaspatīn |
prāṇihiṃsāparo jantughātī vā mṛtasantatiḥ ||
mudhe kṛṣṇavraṇī śvitrī vandhyo vā syād visarpavān |
valmīkaṃ tu khanitvā yo hanti vāyubhujo naraḥ ||
gajacarmī jvarī ca syād ājñayā pūrvakarmaṇaḥ |
bhrūṇahā svinnahastaḥ syād grahaṇīvān apatyahā ||
[250] iti | viṣṇudharmottare |
yaṃ kiṃcid ghātayitvā tu prāṇinaṃ bhṛgunandana |
māṃsaṃ bhuktvā tu dharmajña bhavatīha gadāturaḥ || iti | (ViDhU 2.108.4)
iti hiṃsāśeṣakarmaphalāni
athāpeyapānābhakṣyabhakṣaṇakarmaviśeṣād yoniviśeṣaḥ
tatra śivadharmottare |
kṛmikīṭapataṅgānāṃ viḍbhujāṃ caiva pakṣiṇām |
surāpaḥ prāpyate yoniṃ pūrvakarmānusārataḥ || iti |
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
madyavyasanino ye ca ye madyaṃ sevayanti ca |
śālāṃ tu kārayed yas tu madyapānaṃ dadāti yaḥ ||
madyavikrayiṇo ye ca madyaṃ ye krīṇayanti ca |
pibanti ye narā devi sarve te samabhāginaḥ ||
te śauṇḍikā vā caṇḍālā lubdhakā matsyaghātakāḥ |
dāsatvenaiva suśroṇi jāyante puruṣādhamāḥ ||
[251]        tiryañco ‘pi smutpannā yathā mṛtyum upāgatāḥ |
madyapānaratā ye ca śṛṇu teṣāṃ gatiṃ śubhe ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi kṛmir bhūtvā mahītale |
kṛmiyonikṣaye bhūte pāpaṃ tu kathayāmy aham ||
śvānayoniśataṃ gacchej jambuke pañcaviṃśatim |
madyapānaratā ye tu kāṃ gatiṃ yānti suvrate ||
śūdrasya cedṛśaṃ pāpaṃ madyapānarastasya ca |
viprasya kathayiṣyāmi madyapasya durātmanaḥ ||
kāko bhūtvā ciraṃ devi viṣṭhābhakṣas tu jīvati |
varṣāṇāṃ dvādaśe lakṣye sa jīvet pāpakarmakṛt ||
sve māṃse paramāṃsena yo dehī vṛddhim icchati |
tasya yoniśataṃ gatvā śarīraṃ vyādhitaṃ bhavet ||
yāsu yoniṣu jāyante narā māṃsasya bhakṣakāḥ|
vyādhirogaśatair yuktā jāyante tāsu karmaṇā ||
[252] iti | manuḥ |
kṛmikīṭapataṅgānāṃ viḍbhujāṃ caiva pakṣiṇām |
hiṃsrāṇāṃ caiva sattvānāṃ surāpo brāhmaṇo vrajet || iti | (MDh 12.56)
viṣṇur api: “abhojyānāṃ bhonaje abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe ca kṛmiḥ” iti (ViDh 44.11) | vṛddhagautamaḥ |
vipravṛttivilopena durduraḥ saptajanmasu |
niṣiddhānnaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte kamaṭhaḥ so ‘bhijāyate ||
kīṭāśī śītalānnāśī matsyāśī yācitānnabhuk |
āmantraṇaṃ vinā bhuktvā parānnaṃ kākatāṃ vrajet || iti |
athāpeyapānādikarmaśeṣaphalāni
tatra hārītaḥ: “surāpo baddhijihvaḥ | abhakṣyabhakṣaḥ śyāvadantaḥ | (HārSm 24.68–69) | [253] iti | skānde revākhaṇḍe |
pāpaśeṣāt tu bhavati surāpaḥ śyāvadantakaḥ | iti |
śivadharmottare: “abhakṣyabhakṣī gaṇḍamālī” iti | śaṅkhalikhitau: “abhakṣyabhakṣī gaṇḍamālī” iti | kāśyapaḥ: “surāpo lolajihvaḥ” iti (KāśSm 13.19) |
atha steyakarmaviśeṣād yoniviśeṣāḥ
tatra manuḥ |
lūtāhisaraṭhānāṃ ca tiraścām cāmbucāriṇām |
hiṃsrāṇāṃ ca piśācānāṃ steno vipraḥ sahasraśaḥ ||
saṃyogaṃ patitair gatvā parasyaiva ca yoṣitam |
apahṛtya ca viprasvaṃ bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ ||
[254]        maṇimuktāpravālāni hṛtvā lobhena mānavaḥ |
vividhāni ca ratnāni jāyate lohakartṛṣu ||
dhānyaṃ hṛvā bhavaty ākhuḥ kāṃsyaṃ haṃso jalaṃ plavaḥ |
madhu daṃśaḥ payaḥ kāko rasaṃ śvā nakulo ghṛtam ||
māṃsaṃ gṛdhro vasāṃ madgus tailaṃ tailapakaḥ khagaḥ |
cīrīvākas tu lavaṇaṃ balākā śakunir dadhi ||
kauśeyaṃ tittirir hṛtvā kṣaumaṃ hṛtvā tu darduraḥ |
kārpāsatāntavaṃ krauñco godhā gāṃ vāggudo guḍam ||
cuccundarī śubhān gandhān patraṃ śākaṃ tu barhiṇaḥ |
śvāvit kṛtānnaṃ vividham akṛtānnaṃ tu śalyakaḥ ||
bako bhavati hṛtvāgniṃ gṛhakārī hy apaskaram |
raktāni hṛtvā vāsāṃsi jāyate jīvajīvakaḥ ||
vṛko mṛgebhaṃ vyāghro ‘śvaṃ phalapuṣpaṃ tu markaṭaḥ |
strīm ṛkṣas tokako vāri yānāny uṣṭraḥ paśūn ajaḥ ||
yad vā tad vā paradravyam apahṛtya blān naraḥ |
[255]        avaśyaṃ yāti tiryaktvaṃ jagdhvā caivāhutaṃ haviḥ ||
striyo ‘py etena kalpena hṛtvā doṣam avāpnuyuḥ |
eteṣām eva jantūnāṃ bhāryātvam upayānti tāḥ || iti | (MDh 12.57–69)
yājñavalkyaḥ |
hīnajātau prajāyeta pararatnāpahārakaḥ |
patraśākaṃ śikhī hṛtvā gandhāñ cucchindarī śubhān ||
mūṣako dhānyahārī syād yānam uṣṭraḥ phalaṃ kapiḥ |
jalaṃ plavaḥ payaḥ kāko gṛhakārī hy upaskaram ||
madhu daṃśaḥ palaṃ gṛdhro gāṃ godhāgniṃ bakas tathā |
śvitrī vastraṃ śvā rasaṃ ca cīrī lavaṇahārakaḥ ||
pradarśanārtham etat tu mayoktaṃ steyakarmaṇi |
dravyaprakārā hi yathā tathaiva prāṇijātayaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.213–16)
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
nyāsāpahartā narakād vimukto jāyate kṛmiḥ |
dhānyaṃ yavāṃs tilān māṣān kulitthān sarṣapāñ caṇān ||
[256]        sasyāny anyāni vā hṛtvā mohāj jantur acetanaḥ |
saṃjāyate mahāvaktro mūṣako vajrasaṃnibhaḥ ||
bhojanaṃ corayitvā tu makṣikā jāyate naraḥ |
hṛtvā dugdhaṃ tu mārjāro jāyate narakāc cyutaḥ |
tilapiṇyākasaṃmiśram annaṃ hṛtvā tu mūṣakaḥ ||
ghṛtaṃ hṛtvā tu nakulaḥ kāko muhur ajāmiṣam |
śyeno mārgāmiṣaṃ hṛtvā cīrī lavaṇahārakaḥ ||
corayitvā payaś cāpi balākā saṃprajāyate |
yas tu corayate tailaṃ tailapāyī sa jāyate |
madhu hṛtvā naro daṃśo ghṛtaṃ hṛtvā pipīlikā ||
corayitvā tu niṣpāvān jāyate gṛhagolakaḥ |
āsanaṃ corayitvā tu tittiratvam avāpnuyāt ||
ayo hṛtvā tu pāpātmā vāyasaḥ saṃprajāyate |
[257]        hṛte kāṃsye ca hārītaḥ kapoto raupyaje hṛte ||
jīvañjīvakatāṃ yāti raktavastrāpahārakaḥ ||
cucchundarī śubhān gandhān sasyaṃ hṛtvā śaśo bhavet |
ṣaṇḍhaḥ palālaharaṇe kāṣṭhahṛt ghuṇakīṭakaḥ ||
puṣœāpahṛd daridraś ca paṅgur yānāpahārakaḥ |
śākahartā ca hārītas toyahartā ca cātakaḥ ||
bhūhartā narakān gatvā rauravādīn sudāruṇān |
tṛṇagulmalathāvallītvaksāratarutāṃ kramāt || iti | (MārPu 15.6–32)
gautamaḥ |
anṛtavāk skhalitajihvo muhur muhuḥ saṃlagnavāk | [258] jalodaro dāratyāgī | kūṭasākṣī ślīpadī ucchūnajaṅghācaraṇaḥ | vivāhavighnakartā chinnoṣṭhaḥ | avagūraṇaś chinnahastaḥ | mātṛdhno ‘ndhaḥ | snuṣāgāmī vātavṛṣaṇaḥ | catuṣpathe viṇmūtravisarjane mūtrakṛcchraḥ | kanyādūṣakaḥ ṣaṇḍhaḥ | paryuṣitabhojī kṛmiḥ | īrṣyālur maśakaḥ | pitror vivadamāno ‘pasmārī | nyāsāpahāry anapatyaḥ | ratnāpahārī atyantadaridraḥ | vidyāvikrayī puruṣamṛgaḥ | vedavikrayī dvīpī | bahuyājako jalaplavaḥ | ayājyayājako varāhaḥ | animantritabhojī vāyasaḥ | mṛṣṭaikabhojī vānaraḥ | itas tato ‘śnan mārjāraḥ | kakṣavanadāhī khadyotaḥ | dārakācāryo mukhavigandhaḥ | adattādāyī balīvardaḥ | matsarī bhramaraḥ | agnyutsādī maṇḍalakuṣṭhī | śūdrāvāryaḥ śvapākaḥ | gohartā sarpaḥ | snehāpahārī kṣayī | annāpahāry ajīrṇī | jñānāpahārī [259] mūkaḥ | jalāpahārī matsyaḥ | kṣīrahārī balākaḥ | paradravyāpahārī parapreṣyaḥ | iti | (GDh 20.4–38 ??)
śaṅkhalikhitau |
veśyāpahārī adrisarpaḥ | parajalāpahārī śiśumāraḥ | sasyāpahārī kapotaḥ | kavyāpahārī vāyasaḥ | tāmrāpahārī valgulaḥ | apūpāpahārī pretaḥ | śāstrāpahārī jaḍaḥ | chatrāpahārī kāraṇḍavaḥ | gandhāpahārī pataṅgaḥ | dhvajāpahārī kṛkalāsaḥ | śākāpahārī paśuḥ | śayyāpahāry aśayanaḥ | puṣpāpahārī durgandhī | phalāpahāry aphalī | dīpāpahārī kauśikaḥ | bhūmyapahārī nakulāhibiḍālākhūnām anyatamaḥ iti |
[260]                yad vā tad vāpi pārakyaṃ svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu |
hṛtvā vai yonim āpnoti tat tad bhogānurūpataḥ || iti |
atha steyakarmaśeṣaphalāni
tatra viṣṇudharmottare |
maṇimuktāpravālāni ratnāni vividhāni ca |
apahṛtya narā rāma jāyante hemakartṛṣu ||
yad vā tad vā paradravyam apahṛtya balān naraḥ |
prāpnoti bhṛtuśārdūla mānuṣye bhāravāhitām ||
dhānyacoro ‘ṅgahīnaḥ syād atyaṅgo miśrahṛd bhavet |
vyādhitaś cānnahartā syān mūko vāgapahārakaḥ ||
vastrāpahārī śvitrī syāt paṅguś cāśvāpahārakaḥ |
andho dīpāpahārī syāt kāṇo nirvāpako bhavet ||
daridraḥ paravittaghnaś conmattaś ca tathāgnidaḥ ||
paṅgur eva tathā jñeyaḥ sarvyānāpahārakaḥ |
[261]        andhaś ca jāyate loke gāṃ hṛtvā ca narādhamaḥ ||
vastrabhojyāpahartāro tathā nagnabubhukṣitau |
ratnāpahārī bhavati tathā kuṣṭhī narādhamaḥ || iti | (ViDhU 2.107.2–9)
vāyupurāṇe |
vātavyādhiyutaḥ steyī tathā kuṣṭhī naro bhavet |
brāhmaṇasvarṇahārī ca pramehī jāyate naraḥ ||
prasvinnapāṇipādaḥ syād āhared yo rasādikam |
annacoro bhaved yas tu so ‘kasmāj jāyate kṛśaḥ ||
svaropaghātī vākyaṃ ca hṛtvā martyaḥ prajāyate || iti | (VāyPu 2.31.25–27)
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde |
dhānyacauras tu hīnāṅgas tṛṇahartā tu vā punaḥ |
paradravyāpahārī ca bhṛtyānāṃ vṛttijīvakaḥ ||
annaṃ kṣetraṃ gṛhaṃ vāpi paradravyaṃ haranti ye |
suvarṇamaṇimuktāni vastrāṇy ābharaṇāni ca ||
[262]        āsanāni vicitrāṇi dhanadhānyam upaskaram |
ye haranti narā nityaṃ paradravyāṇi mohitāḥ ||
ihalokamṛtānāṃ ca teṣām eṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
nagnā dīnāḥ kṣudhārtāś ca kucailā duḥkhitāḥ sadā ||
bandhubhiḥ paribhūtāś ca bhogyabhogavivarjitāḥ |
tāṃs tu te prārthayantīha dāsavṛttim upāgatāḥ ||
ye haranti ca tūryāṇi vādyāni vividhāni ca |
tena pāpena te devi tiryaktvaṃ yānti mānavāḥ ||
hṛtvānaddhādivādyāni jhallarīpaṭahāni ca |
tena pāpena te mūḍhā bādhiryaṃ yānti mānavāḥ ||
kāṃsyakaṃ murajaṃ vaṃśaṃ vīṇāṃ tumburukaṃ tathā |
ye haranti ca pāpena piśācās te bhavanti ca ||
paṭahaṃ lepanaṃ caiva tathā saṃpuṭikāś ca yet |
[263]        haranti puṣkaraṃ ye tu tena te yānti mūkatām ||
prāsādeṣu ca ye devi harante dīpakān narāḥ |
gṛheṣu caiva viprāṇāṃ tena te yānti cāndhatām || iti |
śaṅkhalikitau |
suvarṇasteyī kunakhī | vastrāpahārī śvitrī | hiraṇyāpahārī dadruṇaḥ | taijasāpahārī maṇḍalī | snehāpahārī kṣayāmayī | ajīrṇavān annāpahārī | vāgapahārī mūkaḥ | gṛdhro devadravyopajīvakaḥ | pāṇḍurogī dīpahārī | brāhmaṇavṛttighnaś ca daridraḥ | karmaphalaviśeṣeṇa kubjaṣaṇḍhapaṅgudaridrāś ca jāyante | iti |
yamaḥ |
dravyauṣadhāpahartā ca dīrgharogitvam āpnuyāt |
dhānyacoro vraṇī ca syāt kāsī bhaktāpahārakaḥ ||
galagaṇḍī gaṇadravyahartā bhavati mānavaḥ |
[264]        vidhṛtir balahartā ca śyeno māṃsāpahārakaḥ || iti | (YaSm 5.3–5)
skānde revākhaṇḍe |
mānakūṭaṃ tulākūṭaṃ kūṭasākṣyaṃ vadanti ye |
narake te ‘ndhatāmisre prapacyante narādhipa ||
śatasāhasrikaṃ kālam uṣītvā tatra te narāḥ |
iha śatrugṛhe baddhā bhramante kālam īpsitam ||
kunakhī svarṇaharaṇān mūkaḥ pustakahārakaḥ |
phalāny āharato ‘patyaṃ mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
athāgamyāgamanakarmaviśeṣād yoniviśeṣaḥ
tatra skānde revākhaṇḍe |
gurudārābhimarśī ca kṛkalāso bhaved dhruvam |
rājñīgāmī bhaved uṣṭro vṛściko vṛṣalīpatiḥ ||
[265] iti | gautamaḥ: “cāṇḍālīpulkasīgamane ‘jagaraḥ | pravrajitāgamane marupiśācaḥ | śūdrāgamane dīrghakīṭaḥ | savarṇābhigāmī daridraḥ | gogāmī maṇḍūkaḥ” iti |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
paradārābhigamane raktadroṇas tu jāyate |
śvasṛgālebhagṛdhreṣu kramāj jātiṣu jāyate ||
bhrātṛbhāryā ca durbuddhir yo dharṣayati pāpakṛt |
puṃskokilatvam āpnoti sa pāpo narakāc cyutaḥ ||
sakhibhāryāṃ guror bhāryāṃ rājabhāryāṃ ca pāpakṛt |
pradharṣayitvā kāmātmā sūkaro jāyate naraḥ ||
śūdraś ca brāhmaṇīṃ gatvā kṛmiyonau prajāyate |
tasyām apatyam utpādya kāṣṭḥāntaḥ kīṭako bhavet ||
sūkaraḥ kṛmiko madguś caṇḍālaś ca prajāyate || (Mārœu 15.9–16)
[266] iti | brahmapurāṇe |
sakhibhāryāṃ guror bhāyāṃ rājabhāryāṃ tathaiva ca |
pradharṣayati kāmād yo mṛto jāyeta sūkaraḥ ||
sūkaraḥ pañcavarṣāṇi daśa varṣāṇi vai vṛkaḥ |
pipīlikā tu māsāṃs trīn kīṭaḥ syān māsam eva tu ||
etān āsādya saṃsārān kṛmiyonau prajāyate |
tatra jīvati māsāṃś ca kṛmiyonau caturdaś ||
tato ‘dhramakṣayaṃ kṛtvā tato jāyeta vai vṛkaḥ |
śvā sṛgālas tato gṛdhro vyālaḥ kāko bakas tathā ||
bhrātṛbhāryāṃ tu pāpātmā yo rocayati mohitaḥ |
kokilatvam avāpnoti so ‘pi saṃvatsaraṃ dvijaḥ ||
manasāpi guror bhāryāṃ yaḥ śiṣyo yāti pāpakṛt |
ugrān sa yāti saṃsārān adharmeṇeha cetasā ||
śvayonau tu sa saṃbhūtas trīṇi varṣāṇi jīvati |
tatrāpi nirayaṃ prāptaḥ kṛmiyonau prajāyate ||
[267]         kṛmibhāvam anuprāpya varṣam ekaṃ tu jīvati |
tatas tu nirayaṃ prāpto brahmayonau prajāyate ||
vṛṣalo brāhmaṇīṃ gatvā kṛmiyonau prajāyate |
tataḥ saṃprāpya nidhanaṃ jāyate sūkaraḥ punaḥ ||
śvā tato jāyate mūḍhaḥ karmaṇā tena bho dvijāḥ ||
śvā bhūtvā kṛkalāsas tu jāyate mānavas tataḥ |
tatrāpatyaṃ samutpādya tato jāyeta mūṣakaḥ || iti | (BrPu 217.66–80)
nāradapurāṇe |
cintayed vīkṣayed vāpi jananīṃ vā sutāṃ ca vā |
vadhūṃ vā bhrātṛdārān vā gurubhāryāṃ nṛpastriyam ||
[268]        sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ saṃcintya śvapacīm api |
dṛṣṭvā hi pramadā hy etā yaḥ kṣobhaṃ vrajate naraḥ ||
tasya puṇyaṃ kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ vṛthā bhavati nānyathā |
puṇyasya saṃkṣayāt pāpī pāṣāṇaś ca bhaved dhruvam || iti |
vyabhicāriṇīm prati varāhapurāṇe |
tataḥ śunī bhavet paścāt sūkarī ca tataḥ param |
karmakṣaye tataḥ paścān mānuṣī kutsitā bhavet ||
na ca saukhyam avāpnoti tena duḥkhena duḥkhitā | iti | (VāmPu 202.71–72)
tṛṇagulmalatānāṃ ca kravyādadaṃṣṭriṇām api |
krūrakarmaratānāṃ ca kramaśo gurutalpagaḥ ||
hiṃsrā bhavanti kravyādāḥ kramaśo ‘bhakṣyabhakṣiṇaḥ |
parasparādino matsyāḥ pretyāntyastrīniṣevakāḥ || (VāmPu 202.30–31)
[269] iti | agamyāgamanakarmaviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣaḥ padmapurāṇe |
udaravyādhiyuktas tu tathā gurvaṅganāgame |
ācāryabhāryāgamane bhagandarayuto bhavet ||
tathā pāpena mahatā gulmī syād antyajāgame || iti |
viṣṇudharmottare |
ṣaṇḍhaś ca jāyate loke dārātikramakārakaḥ |
parapuṃsi prasaktā ca tasya bhāryā tathā bhavet ||
kuṣṭhī bhavati dharmajña apahṛtya tathā striyam |
liṅgavyādhim avāpnoti viyonau maithune rataḥ ||
gāṃ gatvā tu dvijaśreṣṭha liṅganāśam avāpnuyāt |
dṛṣṭvā parastriyaṃ nagnāṃ naro duṣṭena cetasā ||
jātyandhatvam avāpnoti paraloke na saṃśayaḥ |
avakīrṇī ca bhavati liṅgavyādhisamanvitaḥ ||
prakīrṇamaithuno jantuḥ klībo bhavati vai dvija || iti | (ViDhU 2.70.41–46)
śaṅkhalikhitau: “parvamaithunī pramehī | puruṣavyādhipīḍitaḥ | paradāragāmy aguptadāraḥ | goṣv avakīrṇī duścarmā | [270] kharābhigāmī mūtramehī | sagotrābhigāmī madyapastrīgāmī ca ślīpadī | mātṛpitṛbhaginīgāmy abījī | svadārād anyatra dīvyataḥ keśālpatvam | gurutalpago duścarmā | caṇḍālyavakīrṇī kanyādūṣī sagotrābhigāmy abījī | dharmapatnyām āsye pravartakaḥ khalvāṭaḥ | mātṛbhaginīgāmy avakīrṇī ślīpadī vadhrī madhumehī ca” iti | vṛddhabaudhāyanaḥ |
gāṃ gatvā madhumehī syān mūtrakṛcchrī catuṣpadam |
madhumehi kharāśvādigāmī bhavati mānuṣaḥ ||
savarṇāgamane raktaḥ pitṛhā pāṇḍurogy atha |
antyajāgamane martyo jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
vṛddhagautamaḥ |
dharmapatnī guror gatvā vṛściko bhujagas tathā |
pakṣidaṃṣṭrimṛgavyālakravyādo ‘pi tato ‘sakṛt ||
tathā mānuṣyatāṃ prāptaḥ kṣayī kuṣṭhī ca vā bhavet |
mahodarī pramehī ca duścarmāpi bhagandarī ||
jvarātisārī dāhī ca pādadāhī kṛmivraṇī |
gatvā tu mātṛbhaginīṃ dāreṣu madhumehavān ||
[271]        kanyāgāmy aśmarī śūlī pramehī mūtrakṛcchry api |
kanyāṃ ca gotrajāṃ gatvā bāhuhīno bhaven naraḥ ||
sagotrāṃ ca savarṇāṃ ca gatvā syāt ślīpadī naraḥ |
mātaraṃ ca svasāraṃ ca gatvā ślīpady ajīvakaḥ ||
anasthiprāṇipādaḥ syāt śvaśrūgaḥ sakhimbhāryagaḥ |
mūtrakṛcchrī tathājihvī snuṣāgo muṣkavṛddhimān ||
rajasvalābhigāmī ca caṇḍālīgamane rataḥ |
dāhajvarī bhaven martyaḥ antyajanmasu gulmavān ||
gatvā tapasvinīṃ nārīṃgurviṇīṃ mūtrakṛcchry api |
syād āmayāvī bhavati vidhavāgo ‘śmarīyutaḥ ||
pramehī mūtrakṛcchrī ca śūlī martyo ‘bhijāyate |
paradārābhigāmī ca pramehī gurudāragaḥ ||
dāsīgāmī krūrakarmā sadā kāmarato bhavet |
svajātiparanārīgaḥ kuṣṭhī martyo ‘bhijāyate ||
vaktramaithunakartā ca khalvāṭo jāyate naraḥ |
gatvā tu mahiṣīṃ rājñaḥ ṣaṇḍho bhavati mānavaḥ ||
[272]        netrarogī bhavet prekṣya nitarāṃ parayoṣitaḥ |
bahutve yoṣitām ekapatnīgaḥ khañjatāṃ vrajet ||
svadārebhyo ‘nyato dīvyann alpakeśo bhaven naraḥ |
puṃścalyo yoṣito bālaraṇḍāḥ syur badhirās tathā ||
dāsyo vā dīrgharogiṇyo dṛṣṭihīnā bhavanti tāḥ || iti |
brahmapurāṇe |
paradāreṣu ya cāpi cakṣur draṣṭuṃ prayuñjate |
tena duṣṭasvabhāvena klībatvam upayānti vai ||
paśūṃś ca ye vivrajanti ye caiva gurutalpagāḥ |
prakīrṇamaithunā ye ca klībā jāyanti te narāḥ || iti | (BrPu 217.64–65)
ted evaṃ kānicic chāstrāṇy udāhṛtya kiyān api karmavipāko nirūpitaḥ | anayaiva diśā vipākāntarāṇy unneyāni | na hi niḥśeṣeṇa kenacid udāhartuṃ śakyante | tad uktaṃ viṣṇudharmottare |
uktās tavemās tu mayā tiraścāṃ
yā yonayo yāni ca lakṣaṇni |
śakyāni kārtsnyena mayā na vaktuṃ
yā yonayaś caiva narādhamānām || (ViDhU 2.72.67)
[273] iti | aśaktiś ca karmānantyāt | yady apy atipātakādīni prakīrṇakāntāni pāpāni daśavidhatvena viṣṇunā saṅkalitāni tathāpy atipātakādijātyupādhāv eva tatsaṅkalanam | pāpavyaktīnāṃ tu svarūpato deśakālakartravasthādibhedāc ca neyattā | yadā karmaṇām eveyattā nāsti tadā tadvipākānām iyattā dūrāpāstā | ekaikasya karmaṇo bahuvidhavipākavattvasmaraṇāt | udāhṛtāś ca bahuvipākābhidhāyikāḥ śvasūkaretyādismṛtayaḥ | tathāpi teṣāṃ niḥśeṣakathanaṃ manasāpi cintayitum aśakyam | ato diṅmātraṃ pradarśitam |
etac ca vipākābhidhānaṃ saṃceṣyamāṇebhyaḥ puruṣaṃ nivartayat saṃciteṣu codvegaṃ janayan niṣedheṣu prāyaścittavidhiṣu cārthavādatayānvetīti pūrvam abhihitam | tatra niṣedhā ācārakāṇḍe prapañcitāḥ | prāyaścittavidhayaś ca prāyaścittakāṇḍe | tāni ca prāyaścittāni saṃcitaviṣayāṇi | prārabdhe tu vicāraṇīyam | kiṃ prāyaścittam asti uta neti | tatra tāvad astīti kecid āhuḥ |
udāharanti ca vacanam –
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vyādhirūpeṇa bādhate |
tacchāntir auṣadhair dānair japahomārcanādibhiḥ ||
[274] iti |
nanu – naitad yuktisaham | tathā hi, niṣiddhasya karmaṇaḥ prāyaścittanivartanīyatvam | yadā phalam ārabdhaṃ tadaiva karmatvadaśām atītya phalatvadaśāyāṃ praviṣṭam | na ca phalasya prāyaścittaṃ kvacit prasiddham asti |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, phaltvadaśāyām api karmatvasyātyāntikanivṛttyabhāvāt | anyathā pāpakarmaṇaḥ phalam iti vyavahārānupapatteḥ | atha vā prāyaścittasadasadbhāvaḥ śāstraikasamadhigamyaḥ | śāstraṃ cet phalasyāpi prāyaścittaṃ brūte kiṃ tava chidyate | udāhṛtaṃ ca śāstram “tacchāntir auṣadhaiḥ” ityādi | tasmād ārabdhaphalasyāpy asty eva prāyaścittam |
apare punar manyante | nāsty evārabdhaphalasya prāyaścittam | kunakhitvādinivṛttyadarśanāt | yady ārabdhaphalaṃ prāyaścittān nivartate [275] tadā naisargikam api kunakhitvādikaṃ prāyaścittān nivarteta | na tv evam upalabhāmahe | yathā muktasyeṣor lakṣyavedho ‘pratisamādheyaḥ tathā prārabdhaphalaṃ na pratisamādhātuṃ śakyam | etad evābhipretya bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ sūtrayāmāsa: “anārabdhakārya eva tu pūrve tadavadheḥ” iti (BrSū 4.1.15) | asyāyam arthaḥ – ye sukṛtaduṣkṛte tattvajñānanivartyatvena pūrvayor adhikaraṇayoḥ prakṛte, te anārabdhakārye eva | na tv ārabdhaphalayoḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛtayor nivṛttiḥ | kutaḥ | tadavadheḥ | “tasya tāvad eva ciram” (ChUp 6.14.2) iti śrutāv utpannajñānasyāpi mokṣaṃ prati śarīrapātāvadhiśravanād iti | evaṃ – tarhi tacchāntir ityādeḥ kā gatiḥ – iti cet,
ucyate – na hy atra prāyaścittam abhidhīyate, tatprakaraṇe pāṭhābhāvāt | cikitsāprakaraṇe hi idaṃ paṭhitam | tathā ca cikitvā yathā [276] roganivṛtter dṛṣṭa upāyaḥ tathā dānādir apy adṛṣṭa upāyaḥ | tathā sati cikitsāvad dānāder nātra prāyaścittatvam | kiṃ tu roganivartakaṃ kiṃcit prabalaṃ sakṛtāpūrvam utpādayanti dānādayaḥ | tac ca sukṛtapravṛttāny api phalāni pratibadhya svaphalaṃ prayacchati | pratibaddhaś ca phalaśeṣaḥ svapnādau janmāntare vopabhujyate | etat sarvam upapurāṇe parāśareṇa prapañcitam |
śarīrārambhakaṃ karma yogino ‘yogini ‘pi ca |
vinā phalopabhogena naiva naśyaty asaṃśayam ||
vartamānaśarīreṇa saṃpannaṃ karma dehinaḥ |
iha vāmutra vājñasya dadāti svaphalaṃ śuka ||
ihaiva phaladaṃ karma prārabdhaṃ pratibadhya ca |
phalaṃ dadāti svapne vā jāgratkāle ‘tha vānagha ||
nivṛttapratibandhaṃ tu prārabdhaṃ karma sattama |
niruddhāṃśaphalaṃ svapne dadāti sthiram uttamam ||
niruddhāṃśasamo bhogo yathodbhūtaś ca jāgrati |
tathā niruddhaṃ tenaiva saha karma dadāti vai ||
evaṃ niruddhabhogaṃ tu svapne jāgrati vā phalam |
[277]        ārabdhasyānuguṇyena bhuṅkte dehī na saṃśayaḥ ||
atyutkaṭair ihatyais tu puṇyapāpaiḥ śarīrabhṛt |
prārabdhaṃ karma vicchidya bhuṅkte tattatphalaṃ budhaḥ ||
prārabdhaśeṣaṃ vicchinnaṃ punar dehāntareṇa tu |
bhuṅkte dehī tato bhūṅkte tal laṅghayati kaḥ pumān ||
avaśyam anubhoktavyaṃ yugapad vā krameṇa vā |
śvasūkarakharoṣṭrādiviruddhair vigrahair api |
bhoktavyasya phalasyaiva śuka karmāṇi ca kramāt ||
kāraṇāni bhavanīha cakravat parivartane |
ārabhante krameṇaiva teṣāṃ nāśas tu bhogataḥ ||
saṃciteṣu samasteṣu prabalaṃ karma dehinaḥ |
phalam ārabhate deham api tatsādhanaṃ budhaḥ ||
durghaṭaṃ sakalaṃ śaktir māyā māheśvarī jaḍā |
ghaṭayaty atra saṃdeho nāsti sā tādṛśī gatiḥ || iti |
prabalena karmaṇā prārabdhaphalasya pratibandhāṅgīkāre naisargikāḥ kunakhitvādayo ‘pi kvacit pratibadhyeran – iti cet, [278] pratibadhyantāṃ nāma | yatra pratibandho na dṛśyate tatra daurbalyaṃ kalpanīyam | prabalasya tu varaśāpābhyāṃ janmānyathābhāvo ‘pi kvacit smaryate | tathā ca skandapurāṇe vyāghra īśvareṇa gaṇeśvaratvaṃ prāpta ity abhihitam | yadā jātasya dehasyāpi prabalena nimittenānyathābhāvo ‘sti tadā kim u vaktavyaṃ kaunakhyāder anyathābāva iti |
evaṃ ca sati rogaśāntyādipratipādakāni sarvāṇi śāstrāṇi prārabdhabhogāvirodhenānugṛhītāni bhaviṣyanti | rogaśāntiś [279] cauṣadhādisādhyā | tatrauṣadhāny āyurvedaprasiddhāni | dānāni ca baudhāyanakalpa-brahmāṇḍapurāṇādibhyo ‘vagantavyāni | japas tu śaunakādiproktaṛgvidhānasāmavidhānādau bahudhā prapañcitaḥ | tathā ca ṛgvidhāne smaryate |
ṛtasyeti japet sūktaṃ tryayutaṃ viṣṇumandire |
aśvatthe māghamāse ca mūtrarogo vinaśyati ||
vayo śukro japet sūktam aśvatthe tryayutaṃ tu cet |
nānāvidho vātarogo vinaśyati na saṃśayaḥ ||
vāruṇaṃ vā japet sūktam ayutaṃ dhātrimūlake |
tāpādivyādhināśe tu sukhī paścāt sthito bhuvi ||
kadrudrāya japed sūktaṃ śītajvaravināśanam |
liṅgaṃ spṛṣṭvāyutaṃ dhīmān ārogyaṃ śīghratas tathā ||
imā rudrāya tavase japet sūktaṃ śivālaye |
uṣṇajvaravināśāya ayutaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
[280]        imāṃ rudrāya sūktaṃ tu japec cec chivamandire |
kāsaśūlādirogaghnam ayutaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
īṅkhayantī japet sūktaṃ lakṣaṃ bilve śive ‘pi vā |
kuṣṭhanāśo bhavec chīghraṃ māghamāse na saṃśayaḥ ||
haṃsamantraṃ japed dhīmān tryayutaṃ viṣṇumandire |
arghyaṃ dadyāt kuṣṭharogī suvarṇasamatām iyāt ||
japec citraṃ devamantraṃ śirorogo vinaśyati |
āyurvṛddhyādikaṃ paścād ayutaṃ viṣṇumandire ||
amīvahā svaprayuktaṃ japet sūktaṃ śivālaye |
tryayutaṃ cet kuṣṭharogī svarṇavarṇaṃ prayāti saḥ ||
mitrasyeti japet sūktam apasmāro vinaśyati |
mahāpātakayukto ‘pi nīrogo bhūtale sthitaḥ ||
ud asau kukṣiśūlī tu japet sūktaṃ śivālaye |
ayutaṃ māghamāse tu mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
[281]        sūryo no divaḥ sūktaṃ pratyahaṃ prajaped daśa ||
badaryāṃ ca trivarṣaṃ tu kṣayādivyādhināśanam || iti | (ṚgVi 101–51)
ta ete japā ṛgvidhāne śaunakena proktāḥ | homaṃ ca tatra sa evāha |
tataś ca citramantreṇa homaṃ kuryāt sahasrakam |
arkeṇa trimadhuṃ kṛtvā śirorogo vinaśyati ||
saptamyāṃ raviyuktāyāṃ jalamadhye yadā daśa |
karavīrais tu gāyatryā homaṃ kuryāt sahasrakam ||
vyādhināśo bhavec chīghraṃ mṛtyuṃ jayati saḥ pumān || iti | (ṚgVi 169–71)
baudhāyano ‘pi |
asādhyavyādhinā grasta ugreṇa prāṇahāriṇā |
ā te rudreti sūktena pratyṛcaṃ vāgyataḥ śuciḥ ||
pūrvam ājyāhutīr hutvā upasthāya ca śaṅkaram |
haviḥśeṣeṇa varteta ekottaram atandritaḥ ||
pūrṇe māse jayen mṛtyuṃ rogebhyaś ca vimucyate |
homakarmaṇy aśaktānāṃ japas taddviguṇo bhavet ||
[282] iti | taittirīyakaśākhāyāṃ ca dvitīyakāṇḍe roganivṛttyarthā homā bahudhopavarṇitāḥ |
tatra tāvat prathamapraśne kāmyapaśuprakaraṇe prathmānuvāke “vāyavyaṃ śvetam ālabheta” (TS 2,1,1,1) iti śvetapaśvālambhanānuvṛttau śrūyate “vāyave niyutvata ālabheta jyogāmayāvī” (TS 2.1.1.3) iti | jyogāmayāvī dīrgharogī | vāyor aśvā niyutaḥ tadvate niyutvate | phalaṃ ca vākyaśeṣe śrutam: “sa evāsmin prāṇāpānau dadhāty uta yadītāsur bhavati jīvaty eva” iti (TS 2.1.1.4) | sa eva niyutvān vāyur eva | asmin dīrgharogavati itāsur gataprāṇaḥ |
dvitīyānuvāke śrūyate: “yo varuṇagṛhītaḥ syāt sa evaṃ vāruṇaṃ kṛṣṇam ekaśitipādam ālabheta” (TS 2.1.2.1) [283] iti | varuṇagṛhīto jalodarākhyavyādhigṛhīta ity eke | ajñātarogagrasta ity anye | ekaḥ śitiḥ śvetaḥ pādo yasya sa ekaśitipāt | “sa evainaṃ varuṇapāśād vimuñcati” (TS 2.1.2.2) iti phalam | sa eva varuṇa eva | varuṇapāśād uktarogāt |
tasminn evānuvāke “āgneyaṃ kṛṣṇagrīvam ālabheta saumyaṃ babhruṃ jyogāmayāvī” iti (TS 2.1.2.7) | babhruḥ piṅgalavarṇaḥ | tatphalaṃ tu: “agner evāsya śarīraṃ niṣkrīṇāti somād rasam uta yadītāsur bhavati jīvaty eva” iti (TS 2.1.2.7) |
caturthānuvāke “yadi bibhīyād duścarmā bhaviṣyāmīti somā pauṣṇaṃ śyāmam ālabheta” iti (TS 2.1.4.3) | kaṇḍūtyādibhiś cihnair bhaviṣyadrogaṃ niścitavataḥ tasmād bhayaṃ saṃbhavaty eva | tatphalaṃ tu “na duścarmā bhavati” (TS 2.1.4.3) [284] iti |
ṣaṭhānuvāke “prājāpatyaṃ tūparam ālabheta yasyāvajñātam jyogāmayeta” iti (TS 2.1.6.3) | tūparaḥ śṛṅgahīnaḥ paśuḥ | tatphalaṃ tu “sa evainaṃ tasmāt srāmān muñcati” iti (TS 2.1.6.5) | sa eva prajāptir eva | tasmāt srāmād aspaṣṭād dīrgharogāt |
navamānuvāke “maitraṃ śvetam ālabheta vāruṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ jyogāmayāvī” iti (TS 2.1.9.3) | tatphalaṃ tu “sākād evainaṃ varuṇapāśān muñcati uta yadītāsur bhavati jīvaty eva” iti (TS 2.1.9.3) | evam ete roganivartakāḥ paśavaḥ prathamapraśne ‘bhihitāḥ | tathā dvitīyapraśne kāmyeṣṭiprakaraṇe prathamānuvāke śrūyate “agnaye surabhimate puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvaped yasya gāvo vā puruṣā vā pramīyeran yo vā vibhīyāt” (TS 2.2.2.3–4) [285] iti | bhayapratiyogī tu rogātmakaḥ pūtigandho ‘vagantavyaḥ, vākyaśeṣe “pūtigandhasyāpahatyai” (TS 2.2.2.4) iti tatphalasaṃkīrtanāt | tṛtīyānuvāke “agnaye pavamānāya puroḍāśam aṣṭākapālaṃ nirvaped agnaye pāvakāgnaye śucaye jyogāmayāvī” iti (TS 2.2.4.2) | tatphalaṃ tu “yad agnaye pavamānāya nirvapati prāṇam evāsmiṃs tena dadhāti | yad agnaye pāvakāya vācam evāsmiṃs tena dadhāti | yad agnaye śucaye āyur evāsmiṃs tena dadhāti | uta yadītāsur bhavati jīvaty eva” iti (TS 2.2.4.2–3) | tathā tatraiva śrutam “etām eva nirvapec cakṣuṣkāmaḥ” iti (TS 2.2.4.3) | cakṣuṣkāmo netrarogagrastaḥ, “yad agnaye śucaye cakṣur evāsmiṃs tena dadhāty uta yady andho bhavati praiva paśyati” (TS 2.2.4.3–4) [286] iti tatphalaśravaṇāt |
anayaiva diśā vākyāntarāṇy apy udāharaṇīyāni | ṛgvedayajurvedāśritau japahomau pradarśitau sāmavedāśritāv api sāmavedaparyālocanayā pradarśanīyau |
arcanāni tu śaivavaiṣṇavādyāgamādiṣu prasiddhāni | arcanādibhir ity ādiśabdenārghyadhyānayogaprāṇāyāmādayo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopāyā vyādhinivartakā avagantavyāḥ | te ca yogaśāstrādau prasiddhāḥ | asmābhir granthavistarabhayān nātra likhyante |
na ca – ukteṣu japādiṣv anuṣṭhiteṣv api kvacit phalasaṃvādadarśanāt na visrambhapravṛttir iti śaṅkanīyam, auṣadheṣv apy asya nyāyasya samānatvāt | tatrāpi mā bhūd visrabdha – iti cet, hantaivam āyurvedo dattajalāñjaliḥ syāt |
atha manyase prayujyamāneṣv auṣadheṣu kasmāc ciddoṣān nivartyadoṣaprabalatvād vā nivartakauṣadhaprayogasyābhyāso ‘pekṣitaḥ – iti | tarhi japādiṣv api tulyam etad uttaram | “sakṛt kṛte kṛtaḥ śāstrārthaḥ” iti nyāyas tv adṛṣṭaphaleṣv evāvagantavyo na dṛṣṭaphaleṣu | ata evāvaghātādiṣv āvṛttiḥ parikalpyate | na khalv aniṣpanneṣu taṇḍuleṣu sakṛnmusalaprahāreṇāvaghātavidheś caritārthatāṃ mīmāṃsakā [287] manyante | tatra yathā taṇḍulaniṣpattiparyanto ‘vaghātābhyāsaḥ evam atrāpi roganivṛttiparyanto japādyabhyāso draṣṭavyaḥ |
yat tu kvacij japādīnām alpasaṃkhyāśravaṇaṃ tad alpadoṣaviṣayatayā vyavasthāpanīyam | evaṃ ca sati na kvāpi visaṃvādaḥ |
ayaṃ ca vyādhiparīkāranyāyaḥ sarveṣv api aniṣṭeṣu prārabdhaphaleṣu karmasu yojanīyaḥ | yadi japādīnāṃ prāyaścittatvam aṅgīkṛtya prārabdhakarmanivṛttir abhyupeyate yadi vā tadanaṅgīkāreṇa phalamātrapratibandhaḥ sarvathāpi vihite japādau visrambheṇa pravṛttiḥ sidhyati |
yadā bhogaikanivartyatvaśaṅkāyukteṣv api prārabdhaphaleṣv īdṛśapratīkāravisrambhaḥ tadānarabdhaphaleṣu karmasu prāyaścittapravṛttivisrambho ‘stīti kim u vaktavyam | tasmād yathokteṣu prāyaścitteṣu niḥsaṃdigdhatayaiva pravartitavyam ity aśeṣam atimaṅgalam ||
tad evam ayaṃ bhagavān parāśarācāryaḥ kāṇḍadvayena vyavahārasūcanasahitam ācāraprāyaścittaprapañcam abhidhāya śāstram upasaṃharati –
etat pārāśaraṃ śāstraṃ ślokānāṃ śatapañcakam || 12.81 ||
dvinavatyā samāyuktaṃ dharmaśāstrasya saṃgrahaḥ || iti |
parāśareṇa kasyacid upapurāṇasyāpi nirmitatvāt tasya ca jñānapradhānatvāt tadvyavacchettum etad ity uktam | “athāto himaśailāgre” (PārSm 1.1) [288] ity ārabhya praṇīto dvādaśadhyāyātmako granthasandarbhaḥ prakṛtatvād etacchabdena parāmṛśyate | asya śāstrasya kalidharmaviṣaye prāmāṇyātiśayaṃ pūrvoktam anusmārayituṃ parāśarasaṃbandhopavarṇanam | śāstraśabdo ‘tra vidhāyakaṃ vacanam ācaṣṭe, śāsanāc chāstram iti tadvyutpatteḥ |
nanu – śaṃsanāc chāstram ity api tadvyutpattir asti | tad uktaṃ purāṇe,
śāsanāc chaṃsanāc caiva śāstram ity abhidhīyate || iti |
bāḍham | sā vyutpattir mokṣaśāstraviṣayā, tasya siddhabrahmatattvākhyānarūpatvāt | atra tv anuṣṭeyārthakathanāt pūrvoktaiva | tad evābhivyañjayituṃ dharmaśāstrasyety uktam | vacanāntarāṇām āvāpodvāpanivṛttaye saṃkhyābhidhānam | manvādismṛtivad granthabāhulyābhāvāt keṣāṃcid dharmāṇām anuktim āśaṅkya tadvyavacchedāya saṅgraha ity uktam |
asya śāstrasyārthānuṣṭhānapradhānatvāt pāṭhasya svargaṃ prati akiṃcitkaratvam āśaṅkya vedādhyayanavat svargasādhanatvam āha |
yathādhyayanakarmāṇi dharmaśāstram idaṃ tathā || 12.82 ||
adhyetavyaṃ prayatnena niyataṃ svargagāminā | iti |
[289]
nigadavyākhyātam etat |
nanu – vyāsena kalidharmāṇāṃ pṛṣṭatvā, “kalau pārāśarāḥ smṛtāḥ” (ParSm 1.24) iti parāśareṇottaritatvāc ca parāśaroktāḥ sarve kalidharmā eveti vaktavyam | kiṃ ca, “śeṣaṃ munivibhāṣitam” (ParSm 1.34) iti itaramunyuktakaliniṣedhānām apy abhyupagatiḥ spaṣṭaṃ lakṣyate | ato mūloktāghasaṅkocādīnāṃ mūlonnītadūrayātrādīnām asmin yuge pravṛttāv apravṛttau ca virodho jāgarūkaḥ | na ca bāhulyamātreṇa smṛteḥ kalidharmaviṣayatvaṃ kiṃ na syā – iti vācyam, vibhājakanimittābhāvāt, niṣedhavacanānāṃ nimittatve kṛṣyādikasyāpi ananuṣṭheyatvāpatteḥ, saṃnyāsādyāśramakṛṣyādivyatiriktāpadvṛttiḥ setuyātrāvyatiriktadūrayātrādīnām agrāhyatvāpatteḥ, ācārasya tathātve atiprasaṅgāpatteḥ, smṛtivaiyarthyāpatteś ca – iti cet |
atrocyate — [290] kalau parāśaroktaṃ tadvacanonnītaṃ ca sarvam apy anuṣṭheyam eva | nānuṣṭheyaṃ kiṃcid apy asti | kiṃ tv anuṣṭhāne viśeṣo vijñāyate, samanvayabalāt | tathā hi – niṣedhāś ca yugaprayuktānupapattimūlakāḥ | anyathā vidhyutpattivaiyarthāt | anupapattiś ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭā ceti dvividhā | tatra dṛṣṭānupapattimūlakānām anuṣṭhānam ananuṣṭhānaṃ cāninditam | yatra ananyathāsiddhipratiprasavavidhir unnīyate tatra dṛṣṭānupapattiḥ kalpyate | “dūrādhvopagataṃ śrāntam” (ParSm 1.41), “yatihaste jalaṃ dadyāt” (ParSm 1.53), “vaiśvadeva upasthitam” (ParSm 1.41), “cāturvarṇyāśramāgatam” (ParSm 2.1), “ekapiṇḍās tu dāyādāḥ” (ParSm 3.7), “deśāntaramṛtaḥ kaścit” (ParSm 3.10), “brahmacārī gṛhe yeṣām” (ParSm 3.18), “savrataḥ sattrapūtaś ca” (ParSm 3.21), [291] “āhitāgniś ca yo dvijaḥ” (ParSm 3.21), “rājñaś ca sūtakaṃ nāsti” (ParSm 3.22), “yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ” (ParSm 3.22), “saṃpraṇītaḥ śmaśāneṣu” (ParSm 8.22), “dāsanāpitagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ” (ParSm 11.21), “yajeta vāśvamedhena” (ParSm 12.71), ityādyanyathānupapattyā dūrayātrāvidhir unneyaḥ | “ekāhāc chudhyate vipraḥ” (ParSm 3.4) ity aghasaṅkocavidhir yāyāvaraviṣayatvena vā aupāsanoktāpavādapratiprasavaviṣayatvena vā anyathāsiddhiḥ | “naṣṭe mṛte pravrajite” (ParSm 4.28) punar udvāhavidhiḥ parivedādyakramavivāhaviṣayatvena anyathāsiddhiḥ | “kṣetrajaś caiva” (ParSm 4.22) [292] ityādi kṣetrajādiputrabhedapradarśanaṃ kuṇḍagolakapradarśanavat prāyaścittārthatvena anyathāsiddham |
na ca – dattaurasayor api tathā syāt – iti vācyam | ṛtugamanapraśaṃsanena vaiparītyanindanena ca aurasasyeva lakṣaṇapraṇayanena dattasyāpi vidhyabhiprāyaviṣayatvapratipādanāt dattaurasakathanaṃ na prāyaścittarūpam iti spaṣṭīkṛtam | “saṃsargam ācared vipraḥ” (ParSm **) ityādi saṃsargaprāyaścittaṃ lokavilakṣaṇayājanādiviṣayatvena anyathāsiddham | “āsanāt śayanāt yānāt” (ParSm 12.79) iti tu pātityamātrāpādakatvena anyathāsiddham |
anyatra govadhādau adṛṣṭānupapattiḥ kalpyate | teṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ ninditam | yatra pratyakṣo ‘pūrvavidhir dṛśyate kṛṣyādau tasya ananuṣṭhānaṃ ninditam | taditaraviṣayatvenaiva niṣedhānāṃ pravṛttatvāt | pūrvoktānanyathāsiddhavidhiviṣayāṇām apy evam eva draṣṭavyam | teṣām api svasvaviṣayeṣu pratyakṣāpūrvavidhitvāt | etadabhiprāyeṇaiva sarvam api vyākhyānaṃ saṃgacchata iti sarvaṃ samañjasam ||
adhyāyācārakāṇḍānabhihitasamayācārabhedān dvitīye
kāṇḍe ‘dhyāyāṣṭake ‘pi kvacid anabhihitān niṣkṛtīnāṃ prabhedān |
adhyāye dvādaśe ‘sminn akathayad anaghaḥ śaktisūnur munīndraḥ
taṃ śiṣṭānugrahārthaṃ suvivṛtam akaron mādhavācāryavaryaḥ || 1 ||
adhyāye ‘trāvaśiṣṭaṃ samuditam ubhayoḥ kāṇḍayor dvādaśe syāt
prāyaścittaṃ yad asminn anupadam avadat pātakādau rahasye |
anyat saumyādikṛcchraṃ suvihitaphaladaṃ karmaṇo yo vipākaḥ
sarvaṃ bhāvopalakṣyaṃ smṛtihṛdayavidā vyākṛtaṃ mādhavena || 2 ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravarttika-śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya
mādhvāmātyasya kṛtau pārāśravyākhyāyāṃ
mādhavīyāyāṃ dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ |
samāptam idaṃ prāyaścittakāṇdam
